- med · *dialog-feedback* — **"Restart required" banner unhides at the bottom of the page, off-screen**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/channel_detail.html:257`
  - *Evidence:* channel_detail.html:257 places `#channel-restart-banner` after the whole two-column grid, i.e. below ~1400px of content, and `.banner` (design-system.css:1406-1413) is static flow with no sticky/fixed positioning. `showChannelRestartBanner()` (dc_settings_controller.js:1085-1088) only sets `hidden = false`, with no scroll or focus move. Meanwhile the template already has a top banner slot at line 7 (`${bannerHtml}`, fed by the richer `.banner-restart` with actions, app.css:1979-1986). After a mode change the user sees only a toast; the banner they are meant to act on is invisible.
  - *Fix:* Move `#channel-restart-banner` into the top slot next to `${bannerHtml}` (or reuse that one banner) so both the server-detected and client-detected restart-pending states surface in the same place.

- med · *interaction-glitch* — **Mode-card "ACTIVE" badge desyncs from the selected card after a click**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/controllers/dc_settings_controller.js:1219`
  - *Evidence:* The badge is server-rendered only: channel_detail.html:59 `<span tl:if="${mode.active}" class="channel-mode-badge">Active</span>`. The client updater dc_settings_controller.js:1219-1223 (`syncModeCards`) toggles only the `.active` class and `aria-pressed`. After switching DM mode from Pairing to Open, the Open card gets the accent border but no badge, while the Pairing card keeps a stale "ACTIVE" badge until a full reload — two cards claiming to be active. Visible baseline in desktop-dark/channel-whatsapp.png where badge and border currently agree.
  - *Fix:* In `syncModeCards`, create/remove the `.channel-mode-badge` element alongside the class toggle (or render the badge for every card and control it with `.channel-mode-card:not(.active) .channel-mode-badge { display:none }`).

- med · *layout-spacing* — **Channel hero title block is centered, not left-aligned — title x-position shifts per channel**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:1284`
  - *Evidence:* app.css:1284-1290 `.channel-detail-hero-row { display:flex; justify-content:space-between }` with three content-sized children (identicon, `.channel-detail-hero-title`, `.status-badge` — channel_detail.html:11-18). The middle block has no `flex:1`, so free space is split evenly on both sides of it. Screenshots prove it: desktop-dark/channel-whatsapp.png title "WhatsApp" starts at x≈578, channel-signal.png "Signal" at x≈512, channel-gchat.png "Google Chat" at x≈558 — the heading drifts with subtitle length, and sits ~140px away from the 26px identicon it is supposed to label.
  - *Fix:* Give `.channel-detail-hero-title { flex: 1 1 auto; min-width: 0 }` (or push the badge with `margin-left:auto`) so the identicon-title pair is a left-aligned unit and the badge is the only right-aligned item.

- med · *state-coverage* — **Server-rendered allowlist "No entries" row never appears — empty list is truthy in Trellis**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/channel_detail.dart:80`
  - *Evidence:* channel_detail.html:111 and :175 use `tl:unless="${dmAllowlist}"` / `tl:unless="${groupAllowlist}"`, but channel_detail.dart:80/85 pass the raw (empty, non-null) lists. The same file proves the author knew the idiom fails: line 97 explicitly maps `pendingPairings.isNotEmpty ? pendingPairings : null` so its `tl:unless` fires — and "No pending pairing requests" does render in desktop-dark/channel-whatsapp.png while both allowlists show only "Allowlist (0 entries)" followed immediately by the input, with no empty row. Worse, the client-side path DOES emit it (dc_settings_controller.js:1050-1054 appends `.allowlist-empty` "No entries"), so the section silently gains an empty state after any add/remove round-trip.
  - *Fix:* Pass `dmAllowlist.isEmpty ? null : dmAllowlist` (same for group) from channel_detail.dart, matching the pendingPairings treatment — or use the canon `.empty-state` block for the zero case so server and client render identically.

- med · *state-coverage* — **"Pending Pairing Requests" block contradicts its own hint — visibility never updates client-side**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/controllers/dc_settings_controller.js:786`
  - *Evidence:* channel_detail.dart:96 computes `showPairingSection: dmAccessMode == 'pairing'` at render time; channel_detail.html:94 tells the user "This block appears only while DM mode is set to pairing" (rendered text visible in desktop-dark/channel-whatsapp.png). But the mode-change handler (dc_settings_controller.js:786-794) only toggles `.channel-mention-section`; it never shows or hides `[data-section="pairing"]`. Switching DM mode to `pairing` leaves the block absent, switching away leaves it present with a live 5s poll still running (initPairingPolling, dc_settings_controller.js:1087+).
  - *Fix:* Render the pairing sub-card unconditionally with a `hidden` attribute and toggle it in the `group_access`/`dm_access` success callback exactly as the mention section is toggled; start/stop the pairing poll with it.

### `knowledge-hub` (6)

- **HIGH** · *component-adoption* — **The search field is completely unstyled — browser UA chrome sitting in the middle of the page**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/knowledge_hub.html:23`
  - *Evidence:* knowledge_hub.html:23 renders `<input id="knowledge-search" name="q" type="search" …>` with no class, and the only rule matching it is `.knowledge-search-form input[type="search"] { min-width: 0 }` (app.css:3076-3078). There is no global `input` reset (design-system.css:39 resets only box-sizing/margin/padding). Sampled fill from the screenshots: #3b3b3b in dark and #ffffff in light — neither is in the palette (nearest tokens are `--bg-base` #1e1e2e / #eff1f5), the border is the UA gray hairline, and there is no accent focus ring or `--fg-overlay` placeholder. The app's own canonical control `.form-input` (app.css:828-840) supplies exactly those: `background: var(--bg-base); border: var(--border); font-family: var(--font-mono); :focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--accent) }`.
  - *Fix:* Add `class="form-input"` to the search input in knowledge_hub.html:23 (and drop the now-redundant `min-width` override, or fold it into the form grid).

- **HIGH** · *copy-content* — **Layer counters read 0 for every non-selected layer, contradicting the results on screen**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/knowledge/knowledge_hub_service.dart:108`
  - *Evidence:* knowledge_hub_service.dart:108-115 — `collect()` returns early when `query.layer != KnowledgeHubLayer.all && query.layer != layer`, and the counts map at :123-126 is built only from the collected `items`, so unselected layers are always 0. Visible across the captures: knowledge-hub.png shows `1 WIKI`, while knowledge-memory.png / knowledge-kg.png / knowledge-inbox.png show `0 WIKI` on the identical corpus. Secondary defect in the same block: `limit = query.perPage * query.page + query.perPage` (:117) caps the collected list, so the counts (and `totalPages` at :129) are pagination-capped rather than true totals — "Page X of Y" changes Y as the user pages.
  - *Fix:* Compute the summary counts from an unfiltered per-layer count query (independent of `query.layer` and of `limit`), or relabel the strip as "results in view" so it stops presenting itself as a corpus summary.

- med · *copy-content* — **Result snippets show raw markdown, whitespace-flattened and hard-cut mid-word with no ellipsis**
  - `packages/dartclaw_storage/lib/src/search/wiki_search_source.dart:106`
  - *Evidence:* `WikiSearchSource._snippet` (packages/dartclaw_storage/lib/src/search/wiki_search_source.dart:106-117) collapses all whitespace with `replaceAll(RegExp(r'\s+'), ' ')` and then does `compact.substring(start, end)` at a fixed 240 chars with no word-boundary snap, no ellipsis and no markdown stripping. Rendered verbatim by knowledge_hub.html:50. Visible in every hub capture: "# Wiki Use `wiki/` for synthesized… - `MEMORY.md` is the chronological memory stream and quick fact store. - `wiki/` pages are curated summaries, gui" — heading marker, backticks and list dashes inline, ending mid-word on "gui".
  - *Fix:* Strip leading markdown syntax (ATX headings, list markers, backticks/emphasis) before compacting, snap the cut to the last word boundary, and append an ellipsis; alternatively clamp visually with `-webkit-line-clamp: 3` on `.knowledge-result-snippet` and cut on a longer boundary.

- med · *interaction-glitch* — **Attribution popover opens on hover but has no hover-out close, so it stays pinned open**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/controllers/dc_attribution_controller.js:1`
  - *Evidence:* source_attribution.html:5 binds `mouseenter->dc-attribution#show` and `keydown.esc->dc-attribution#hide` on the `[1]` button, but dc_attribution_controller.js defines only `toggle/show/hide/handleDocumentClick` — there is no `mouseleave` or `focusout` action. Merely moving the pointer across a citation marker opens a 260-360px popover that stays open until the user clicks somewhere else, and the Esc binding is on the button so it does nothing once the popover was opened by hover without focus. Additionally `.attribution-popover` is `position: absolute; left: 0` with `max-width: min(360px, 80vw)` (app.css:3226-3237) and the controller has no flip/collision logic, so a marker near the right edge overflows its card.
  - *Fix:* Add `mouseleave->dc-attribution#hide` (with a small close delay so the pointer can travel into the popover) and move the Esc binding to the controller element or `document` while open; or drop hover-open entirely and keep it click/focus-only.

- med · *state-coverage* — **Knowledge Hub layer-count cards collapse to 0 for every non-selected layer, and a failed layer is indistinguishable from an empty one**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/knowledge/knowledge_hub_service.dart:123`
  - *Evidence:* knowledge_hub_service.dart:108-110 `collect()` returns early (`if (query.layer != KnowledgeHubLayer.all && query.layer != layer) return;`) so unqueried layers contribute nothing, and :123-126 builds `counts` purely from the returned `items`. The four summary cards (knowledge_hub.html:34-39, fed by knowledge_hub.dart:66-69) therefore report the filtered result set, not the corpus. Proven: desktop-dark/knowledge-hub.png (All filter) shows WIKI 1 / KG 0 / MEMORY 0 / INBOX 0; desktop-dark/knowledge-kg.png (KG filter) shows WIKI 0 — the one document the user just saw has silently become zero. Separately, knowledge_hub_service.dart:111-114 catches a layer failure into `failed` but that layer's count is also 0, so 'this layer broke' and 'this layer is empty' render identically in the summary strip; only the warning notice at knowledge_hub.html:41 distinguishes them.
  - *Fix:* Compute layer counts from an unfiltered corpus query (or cache totals) so the summary strip is stable across filters, and render a failed layer's count cell with an explicit error marker rather than 0.

- low · *responsive* — **Knowledge grids collapse at 767px while the rest of the app switches at 768px**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:3204`
  - *Evidence:* app.css contains exactly three `@media (max-width: 767px)` blocks — 3006 (`.citation-marker` touch target), 3204 (`.knowledge-search-form`, `.knowledge-summary-strip`, `.knowledge-result-row` → 1 column) and 3393 (kg-timeline) — against 22 uses of `max-width: 768px` everywhere else (e.g. `.page-content` mobile padding at :599, `.tab-bar` wrap at :1068). At exactly 768px — the viewport used for the mobile captures — the app's mobile padding fires but the knowledge grids do not: mobile-dark/knowledge-hub.png shows the tightened 12px page padding together with a still-4-across summary strip and a still-2-column result row, and the citation marker keeps its 32px (not 44px) touch target.
  - *Fix:* Change the three knowledge/kg `@media (max-width: 767px)` queries to `768px` to match the rest of app.css.

### `global` (5)

- **HIGH** · *layout-spacing* — **App shell overflows the viewport on long pages — sidebar/topbar end mid-page and raw canvas shows below**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/design-system.css:90`
  - *Evidence:* `.shell` is `height: 100dvh` with `grid-template-rows: var(--topbar-h) 1fr` (dev/design-system/DESIGN.md canon → packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/design-system.css:87-92). A bare `1fr` grid row has `min-height: auto`, so a tall scroll child pushes content out of the fixed-height shell instead of scrolling inside it. Neither scroll container sets the escape hatch: `.page-content { overflow-y: auto; padding: … }` (static/app.css:598) and `.content-area { flex: 1; overflow-y: auto }` (static/design-system.css:1550-1554 — `flex` is inert on a grid item). Proof in the captures: every page whose content fits is exactly 1440x900, but shots/desktop-dark/memory.png is 1440x1333 and shots/desktop-dark/task-detail.png is 1440x921. In memory.png the sidebar, its border and the gradient ground all stop hard at y=900 and everything below is unpainted black canvas, because `body { background: …; background-attachment: fixed }` (design-system.css:51-59) only paints the viewport box. The author already knows the fix — `.chat-area { … min-height: 0 }` at design-system.css:409-414 — and the shell's *columns* correctly use `minmax(0, 1fr)` while the rows do not.
  - *Fix:* Change `.shell` rows to `grid-template-rows: var(--topbar-h) minmax(0, 1fr)` and add `min-height: 0` to both `.page-content` (app.css:598) and `.content-area` (design-system.css:1550). Drop the inert `flex: 1` from `.content-area`.

- **HIGH** · *state-coverage* — **No global htmx error handler — all 28 hx-get navigation and polling sites fail completely silently**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/controllers/dc_shell_controller.js:395`
  - *Evidence:* `grep -c hx-get templates/*.html` = 28 sites (sidebar.html 9, task_timeline.html 5, topbar.html 2, workflow_list.html 2, plus health/memory/workflow polls). `htmx:responseError`/`htmx:sendError` are bound in exactly one place — dc_shell_controller.js:395 `bindHtmxRequestErrors(source, fallbackMessage)`, called only at dc_shell_controller.js:351 for the archive-chat button. There is no body-level listener. htmx does not swap on 4xx/5xx by default, and _htmlNotFound/_htmlError (web_routes.dart:630-634) return 404/500 full pages, so clicking a stale sidebar link for a deleted session, or any nav click while the server is down, produces zero visible feedback — no toast, no message, no state change.
  - *Fix:* Register one body-level `htmx:responseError` + `htmx:sendError` listener in dc_shell_controller.js connect() that calls the existing `showToast('error', readHtmxErrorMessage(...))`, and add `hx-target-404`/error-swap handling so 404 responses render the error_page body into #main-content instead of being dropped.

- med · *copy-content* — **Timestamps never roll over past days and appear in three unrelated formats across pages**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/helpers.dart:31`
  - *Evidence:* helpers.dart:31 — `if (diff.inDays > 0) return '${diff.inDays}d ago';` — has no weeks/months/years branch, so seeded data reads "129d ago" (tasks.png), "105d ago" / "103d ago" / "102d ago" / "101d ago" (workflows.png), "103d ago" ×3 (task-detail.png). No call site attaches a `title=` attribute with the absolute timestamp, so there is no hover disclosure anywhere. On the same product, session_info.dart:95 passes `createdAt` straight through unformatted — desktop-dark/session-info.png shows `Created  2026-04-15T10:00:00.000`, milliseconds included — while memory_dashboard renders `2026-03-20 02:00` and kg_timeline.html:27 shows `2026-01-15T00:00:00Z`. Four presentations of one data type.
  - *Fix:* Extend `formatRelativeTime` (helpers.dart:29) to fall back to an absolute short date (`d MMM` / `d MMM yyyy`) beyond ~30 days, and have every call site emit `title="<ISO>"` for hover disclosure. Route session_info.dart:95, memory_dashboard and kg_timeline through the same helper so one format governs.

- low · *component-adoption* — **Two broken button classes ship in production markup: a primary button missing its base `.btn`, and an undefined `.btn-secondary`**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/restart_banner.html:7`
  - *Evidence:* restart_banner.html:7 — `<button class="btn-sm btn-primary" …>Restart Now</button>` — omits the base `.btn`. `.btn-primary` only supplies the accent fill and text colour; `.btn` (design-system.css) supplies `inline-flex`, `padding: var(--sp-2) var(--sp-3)`, `border-radius`, `border`, the inset top-edge highlight and the hover transition. The banner's call to action therefore renders with native browser button chrome plus an accent background, and it appears on every page after a settings save (the restart notice is visible in desktop-dark/settings.png). Separately, `.btn-secondary` is used at tasks.html:22 ("Show workflow artifacts") and workflow_detail.html:133 but is not defined in app.css, design-system.css, or the canon dev/design-system/components.css — those buttons silently degrade to a plain `.btn`, so the author's intended tertiary emphasis is dropped without any signal.
  - *Fix:* Add `btn` to restart_banner.html:7 (`class="btn btn-sm btn-primary"`). Either define `.btn-secondary` in the canon button set (DESIGN.md § Buttons currently lists only default/primary/ghost/danger/icon/full) or replace the two usages with `.btn-ghost`, which is the existing lower-emphasis variant.

- low · *typography* — **The one typeface the whole design system rests on is the only unvendored runtime asset, loaded with display=swap and no preconnect**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/layout.html:12`
  - *Evidence:* layout.html:12 fetches JetBrains Mono from fonts.googleapis.com with `display=swap` and no `<link rel=preconnect>` to fonts.gstatic.com — so every cold load renders a full FOUT in the fallback face before swapping. Every other runtime asset is vendored per packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/VENDORS.md and the package rule "Do not add new npm/CDN runtime fetches" (packages/dartclaw_server/CLAUDE.md § Conventions); highlight.js, DOMPurify, htmx-ext-sse and Stimulus are all local. DartClaw is a self-hostable server, so air-gapped and CSP-restricted installs render the entire UI in SF Mono/Menlo/Consolas — different x-height, different stem contrast, and different cap-height ratios, which shifts every one of the size relationships this design system is tuned around. The tokens.css fallback chain (line 67-68) makes the degradation silent rather than visible.
  - *Fix:* Self-host JetBrains Mono woff2 at weights 400/500/600 under packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/fonts/, declare it via @font-face with `font-display: swap` and `size-adjust` tuned to the first fallback, register it in VENDORS.md, and drop the Google Fonts <link> from layout.html:12.

### `memory` (4)

- **HIGH** · *interaction-glitch* — **Memory dashboard's 30s poll full-swaps the page body, resetting the file tab, discarding loaded previews and scroll**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/memory_dashboard.html:7`
  - *Evidence:* templates/memory_dashboard.html:7-8 declares `hx-get="/memory/content" hx-trigger="every 30s" hx-swap="innerHTML" hx-select="#memory-inner"` on `#memory-content`, and web/web_routes.dart:521-532 returns a full 200 fragment unconditionally (no 204 short-circuit). So the whole dashboard body is replaced every 30s. Consequences are all recoverable-state losses: (a) dc_memory_controller.js:42-54 `initMemoryDefaultTab` re-reads the *server-rendered* `.tab-btn.active`, which is always `tab-memory` (memory_dashboard.html:146), so a user reading errors.md/learnings.md is thrown back to MEMORY.md every 30s; (b) `preview.dataset.rawContent`/`.loaded` live on the swapped-away DOM node (dc_memory_controller.js:129-131), so the file is re-fetched from scratch; (c) `.memory-preview { max-height: 300px; overflow-y: auto }` (app.css:1089) scroll position and any text selection are lost. Sibling instance: templates/audit_table.dart:20-21 emits `hx-trigger="every 30s" hx-swap="outerHTML"` on `#audit-table-container`, which collapses any expanded `.audit-detail-row` (toggled at dc_shell_controller.js:418-425) every 30s. Contrast the pairing polls (whatsapp_pairing.html:91, signal_pairing.html:129), which are safe only because the endpoint answers 204 while waiting.
  - *Fix:* Persist the active tab (localStorage, as `toggleView` already does at dc_memory_controller.js:91) and re-apply it in `afterSwap`; carry `rawContent` across swaps or scope the poll to the metric cards only. For the audit table, return 204 when the audit page is unchanged, or poll only `#audit-table-container tbody`.

- med · *interaction-glitch* — **Memory dashboard meter label renders as "of 32 KB      0      %" — the bare % text node becomes a third flex item under justify-content: space-between**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/memory_dashboard.html:21`
  - *Evidence:* app.css:1041 `.meter-label { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; … }`. memory_dashboard.html:20-23 puts three children inside it: `<span>of <span>32 KB</span></span>`, `<span tl:text="${budgetPercent}">0</span>`, and a loose `%` text node. CSS wraps the loose `%` in an anonymous flex item, so space-between distributes three items and strands the percent sign at the far right edge, detached from its number. Visible in shots/mobile-dark/memory.png at y≈184: "of 32 KB" left, "0" centred, "%" flush right — reading as three unrelated values on the card that reports memory budget consumption.
  - *Fix:* Wrap the value and unit together: `<span><span tl:text="${budgetPercent}">0</span>%</span>`. Add a Layer 2 template-render assertion that `.meter-label` contains exactly two element children so the pattern can't regress in the other meter usages.

- med · *interaction-glitch* — **Memory KPI row: a flex row flings the literal "%" to the card's right edge, and 2 of 5 tiles have a hole where their meter should be**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/memory_dashboard.html:22`
  - *Evidence:* memory_dashboard.html:21-22 emits three children into `.meter-label`, which is `display: flex; justify-content: space-between` (app.css:1041): `<span>of <span>32 KB</span></span>`, `<span tl:text="${budgetPercent}">0</span>`, and a bare text node `%`. Because the `%` is outside the span it becomes a third flex item, so the card reads "of 32 KB          0          %" instead of "of 32 KB    0%" — clearly visible in desktop-dark/memory.png and desktop-light/memory.png. In the same row, tiles 2 and 3 (`activeEntriesCardHtml`/`archivedEntriesCardHtml`, memory_dashboard.dart:97/102) go through the plain `metricCardTemplate` and have no `.meter-wrapper`, leaving ~30px of dead space at their base against three neighbours that carry meters — the five-tile row does not read as one component.
  - *Fix:* Change memory_dashboard.html:22 to a single node — `<span tl:text="${budgetPercent} + '%'">0%</span>` — so `space-between` has exactly two items. Either give ACTIVE ENTRIES / ARCHIVED ENTRIES a meter (they have no cap, so a sparkline or nothing is honest) or move the three metered tiles into their own row so every tile in a row has the same anatomy.

- med · *layout-spacing* — **/memory is the only page whose document escapes the 100dvh shell, painting a 433px band of flat unstyled ground below the app frame**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/memory_dashboard.html:4`
  - *Evidence:* `.shell { height: 100dvh }` (design-system.css) with `.page-content { overflow-y: auto }` (app.css:597) is supposed to clamp every page to the viewport and scroll internally. Measured full-page capture heights confirm it holds everywhere except one surface: 22 of 23 desktop captures are exactly 1440×900 (dark and light), and 22 of 23 mobile captures are 768×1024 — but memory.png is 1440×**1333** in both themes and 768×**1705** on mobile. Because `body` uses `background-attachment: fixed` (design-system.css), the 433px region below the shell receives no gradient, no ambient glow and no film grain — it renders as a flat near-black slab in dark and a flat grey slab in light, directly under the app chrome.
  - *Fix:* Diagnose the min-content overflow inside `#memory-inner` (likeliest: a grid/flex descendant without `min-height: 0`/`min-width: 0` forcing the `1fr` shell row past its track, or an intrinsically-sized child of `.page-inner`). Add `min-height: 0` to the shell's row-2 child and confirm /memory captures at 1440×900 like every other surface.

### `shell/sidebar` (4)

- **HIGH** · *color-depth* — **Sidebar section dividers render as the browser's default 2px beveled <hr> — bright off-palette gray on both themes**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/sidebar.html:21`
  - *Evidence:* `.sidebar-divider` is referenced 4× in sidebar.html (lines 21, 52, 71, 86) but has NO rule in app.css or design-system.css (`rg 'sidebar-divider|^hr' app.css design-system.css` → no CSS match), and there is no `hr` reset (the `*` reset at design-system.css:39 zeroes margin/padding but not `border`). Pixel-sampled desktop-dark/tasks.png at x=130: y=110 → rgb(154,154,154), y=111 → rgb(238,238,238); same at y=174/175 and y=244/245; identical values in desktop-light/tasks.png. #EEEEEE is brighter than the primary text token `--fg` (#cdd6f4, luma 214) and matches no token in tokens.css. By contrast the correctly-tokenised `.sidebar-section { border-top: var(--border) }` renders rgb(49,50,68) = `--bg-surface0` at y=521. So the rail carries two incompatible separator treatments, and the wrong one is a 3-D bevel — the exact opposite of DESIGN.md's "tight and engineered" shape language.
  - *Fix:* Add a `.sidebar-divider { border: 0; border-top: var(--border); }` rule (or delete the `<hr>`s and rely on `.sidebar-section`'s `border-top`). Add a global `hr { border: 0; border-top: var(--border); }` reset so no unstyled `<hr>` can ever fall through to UA bevel again.

- med · *interaction-glitch* — **On the active sidebar chat row the archive button renders on top of the still-visible provider badge**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:238`
  - *Evidence:* Clearly visible in every desktop capture — shots/desktop-dark/chat-session.png and desktop-light at ~x=218,y=419 render "CLAU▣E": the archive icon button sits over the CLAUDE provider badge on the selected "Composer Smoke E…" row. Cause: at ≥769px the action button is taken out of flow — `.session-item :is(.session-action, .session-delete) { position: absolute; top:50%; right: var(--sp-1) }` (app.css:229-237) — and the badge is hidden only for hover/focus: `.session-item:has(.session-action, .session-delete):is(:hover, :focus-within) .provider-badge { opacity: 0 }` (app.css:238). But the button is also revealed on the persistent active state — `.session-item.active :is(.session-action, .session-delete) { opacity: 1 }` (app.css:149) — which the badge-hiding selector omits. So the active row is permanently in the overlap state.
  - *Fix:* Add `.session-item.active` to the badge-hiding selector at app.css:238 (i.e. `:is(:hover, :focus-within, .active)`), matching the reveal selector at app.css:147-149.

- med · *interaction-glitch* — **Archive button paints directly on top of the provider badge on the active session row**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:238`
  - *Evidence:* app.css:149 forces the row action visible on the active row (`.session-item.active :is(.session-action, .session-delete) { opacity: 1 }`), and at ≥769px app.css:232-238 absolutely positions that button at `right: var(--sp-1)`. But the compensating rule that clears the badge — app.css:238 `.session-item:has(.session-action, .session-delete):is(:hover, :focus-within) .provider-badge { opacity: 0 }` — keys only on `:hover`/`:focus-within`, never on `.active`. Result in desktop-dark/chat-session.png and desktop-light/chat-session.png (crop x0-260, y395-455): the archive glyph is drawn over the last two letters of the CLAUDE badge on the "Composer Smoke E…" row. This is the default landing surface, so it is on screen in the resting state, not only on hover.
  - *Fix:* Extend the badge-hiding selector to the persistent-action state: `.session-item:has(.session-action, .session-delete):is(:hover, :focus-within, .active) .provider-badge { opacity: 0 }` (app.css:238).

- med · *interaction-glitch* — **Active sidebar session row renders the archive button on top of the provider badge**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:238`
  - *Evidence:* Visible in shots/desktop-dark/chat-session.png: the active 'Composer Smoke E…' row shows a box glyph overlapping the last two characters of its CLAUDE badge ('CLAUD⊟'); the inactive rows below render the badge cleanly. Three rules collide in static/app.css: :149 `.session-item.active :is(.session-action, .session-delete) { opacity: 1 }` makes the button permanently visible on the active row; :230-236 (inside `@media (min-width: 769px)`) makes it `position: absolute; right: var(--sp-1); top: 50%` so it overlays the row rather than displacing content; and :238 — the compensating rule that fades the badge out — is keyed only on `:is(:hover, :focus-within)` and omits `.active`.
  - *Fix:* Extend the selector at app.css:238 to `.session-item:has(.session-action, .session-delete):is(:hover, :focus-within, .active) .provider-badge { opacity: 0 }`, matching the visibility trigger already set at app.css:147-149.

### `task-detail` (4)

- **HIGH** · *layout-spacing* — **Task-detail metadata row sets its labels on four different baselines — TYPE/STATUS/PROVIDER/CREATED BY are visibly staggered**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:1821`
  - *Evidence:* desktop-dark/task-detail.png and desktop-light/task-detail.png, y≈95-105: "STATUS" sits ~3px above "TYPE", "PROVIDER" ~4px below it, and "CREATED BY"/"CREATED"/"STARTED"/"COMPLETED" on a fourth line — a ransom-note row. Identical stagger on mobile-dark/task-detail.png (y≈90-96) and on task-detail-workflow.png. Cause: app.css:1821-1826 — `.task-meta-grid` is `display:flex; align-items:center` over `.task-meta-item` children that are themselves `flex-direction:column` label-over-value stacks. Because the values have different heights (`.type-badge` 1px padding vs `.status-badge` 3px padding + 1px border vs bare text), centering each item vertically pushes every label to a different y.
  - *Fix:* Replace the flex row with `display:grid; grid-auto-flow:column; grid-template-rows:auto auto; align-items:start` (or set `align-items: flex-start` and give values a shared min-height) so all labels share one baseline and all values share another.

- med · *layout-spacing* — **Task-detail meta row is baseline-ragged — labels and values sit at four different heights**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:1821`
  - *Evidence:* In shots/desktop-dark/task-detail.png the caption row reads TYPE (y≈100), STATUS (y≈97), PROVIDER (y≈103), CREATED BY (y≈99) — nothing lines up, and the value row below is equally ragged. Cause: `.task-meta-grid { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: var(--sp-4); align-items: center; }` (static/app.css:1821-1823) over `.task-meta-item { display: flex; flex-direction: column; }` (:1824-1826). Item heights differ because some values are padded pills (`.type-badge`, `.status-badge`, `.provider-badge`) and others are bare text nodes ('workflow', '103d ago' — templates/task_detail.html:12-45), and `align-items: center` then centres each column independently, so neither the label line nor the value line shares a baseline. The class is named `-grid` but is implemented as a flex row, which is why no row alignment exists to inherit.
  - *Fix:* Make it a real two-row grid (`display: grid; grid-auto-flow: column; grid-template-rows: auto auto; align-items: start`) so all labels share row 1 and all values row 2, or keep flex with `align-items: stretch` plus a `.task-meta-value` wrapper carrying the badge's line-height for the bare-text cases.

- med · *layout-spacing* — **Task detail meta row labels are vertically ragged because .task-meta-grid centers flex items of differing heights**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:1821`
  - *Evidence:* app.css:1821 `.task-meta-grid { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: var(--sp-4); align-items: center }`. Each `.task-meta-item` is a two-row column (uppercase `--text-xs` label + value), but the values have different intrinsic heights — a `.type-badge` pill, a `.status-badge` pill with border, a 10px `.provider-badge`, and plain 13px text — so `align-items: center` offsets each column differently. In shots/desktop-light/task-detail.png the labels land at ~y=97 (STATUS), 100 (TYPE), 99 (CREATED BY/CREATED/STARTED/COMPLETED) and 103 (PROVIDER), a ~6px stagger across a single row; the same stagger is in shots/mobile-dark/task-detail.png. Nothing in the row is intentionally offset — this is pure flex centering noise on the page's masthead.
  - *Fix:* Change `align-items: center` to `align-items: start` on `.task-meta-grid` and give `.task-meta-item` a fixed value-row height (e.g. `.task-meta-item > :last-child { min-height: 22px; display: inline-flex; align-items: center }`) so labels share a baseline and pills center within a uniform value band.

- low · *copy-content* — **The task session renders the machine-composed prompt as raw markdown collapsed onto one line**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/chat.html:3`
  - *Evidence:* desktop-dark/task-detail-workflow.png y≈520 (and mobile-dark): the YOU message reads "## Task: code-review — Publish Summary Publish a concise summary of the workflow card spacing review and the remediation that was approved." — the literal `##` is shown and the heading has run into the body because the newline collapsed. Cause: tasks_page.dart:252 feeds the task's server-composed prompt through `messagesHtmlFragment`, whose `userMessage` fragment renders `<div class="msg-content"><p tl:text="${content}">` with no `data-markdown` attribute (chat.html:1-4), unlike `assistantMessage` which has it (chat.html:9) and is processed by shared.js:215-221. Plain text is right for human-typed chat input, but on this surface the "user" message is always machine-composed markdown.
  - *Fix:* Either render task-session user messages with `data-markdown` (they are server-composed, not browser free-text, on this page), or at minimum add `white-space: pre-wrap` to `.msg-user .msg-content p` so the composed prompt's line structure survives.

### `projects` (3)

- **HIGH** · *layout-spacing* — **Every `.project-card-*` class in projects.html has zero CSS in any served stylesheet — the project cards are unstyled block flow**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/projects.html:17`
  - *Evidence:* `grep -n "project-card\|project-error-banner\|project-url" app.css design-system.css tokens.css icons.css` returns NOTHING, yet projects.html:17-48 hangs the whole card anatomy on `.project-card-header`, `.project-card-title-row`, `.project-card-actions`, `.project-card-meta`, `.project-error-banner`, `.project-url`. Result in desktop-dark/projects.png: the Fetch button drops onto its own line under the title instead of sitting right-aligned in the header row (no flex on `.project-card-header`/`-actions`); the meta row (`git@github.com:… / Main / Fetched: Never / Branch Only`) is raw inline flow; and the error sentence "GitHub projects require a GitHub token credential reference." renders as plain 14px body text with no tint, icon, or border despite being wrapped in `.project-error-banner` next to a red Error badge. Both cards are ~90-160px tall of mostly dead space.
  - *Fix:* Either style the six classes or (preferred) recompose the card from canon: `.card-header` (title + badges + actions with `margin-left:auto`), `.card-footer` for the meta row, and `.banner banner-error` (design-system.css) for the error line — the same `.banner banner-error` recipe workflow_detail.html:130 already uses.

- med · *component-adoption* — **The projects page emits six classes that have no CSS in either stylesheet, so its cards render as unstyled markup**
  - *Evidence:* grep across static/app.css and static/design-system.css returns 0 matches for `.project-card`, `.project-card-header`, `.project-card-title-row`, `.project-card-actions`, `.project-card-meta` and `.project-error-banner`, all emitted by projects.html:15-46. Consequence in desktop-dark/projects.png: the "Fetch" action sits on its own orphan line under the title instead of right-aligned in a header row, the git URL/branch/PR-strategy meta row has no treatment, and the error copy "GitHub projects require a GitHub token credential reference." renders as bare body-size foreground text with no banner — despite the card carrying an "Error" badge. Canon has both `.banner-error` and `.card.panel-error` (design-system.css § Cards, `panel-{color}` = 3px left edge + gradient bleed) and neither is applied to the one card in the app that has a severity.
  - *Fix:* Either style the six classes or drop them and compose from canon: `.card.panel-error` on the errored project, `.card-header` for the title/actions row, `.card-footer` for the meta row, `.banner-error` for the error copy.

- med · *state-coverage* — **Project fetch/config errors render as plain body text — .project-error-banner has no CSS rule anywhere**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/projects.html:46`
  - *Evidence:* projects.html:46 `<div tl:if="${project.hasError}" class="project-error-banner">`. `grep -rn 'project-error-banner' static/` matches only dc_tasks_controller.js:815 (which queries it) — there is no rule for it in app.css, design-system.css, or tokens.css. Result in desktop-dark/projects.png: the visual-demo project is badged 'Error' but its message 'GitHub projects require a GitHub token credential reference.' renders at default body color with no red, no border, no icon — visually lower-emphasis than the neutral 'Branch Only' pill beside it. There is also no retry or fix action; the only buttons are the generic Fetch/Edit/Remove row.
  - *Fix:* Replace the dead class with the canon `banner banner-error` (design-system.css:1415) plus the `icon-triangle-alert` glyph, matching workflow_detail.html:120's error treatment, and add an 'Add credential' action linking to the Providers settings tab.

### `health-audit` (2)

- med · *dialog-feedback* — **/health-dashboard/audit serves a bare HTMX fragment as a navigable page — renders completely unstyled**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/web/web_routes.dart:398`
  - *Evidence:* shots/desktop-dark/health-audit.png and shots/desktop-light/health-audit.png are byte-identical and show default-serif text on white with native OS buttons — no tokens, no layout, no sidebar, no theme. Cause: web/web_routes.dart:398-412 registers `GET /health-dashboard/audit` returning `auditTableFragment(...)` through `htmlFragment(...)` with no layout wrapper and no HX-Request check, and templates/audit_table.dart:63-66 builds the filter buttons as plain `hx-get` links to that same URL — so any full-page load, reload after an HTMX navigation, or bookmark of the audit view lands on the raw fragment. Compounding it, audit_table.dart:44 emits a bare `'<div class="table-scroll"><table>'` with no `.data-table` class, so even inside the dashboard it is the only table in the app without the canonical header caps/tracking, row hover and rules (design-system.css:1568-1590); every other table adopts it (scheduling.html:71,167; memory_dashboard.html:85,182,258; tasks.html:67).
  - *Fix:* Return the full page shell when `HX-Request` is absent (as the other fragment routes' page counterparts do), and add `class="data-table"` to the `<table>` at audit_table.dart:44.

- med · *state-coverage* — **/health-dashboard/audit renders as a naked, unstyled HTML fragment on direct navigation**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/web/web_routes.dart:398-413`
  - *Evidence:* desktop-dark/health-audit.png and desktop-light/health-audit.png are byte-identical (md5 955db2b0…) — the page has no theme response at all because it has no stylesheet. The render is browser-default: Times serif body text "No guard events recorded yet", native OS `<button>` chrome for All/Command/File/Network/Input/Content/Pass/Warn/Block, white background, no shell, no sidebar, no topbar. web_routes.dart:398-413 returns `auditTableFragment(...)` directly with `Response.ok(html, headers: {...htmlHeaders, 'vary': 'HX-Request'})` — it declares the Vary header but never branches on `HX-Request`, so a real navigation (bookmark, back/forward, copied filter link — and every `hx-get` in the fragment pushes no URL but the route is publicly reachable) gets the bare fragment. mobile-dark/health-audit.png shows the same.
  - *Fix:* In web_routes.dart:398, branch on `request.headers['HX-Request']`: return the fragment for HTMX requests, and for a plain navigation either redirect to `/health-dashboard` (preserving verdict/guard/page as query params) or wrap the fragment in the standard shell via `layoutTemplate` + sidebar/topbar.

### `memory-dashboard` (2)

- **HIGH** · *interaction-glitch* — **The 30s poll swaps innerHTML and destroys tab selection, loaded file content, and preview scroll position**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/memory_dashboard.html:6`
  - *Evidence:* memory_dashboard.html:6-8 — `hx-get="/memory/content" hx-trigger="every 30s" hx-swap="innerHTML" hx-select="#memory-inner"` replaces the whole `#memory-inner` subtree, including the Memory Files card. The server fragment always renders MEMORY.md active (memory_dashboard.html:146,159 `class="tab-btn active"` / `tab-panel active`). dc_memory_controller.js:26-54 `initializeView()` restores only the raw/rendered toggle from localStorage and then loads the *default* tab's preview. Concretely, every 30s: (a) a user reading errors.md is thrown back to MEMORY.md; (b) `preview.dataset.rawContent` for all tabs is discarded and `/api/memory/files/memory` is re-fetched (dc_memory_controller.js:126) — a second request per poll; (c) `.memory-preview { max-height:300px; overflow-y:auto }` (app.css:1089) means the reader's scroll position inside the file is reset; (d) a pending "Confirm Prune?" state (dc_memory_controller.js:107-116, 4s window) is wiped if a poll lands inside it.
  - *Fix:* Narrow the poll to the volatile regions instead of the whole page: put `hx-get`/`hx-trigger` on the Overview grid and the Pruner card with `hx-swap="outerHTML"` and leave `#memory-files-card` out of the swap. If the whole-fragment swap must stay, persist the active tab id (localStorage, same as the view toggle) and re-apply it in `initializeView()` before loading a preview.

- **HIGH** · *layout-spacing* — **Budget meter label renders as "of 32 KB … 0 … %" — the percent sign is flung to the opposite edge of the card**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/memory_dashboard.html:22`
  - *Evidence:* Visible in all four screenshots (desktop-dark/light memory.png card 1; unmistakable at mobile-dark/memory.png where the card is wide: "of 32 KB" flush-left, "0" centred, "%" flush-right). Cause: `.meter-label { display:flex; justify-content: space-between; }` (packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:1041) receives THREE flex items from memory_dashboard.html:20-23 — `<span>of …</span>`, `<span>0</span>`, and the bare text node `%` that sits outside the closing span. The anonymous text node becomes its own flex item and gets pushed to the far edge.
  - *Fix:* Wrap value and unit in one element: `<span><span tl:text="${budgetPercent}">0</span>%</span>` in packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/memory_dashboard.html:22, so `.meter-label` has exactly the two items its space-between contract assumes.

### `scheduling` (2)

- **HIGH** · *interaction-glitch* — **Scheduled-task toggle button renders as a blank filled square, 3.5x wider than its siblings, and never reflects state**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/scheduling.html:192-195`
  - *Evidence:* Three compounding defects on one control. (1) scheduling.html:195 sets `data-icon="check"`, but icons.css has NO `[data-icon="check"]::before` mapping — the `--icon-check` token (icons.css:53) is only wired to the class form `.icon-check` (icons.css:227). The generic `[data-icon]::before` rule (icons.css:195-209) still paints a 1em x 1em box filled with `currentColor` and no mask-image → a solid gray square. Confirmed in the crop of desktop-dark/scheduling.png at (1030-1290, 610-730): a featureless filled square. (2) app.css:1135 `.scheduling-action-toggle { min-width: 5.5rem }` — a stale rule from when this button had a text label — makes it 88px wide next to its 24px pencil/x siblings, wrecking the action-column rhythm. (3) The icon is hard-coded to `check` regardless of `task.enabled`, so enable and disable look identical; only the `title`/`aria-label` change.
  - *Fix:* Add `[data-icon="check"]::before`, `[data-icon="pause"]::before` and `[data-icon="play"]::before` mappings to icons.css § 4; switch scheduling.html:195 to `tl:attr="data-icon=${task.enabled ? 'pause' : 'play'}"` so the glyph carries the state; delete the `min-width: 5.5rem` from app.css:1135 so the button matches `.btn-icon-sm`.

- med · *interaction-glitch* — **The scheduled-task enable/disable button renders as a solid filled square — `data-icon="check"` has no attribute mapping in icons.css**
  - *Evidence:* templates/scheduling.html:192-195 renders the toggle as `<button class="btn-icon-sm scheduling-action-toggle" data-action="click->dc-scheduling#toggleScheduledTask" … data-icon="check">`. static/icons.css defines the attribute form at :194-210 (`[data-icon]::before { content:''; width:1em; height:1em; background-color: currentColor; mask-size: contain; … }`) and then maps exactly 34 attribute values at :272-311 — `check` is not among them (only the *class* form `.icon-check` exists, at :227). With no `mask-image` resolved, the `::before` paints as a bare 1em `currentColor` block. Verified visually: shots/desktop-dark/scheduling.png, Scheduled Tasks row, ACTIONS column — the toggle shows a solid grey square where its siblings show a pencil and an ×, and the button is ~85px wide against its 28px siblings because the unmasked block plus `margin-right: var(--sp-2)` inflates it. A cross-check of every `data-icon="…"` value used in templates/ and static/controllers/ against the icons.css attribute map shows `check` is the only unmapped one.
  - *Fix:* Add `[data-icon="check"]::before { -webkit-mask-image: var(--icon-check); mask-image: var(--icon-check); }` to static/icons.css alongside the :272-311 block, and mirror it into dev/design-system/icons.css — that file is the one strict-synced design-system asset, so the canonical copy must carry the mapping too. Better: the control is a *toggle*, so swap the glyph on state (`check` when enabled, `circle-x`/`square` when disabled) rather than showing a static check for both.

### `scheduling (mobile)` (2)

- **HIGH** · *responsive* — **Mobile hides the Status column of the scheduled-tasks table, removing the only readable enabled/disabled cue**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:1708-1711`
  - *Evidence:* app.css:1701-1711 hides `.scheduling-tasks-table` columns 3 AND 4 at ≤768px — column 3 is Type, column 4 is Status (scheduling.html:171-175). The jobs table hides only column 3 (Delivery) and keeps its Status. mobile-dark/scheduling.png confirms: the Scheduled Jobs row still shows a green dot + "active", while the Scheduled Tasks row shows Title / Schedule / Actions only — no dot, no "enabled"/"disabled" text. The sole remaining state signal is the toggle button, which per the icon finding renders as an identical blank square in both states. A user on mobile cannot tell whether a scheduled task is running.
  - *Fix:* Keep column 4 (Status) visible on mobile and hide only column 3 (Type) — matching the jobs table's rule. Alternatively inline the status dot into the Title cell so it survives any column collapse.

- med · *responsive* — **Mobile: the table fade overlay paints over the table's right border unconditionally, so scheduling tables never close**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:636-640`
  - *Evidence:* app.css:636-640 attaches `.table-fade-wrap::after { position:absolute; right:0; width:32px; background: linear-gradient(to right, transparent, var(--bg-base)) }` at every viewport ≤768px, with no scroll-state condition. At 768px the tables are `min-width: 460px` (app.css:1713-1716) inside a ~744px column — they do not overflow, so the fade is pure damage. Pixel-sampled mobile-dark/scheduling.png: the jobs table's left border is crisp rgb(49,50,68) at x=12 and its top border is rgb(49,50,68) at y=368, but scanning x=744→767 at both a body row (y=437) and the header (y=390) finds no border pixel at all — values decay smoothly to rgb(30,30,46) = `--bg-base`. The bordered container visibly bleeds off the right edge on both tables.
  - *Fix:* Gate the fade on actual overflow — toggle a class from the scheduling controller (or use `scroll-timeline`/`animation-timeline` where supported) — or at minimum inset the pseudo-element by 1px (`right: 1px`) so the container's own border survives.

### `session-info` (2)

- med · *copy-content* — **Session-info "Created" prints a raw ISO-8601 timestamp with milliseconds**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/web/web_routes.dart:377`
  - *Evidence:* shots/desktop-dark/session-info.png and all other session-info captures show `Created  2026-04-15T10:00:00.000`. Source: `createdAt: session.createdAt.toIso8601String()` (packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/web/web_routes.dart:377), passed straight through session_info.dart:95 into the `.meta-value` at session_info.html:59 with no formatting. The same page formats turn times correctly as "10:00" three sections below (`.turn-time`, session_info.html:72), so the surface is internally inconsistent.
  - *Fix:* Format `createdAt` for display (e.g. "15 Apr 2026, 10:00" plus a relative "3 months ago"), keeping the ISO string in a `title` attribute.

- med · *layout-spacing* — **Session-info Token Usage puts three metric cards in a two-column grid, leaving an orphaned card and 50% dead space**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:433`
  - *Evidence:* `.token-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: var(--sp-3) }` (app.css:433) while `sessionInfoTemplate` emits exactly three `metricCardTemplate` calls — Input, Output, Total (session_info.dart:59-76). Visible in shots/desktop-dark/session-info.png and shots/mobile-light/session-info.png: Input and Output fill row 1, Total sits alone in row 2 with an empty 430px column to its right, inside a `.well` that draws a border around the void. The 2-column rule is also unconditional, so the same broken shape persists at 768px.
  - *Fix:* Use `grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(180px, 1fr))` (or an explicit 3-up) on `.token-grid` so three cards form one row and collapse cleanly on narrow viewports.

### `settings/tab-bar` (2)

- med · *accessibility* — **Tab controls are dead anchors pointing at non-existent ids, with no tab ARIA semantics**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/settings.html:13`
  - *Evidence:* `settings.html:14-23` renders ten `<a href="#agent">…<a href="#security">`; grep for `id="agent"`, `id="workflow"`, `id="server"`, `id="security"` in settings.html returns nothing — no element carries any of those ids, so every link is a dangling fragment that does nothing until `dc_settings_controller.js:3-13` intercepts it with `preventDefault()`. There is no `role="tablist"`, `role="tab"`, `role="tabpanel"`, `aria-selected`, or `aria-controls` anywhere in the file (grep for `role=` returns zero hits); the controller sets only `aria-current="page"` (`:20`), which announces "current page" for an in-page panel switch. The panels are hidden with inline `card.style.display = 'none'` (`:31`) with no `aria-hidden`/`hidden` pairing. The app's other tab bar (`app.css:1056`, memory dashboard) at least uses real `<button>` elements.
  - *Fix:* Switch to `<button role="tab" aria-selected aria-controls="panel-agent">` inside `role="tablist"`, give each `[data-tab]` card `role="tabpanel" id="panel-…"`, toggle the `hidden` attribute instead of inline display, and drop `aria-current="page"`.

- med · *responsive* — **Ten-tab row clips mid-word at 768px with no overflow affordance**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:1167`
  - *Evidence:* mobile-dark/settings.png: "Providers" is cut in half at the right viewport edge and "Security" is entirely off-screen. `app.css:1167-1178` sets `flex-wrap: nowrap; overflow-x: auto;` with only `scrollbar-width: thin` — on macOS/iOS overlay scrollbars the 4px thumb (`app.css:1181`) is invisible until a scroll gesture starts, so nothing signals that 1.5 tabs exist past the fold. The app's *other* tab implementation (`app.css:1056`, `.tab-bar`) solves the same problem the opposite way: `@media (max-width: 768px) { .tab-bar { flex-wrap: wrap; } }`.
  - *Fix:* Add an edge fade to `.settings-tabs` (`mask-image: linear-gradient(90deg, #000 0 calc(100% - 24px), transparent)`) and/or adopt the `.tab-bar` mobile behaviour (`flex-wrap: wrap`) so all ten sections are reachable without a hidden horizontal scroll.

### `workflows` (2)

- **HIGH** · *state-coverage* — **Completed workflow runs render '0/0 steps' with a 0%-filled meter when the stored definition is missing or unparseable**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/web/pages/workflows_page.dart:110`
  - *Evidence:* workflows_page.dart:107-125: `try { definition = WorkflowDefinition.fromJson(run.definitionJson); } catch (_) {}` then `totalSteps = definition?.steps.length ?? 0`, `completedSteps = definition == null ? 0 : ...`, `progressPercent = totalSteps > 0 ? ... : 0`. An unknown value is thus rendered as hard data. desktop-dark/workflows.png proves it: all four seeded runs — three badged 'Completed', one 'Failed' — display '0/0 steps' with an empty `.meter` (workflow_list.html:48-54). A Completed run showing 0/0 and 0% progress is self-contradictory on its face.
  - *Fix:* Make the null case explicit: when `definition == null` or `totalSteps == 0`, omit the `.meter` and render the absent-value placeholder in place of '0/0 steps' rather than fabricating zeros. Keep the meter only when a real step count exists.

- low · *layout-spacing* — **"AVAILABLE WORKFLOWS" uses the sidebar's label class, so it is inset 16px from the cards it labels; the runs list above has no section label at all**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/workflow_list.html:69`
  - *Evidence:* workflow_list.html:69 `<div class="sidebar-section-label">Available Workflows</div>`. `.sidebar-section-label` (design-system.css:248-255) carries `padding: var(--sp-2) var(--sp-4) var(--sp-1)` — a 16px left inset tuned for the sidebar rail. The content-area equivalent `.section-label` (design-system.css:1534-1541) has identical type but `margin-bottom: var(--sp-2)` and no inset. Visible in desktop-dark/workflows.png: the label starts at x≈415 while the definition cards below start at x≈400. The runs list (workflow_list.html:37) carries no label at all, so the page's two sections are unlabelled-then-labelled.
  - *Fix:* Swap to `class="section-label"` and add a matching `.section-label` above the runs list (e.g. "Recent Runs") so both sections are peers.

### `workflows / workflow-detail` (2)

- med · *layout-spacing* — **Spacing is applied twice on both workflow pages: `.content-inner` flex `gap: sp-6` plus per-block `margin-bottom: sp-4`, and an always-rendered empty `.workflow-actions` adds ~64px of dead air**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:2415`
  - *Evidence:* design-system.css:1556-1561 makes `.content-inner` a flex column with `gap: var(--sp-6)` (24px). Every direct child in app.css then adds its own bottom margin: `.workflow-metrics-grid` (2387, mb sp-4), `.workflow-progress-section` (2415, mb sp-4), `.workflow-actions` (2434, mb sp-4), `.workflow-filters` (2742, mb sp-4). Net 40px between every block. Measured in desktop-dark/workflows.png: filter card bottom y=162 → first run card top y=203 = 41px. Worse, workflow_detail.html:47 renders `<div class="workflow-actions">` unconditionally — on a Completed run every button inside is `tl:if`-suppressed, so an empty 0-height flex box still consumes 24 (gap) + 24 (gap) + 16 (margin) = 64px, producing the ~100px void between "0 / 0 steps complete" (y=209) and "No steps defined." (y=310) in desktop-dark/workflow-detail.png.
  - *Fix:* Drop the redundant `margin-bottom: var(--sp-4)` from the four `.workflow-*` blocks and let `.content-inner`'s gap own vertical rhythm; wrap `.workflow-actions` in a `tl:if="${hasActions}"` guard computed in workflow_detail.dart.

- med · *state-coverage* — **Terminal-status runs render a 0% meter and a green "0%" — progress is never coupled to run status, and the percentage color is hardcoded accent**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/web/pages/workflows_page.dart:125`
  - *Evidence:* workflows_page.dart:125 `final progressPercent = totalSteps > 0 ? (completedSteps * 100 ~/ totalSteps) : 0;` — derived only from per-step statuses, with no coupling to `run.status`. In desktop-dark/workflows.png three runs badge "Completed" and one badges "Failed" while all four show "0/0 steps" beside an empty 60px `.meter` track (app.css:2815 `.workflow-run-meter { width: 60px; height: 4px }`), which reads as a stray dash. desktop-dark/workflow-detail.png shows "Completed" in the STATUS tile above "0 / 0 steps complete · 0%". The percentage is also always green: app.css:2428-2431 `.workflow-progress-pct { color: var(--accent) }` with no failed/cancelled variant, so a Failed run's percentage still renders in the success hue — the exact ambiguity DESIGN.md § Status vocabulary forbids.
  - *Fix:* Clamp `progressPercent`/`completedSteps` to `totalSteps` for terminal statuses (completed → 100%), suppress the meter when `totalSteps == 0`, and drive `.workflow-progress-pct` + `.meter-fill` from the run status (`--error` on failed, `--warning` on paused/cancelled) instead of a fixed `--accent`.

### `channel-detail/google-chat` (1)

- **HIGH** · *interaction-glitch* — **Google Chat renders a dead "Pairing / Registration" primary button (anchor with no href)**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/channel_detail.html:34`
  - *Evidence:* channel_detail.dart:45-49 resolves `pairingHref` only for `whatsapp` and `signal`; every other channelType gets `null`. channel_detail.html:34 renders the anchor unconditionally: `<a tl:attr="href=${pairingHref}" class="btn btn-sm btn-primary">Pairing / Registration</a>`. desktop-dark/channel-gchat.png shows the filled accent button present on the Google Chat page, where no `/google_chat/pairing` route exists. An `<a>` without `href` is not keyboard-focusable and has no link role, so the loudest control on the page is inert for both mouse and AT users.
  - *Fix:* Wrap the anchor in `tl:if="${pairingHref}"` (same guard already used for the Disconnect form on channel_detail.html:35), so Google Chat's hero shows no pairing CTA.

### `chat, task-detail, kg-timeline` (1)

- **HIGH** · *interaction-glitch* — **Chat and timeline force scroll-to-bottom on every stream event and every HTMX swap, with no isAtBottom guard**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/controllers/shared.js:224`
  - *Evidence:* `scrollToBottom` is unconditional — `messages.scrollTop = messages.scrollHeight` with no check of `scrollTop + clientHeight >= scrollHeight - ε` (static/controllers/shared.js:224-229). It is called on *every* SSE frame during a turn: dc_chat_controller.js:326-331 `handleSseMessage() { … scrollToBottom(this.element); }` fires for delta, tool_use and tool_result alike. So scrolling up to re-read an earlier message while the agent streams yanks the view back to the bottom several times a second. It is called again globally by the shell on every HTMX swap and history event (dc_shell_controller.js:137, 149, 157) and once more after every turn finalize (dc_chat_controller.js:371). The same pattern repeats for timelines: `applyTimelineAutoScroll()` sets `container.scrollTop = container.scrollHeight` on every `htmx:afterSettle` document-wide (dc_shell_controller.js:427-430, bound at :36). The load-earlier path proves the guard is understood — dc_shell_controller.js:135-139 deliberately skips `scrollToBottom` when the source matches `[data-load-earlier]` — but that exemption was never generalized.
  - *Fix:* Add an `isAtBottom(el, threshold = 32)` check inside `scrollToBottom` (and inside `applyTimelineAutoScroll`) and no-op when the user has scrolled away; optionally surface a 'jump to latest' affordance when suppressed.

### `chat-session` (1)

- med · *state-coverage* — **The empty-chat state renders a solid green square: `<div class="icon">❯_</div>` collides with the icon system's `.icon` base rule**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/components.html:7`
  - *Evidence:* components.html:6-10 (the fragment `chat.dart:95` injects whenever a session has no messages) is `<div class="icon">&#10095;_</div>`. The icon system's base rule is `.icon { display:inline-block; width:1em; height:1em; background-color: currentColor; mask-size: contain; … }` (icons.css:179-192) — with no `mask-image` set, `background-color: currentColor` paints the full 1em box, so at `.empty-state .icon { font-size: var(--text-2xl); color: var(--accent) }` (design-system.css:1517-1522) this is a solid 24px accent square with the "❯_" glyph overflowing it. components.html:6 and :14 are the only two places in the codebase that use bare `class="icon"` with text content; DESIGN.md:817-831 states `.icon` is valid only paired with an `icon-<name>` modifier. Compounding it, the canon also ships an unreachable competing empty state — `.messages:empty::after { content: 'Start a conversation...' }` (design-system.css:424-431) can never fire because `.messages` always receives `emptyStateTemplate()`. And the canon's own showcase renders this state with the 8-bit crab mascot at 64px plus a primary CTA (showcase.html:904-909), none of which the app uses.
  - *Fix:* Drop the `class="icon"` from components.html:7 and :14 (or replace the glyph with the `.pixel-art` mascot + a `.btn-primary` CTA per showcase.html:904-909), and delete the dead `.messages:empty::after` rule from the canon.

### `global (knowledge-hub, kg-timeline, wiki-doc citations)` (1)

- med · *responsive* — **Three responsive blocks use max-width:767px while the rest of the app uses 768px — at exactly 768px the app renders a half-mobile hybrid**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:3006,3204,3393`
  - *Evidence:* design-system.css (lines 107, 170, 295, 650, 1564, 1918, 1996, 2098) and 30+ app.css blocks all key on `@media (max-width: 768px)`; app.css:3006, app.css:3204 and app.css:3393 key on `@media (max-width: 767px)`. At a 768px viewport (standard tablet portrait, and the width these screenshots were captured at) the 768px rules fire and the 767px rules do not. Proof in shots/mobile-dark/knowledge-hub.png: the sidebar has collapsed to the hamburger drawer and `.tab-btn`s show mobile spacing, yet `.knowledge-summary-strip` still renders 4 columns side-by-side (WIKI/KG/MEMORY/INBOX at ~180px each) and `.knowledge-search-form` still renders input+button on one row — because app.css:3204 `{ .knowledge-search-form, .knowledge-summary-strip, .knowledge-result-row { grid-template-columns: 1fr } }` is gated at 767px. Same for `.kg-timeline-controls` (app.css:3393) in shots/mobile-dark/kg-timeline.png, and `.citation-marker` (app.css:3006) never receives its `min-width/min-height: 44px` touch target at 768px even though every sibling touch rule (app.css:3755) fired.
  - *Fix:* Change app.css:3006, 3204 and 3393 to `@media (max-width: 768px)`. Add a fitness/lint check that the only mobile breakpoint literal permitted in app.css/design-system.css is 768px (plus the deliberate 600px/900px/1024px tiers), so the boundary can't drift again.

### `global (memory-dashboard, task-detail, channel pairing)` (1)

- **HIGH** · *layout-spacing* — **Below-fold canvas leak: pages that overflow 100dvh expose the browser default canvas (#121212 dark, #ffffff light) instead of the design ground**
  - *Evidence:* `.shell` is `height: 100dvh` with `grid-template-rows: 48px 1fr` (design-system.css:87-92), but the scrolling grid item — `.content-area` (design-system.css:1550-1554, `flex: 1; overflow-y: auto`, a flex declaration on a grid child) and the app's `.page-content` (app.css:598) — never sets `min-height: 0`, so grid `min-height: auto` lets it overflow the shell. `.chat-area` (design-system.css:414) is the only container that got it right. `html` carries no background, so body's `background-attachment: fixed` gradient is viewport-anchored and the overflow strip falls back to the UA canvas. Measured: desktop-dark/memory.png is 1333px tall and every pixel below y=900 is #121212 (700,905 / 700,1000 / 700,1330 / 130,1000); desktop-light/memory.png is pure #ffffff below y=900 — neither colour exists in either palette (lightest Latte token is bg-base #eff1f5). Also affected: desktop-dark/task-detail.png (921px, #121212 at 700,910), mobile-dark memory.png (1705px), channel-signal.png, channel-whatsapp.png, channel-gchat.png.
  - *Fix:* Add `min-height: 0` to `.content-area` and `.page-content` (matching `.chat-area`), and as a belt-and-braces move the ground declaration from `body` to `html` so any residual overflow still paints the gradient.

### `global (mobile shell)` (1)

- med · *accessibility* — **The mobile off-canvas drawer has no Escape handler, no focus move, and no focus containment — keyboard focus tabs straight into the page behind the scrim**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/controllers/dc_shell_controller.js:229`
  - *Evidence:* dc_shell_controller.js `setSidebarOpen(open)` (lines ~229-246) toggles `.open`, flips `scrim.aria-hidden`/`tabIndex` and updates the toggle's `aria-expanded`/`aria-label` — and does nothing else. It never calls `.focus()` on anything inside the drawer, never marks `#main-content`/`.topbar` `inert` or `aria-hidden`, and never restores focus to `.menu-toggle` on close. `handleDocumentKeydown` (lines ~122-131) intercepts `Escape` only to `closeAllCustomSelects()`, so Escape does not dismiss the drawer. Result at ≤768px: after opening the drawer, Tab moves from `.menu-toggle` through the drawer and then into the visually-obscured page content and topbar underneath the scrim, with no way to close via keyboard. DESIGN.md:427-446 specifies the drawer markup contract but stops before behavior, and DESIGN.md:986-994 requires semantic, keyboard-operable drawer controls.
  - *Fix:* In `setSidebarOpen`: on open, focus the `.sidebar-close` button and set `inert` on `#main-content` and `.topbar`; on close, remove `inert` and return focus to `.menu-toggle`. Add `if (event.key === 'Escape' && document.getElementById('sidebar')?.classList.contains('open')) this.setSidebarOpen(false);` to `handleDocumentKeydown`. Document the behavior contract (focus move, Escape, inert background) alongside the markup in DESIGN.md § Mobile sidebar contract.

### `health-dashboard` (1)

- **HIGH** · *color-depth* — **.card-featured-* gradient border never paints — the health hero card renders completely borderless**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/design-system.css:1075-1147 (canon: dev/design-system/components.css:1076-1147)`
  - *Evidence:* Pixel-sampled desktop-dark/health.png: the plain `.card` service cards have a crisp border at rgb(49,50,68) against a mantle fill of rgb(24,24,37). The `card-featured-accent` status card's border ring measures rgb(24-27,24-27,36-40) on every edge (left x=397-404 y=180; top y=70-71 across x=410..1290; bottom y=237) — indistinguishable from its own fill. Light theme: featured border rgb(223,227,234) vs fill rgb(229,232,237) (Δ6), while plain cards show rgb(204,208,218) vs rgb(230,233,239). Cause: design-system.css:1075-1083 declares `background: var(--bg-mantle) padding-box, linear-gradient(...) border-box;` — a bare <color> in a non-final background layer is not permitted by the `background` shorthand grammar (<'background-color'> is only valid in <final-bg-layer>), so the gradient layer never renders; only the `border: 1px solid transparent` survives. All 8 rules (.card-featured-{accent,info,error,warning} base + :hover) share the defect, in the served copy AND in canon dev/design-system/components.css:1076-1147. Net effect: the "maximum emphasis" variant is strictly LESS visible than a plain .card.
  - *Fix:* Use the standard gradient-border idiom — replace the color layer with an image: `background: linear-gradient(var(--bg-mantle), var(--bg-mantle)) padding-box, linear-gradient(135deg, ...) border-box;` in dev/design-system/components.css and resync design-system.css. Apply to all 8 .card-featured-* rules.

### `kg-timeline` (1)

- low · *copy-content* — **"↺ now" resets the category filter too, and uses a non-canon Unicode glyph instead of an icon**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/kg_timeline.html:30`
  - *Evidence:* kg_timeline.html:30 — `<a class="btn btn-ghost" href="/knowledge/timeline">↺ now</a>`. The label names only the time dimension ("now"), but the bare href drops every query parameter, so clicking it also clears the Category filter the user typed into the adjacent field. Secondly, `↺` (U+21BA) is a raw Unicode glyph in a system that ships masked icons (`--icon-clock`, `--icon-rotate`-family in icons.css) and whose canon enumerates its Unicode exceptions exhaustively — `❯`, `·`, `•`, `—`, `…`, `&`, `█`, `> `, `💬`, `📋` (DESIGN.md:889-895); `↺` is not among them, and it renders as a tiny mismatched-weight mark next to the mono type.
  - *Fix:* Label it for what it does — "Reset filters" — or make it preserve `category` and only drop `as_of` if the intent is time-only. Replace the `↺` character with `<span class="icon icon-…" aria-hidden="true"></span>` from icons.css.

### `knowledge-wiki-doc` (1)

- **HIGH** · *state-coverage* — **Wiki documents are served as raw text/plain markdown — no shell, no rendering, no way back**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/web/web_routes.dart:558`
  - *Evidence:* web_routes.dart:558 `return Response.ok(file.readAsStringSync(), headers: {...htmlHeaders, 'content-type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8'});`. desktop-dark/knowledge-wiki-doc.png shows the result: unrendered markdown source (`# Wiki`, backticks, `-` bullets) in the browser's default monospace, with no sidebar, no topbar, no back link — a hard dead end reached from the source link in every wiki search result (knowledge_hub.html:52). The app already renders markdown elsewhere (task_detail.html:176 `data-markdown`, vendored highlight.js + DOMPurify per static/VENDORS.md). Related: the same route's four failure paths (:536, :539, :545, :548, :553, :556) all return the same opaque 'Wiki source not found' with no indication whether the file is missing, outside the wiki root, or the workspace is unconfigured.
  - *Fix:* Render the wiki document inside the app shell using the existing `data-markdown` pipeline with a breadcrumb back to /knowledge, instead of returning text/plain. Give the not-found path a page-level empty state naming the requested path.

### `scheduling, task-detail timeline` (1)

- med · *component-adoption* — **Undefined icon names render as solid filled squares instead of glyphs**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/icons.css:195`
  - *Evidence:* Both icon mechanisms in static/icons.css set `background-color: currentColor` with mask properties but *no* `mask-image` in the base rule — `.icon` at icons.css:179-192 and `[data-icon]::before` at icons.css:195-211 — so an unmapped name paints an opaque 1em square rather than nothing. Diffing every name used in templates/ and static/controllers/ against icons.css: `data-icon="check"` (templates/scheduling.html:195, the scheduled-task enable/disable toggle) has no `[data-icon="check"]` rule — that is the small solid square visible in the Actions column of shots/desktop-dark/scheduling.png. Three `.icon-*` names are likewise unmapped: `icon-file-json`, `icon-file-warning` (templates/task_event_display.dart:11-14) and `icon-layers` (:18), so structured-output and compaction timeline events render as squares. Their colour-accent classes are dead too: `.task-event-icon-warning`, `.task-event-icon-compaction`, `.tl-event-warning` and `.tl-event-compaction` (task_event_display.dart:34-35, 55, 60) have no rule in app.css or design-system.css, while all their siblings do.
  - *Fix:* Add the four missing mask mappings (`check`, `file-json`, `file-warning`, `layers`) plus the four missing accent classes; and give the base `.icon` / `[data-icon]::before` rules a visible fallback (e.g. a `--icon-fallback` question-mark mask) so a future typo fails loudly rather than as a filled block.

### `settings/agent` (1)

- **HIGH** · *interaction-glitch* — **Agent "Effort" renders as an enabled select whose only option is an em dash — the field cannot be set**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/settings.html:51`
  - *Evidence:* desktop-dark/settings.png shows the Effort control displaying "—". `settings.html:51-53` hardcodes `<select …><option value="">—</option></select>`; `dc_settings_controller.js:163-173` only injects real options when `meta.fields['agent.effort'].allowedValues` exists, and `packages/dartclaw_config/lib/src/config_meta.dart:320-325` defines `agent.effort` with `type: string, mutability: restart, nullable: true` and **no `allowedValues`** (unlike `logging.level`, `logging.format`, `sessions.dm_scope`, `search.backend`, which all declare them). Line 181 then does `input.disabled = false`, so the user gets a live, focusable picker containing one blank choice. `agent.provider` (`settings.html:35`) has the mirror-image defect: an enumerable value rendered as free text because its FieldMeta also omits `allowedValues`.
  - *Fix:* Add `allowedValues: ['low','medium','high', …]` to the `agent.effort` FieldMeta (and the provider id list to `agent.provider`), and give the empty option a real label ("Default") rather than an em dash.

### `settings/all-tabs` (1)

- **HIGH** · *state-coverage* — **Dirty-state tracking is dead code — Save is always enabled and unsaved edits vanish silently on tab switch**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/controllers/dc_settings_controller.js:203`
  - *Evidence:* `dc_settings_controller.js:203-226` `updateFormDirtyState(form)` walks every field, computes `var dirty = false; ... if (current !== initial) dirty = true;` and then **returns without ever using `dirty`** — the function body ends at line 226 with no assignment, class toggle, or button state. It is nevertheless wired to `input`, `change`, submit-failure and cancel (`:330, :345, :366, :385, :404, :409`). Consequences visible in desktop-dark/settings.png: the Save button is rendered enabled on a pristine form; pressing it hits `dc_settings_controller.js:295-298` and emits an "info: No changes" toast. Worse, `activateSettingsTab` (`:30-32`) hides the current card with `card.style.display = 'none'` with no dirty check, so editing Agent and clicking Providers hides unsaved edits (and their Save button) with zero warning.
  - *Fix:* Either finish the function — `form.classList.toggle('is-dirty', dirty)`, disable `.form-actions .btn-primary` when clean, and mark changed `[data-field]` rows — or delete it and its six call sites. At minimum add a dirty guard to `activateSettingsTab` before hiding a card.

### `settings/restart-banner` (1)

- low · *component-adoption* — **Restart banner buttons omit the base .btn class and fall back to UA button chrome**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/restart_banner.html:7`
  - *Evidence:* `restart_banner.html:7-8` renders `class="btn-sm btn-primary"` and `class="btn-sm btn-ghost dismiss"` — with no `btn`. `design-system.css:347-364` puts `display: inline-flex`, `border: var(--border)`, `border-radius: var(--radius)`, `cursor`, the inset top-lit edge and the transitions on `.btn` alone; `.btn-primary` (`:376`) only supplies background/color/border-**color**, and `.btn-sm` (`app.css:569`) only font-size/padding. The result is a square-cornered UA-default button wearing an accent border-colour. This is the only such omission in the whole templates directory — every other button is `class="btn btn-…"` (`btn-icon-sm` in scheduling.html is a self-contained component). Not visible in the captured screenshots because no restart was pending at capture time.
  - *Fix:* Change to `class="btn btn-sm btn-primary"` and `class="btn btn-sm btn-ghost dismiss"`.

### `settings/security` (1)

- **HIGH** · *dialog-feedback* — **Guard editor affordances are inverted: Delete fires with no confirmation, Edit opens two window.prompt() dialogs**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/controllers/dc_settings_controller.js:597`
  - *Evidence:* `dc_settings_controller.js:597-601`: the delete handler goes straight from click to `fetch(..., {method:'DELETE'})` with no `confirm()` — a security guard rule is removed on a single click. Meanwhile the non-destructive edit path (`:569`, `:573`) fires `window.prompt('Guard extension value', …)` and then, for file rules, a **second** `window.prompt('File access level', …)` where the user must free-type one of `no_access` / `read_only` / `no_delete` — even though the Add form six lines away in the same component already renders those exact three values as a proper `<select>` (`settings.html:502-506`). DESIGN.md § Banners and toasts defines the whole feedback vocabulary as banner / toast / `confirm()` for destructive confirmation; `prompt()` appears nowhere in the canon.
  - *Fix:* Add `confirm()` to the delete path (canon's destructive-confirmation mechanism), and replace both prompts with inline row editing that reuses the existing `[data-guard-editor-field]` / `[data-guard-editor-value]` / `[data-guard-editor-level]` controls, so editing a file rule offers the same three-option select as adding one.

### `shell/restart-banner` (1)

- med · *dialog-feedback* — **Restart-banner buttons omit the base `.btn` class, so they fall back to native browser button rendering**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/restart_banner.html:7`
  - *Evidence:* restart_banner.html:7-8 uses `class="btn-sm btn-primary"` and `class="btn-sm btn-ghost dismiss"` — neither carries `.btn`. `.btn-sm` in app.css:569 sets only `font-size` and `padding`; every structural property (`display:inline-flex`, `align-items`, `gap`, `border-radius: var(--radius)`, `border: var(--border)`, `cursor`, `font: inherit`, the top-lit `inset 0 1px 0` edge) lives on `.btn` (design-system.css:347-366). `.btn-primary` (design-system.css:376-381) supplies `background`/`color`/`border-color` but no `border-style`, so no border paints. Net effect when the banner appears: a green rectangle in the UA's default proportional system font with the UA's default border-radius, inside the app's all-monospace chrome.
