The same banner markup is included by 14 templates (`rg bannerHtml *.html`), and its nesting is inconsistent — inside `.content-inner` (settings.html:7), inside `.page-inner` (tasks.html:7), or bare inside `<main>` (health_dashboard.html:5) — so its width and top offset also differ per page.
  - *Fix:* Change to `class="btn btn-sm btn-primary"` / `class="btn btn-sm btn-ghost dismiss"` in restart_banner.html, and hoist the banner into the shell (render it once above `<main>` in the topbar/shell fragment) instead of opting in from 14 templates at three different nesting depths.

### `tasks + task-detail` (1)

- med · *typography* — **.provider-badge bakes a context-specific margin-left into the component, producing a ragged left edge, and hardcodes 10px — below the type-scale floor**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:1775`
  - *Evidence:* app.css:1775-1787 sets `margin-left: var(--sp-2)` and `font-size: 0.625rem` on the component itself. Visible consequence in desktop-dark/tasks.png: inside the runner card, "Primary (#0)", "0 turns" and "0 tokens" all start at x=417 while the CLAUDE badge starts at x=436. Same 8px indent in the task-detail meta card (desktop-dark/task-detail.png: "PROVIDER" label at x=420, CLAUDE badge at x=428) and in the table body, where the CLAUDE pill hangs right of its own PROVIDER column header. 0.625rem = 10px sits below `--text-xs` (12px), the documented floor of the scale (DESIGN.md:378, "Smaller sizes carry metadata and pill text (12–13px)"), and it is uppercase with +0.08em tracking — the least legible text on the surface.
  - *Fix:* Drop `margin-left` from `.provider-badge` and let call sites space it (the table cell, meta item and runner metric all already have gap/padding); raise the size to `var(--text-xs)`.

### `workflow-detail` (1)

- low · *interaction-glitch* — **Active-step pulse bar has no positioned ancestor, so it anchors to the step wrapper — 3px outside the card's own left border and 12px taller than the card**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:2492`
  - *Evidence:* app.css:2492-2501 `.workflow-step-active::before { position: absolute; left: -3px; top: 0; bottom: 0; width: 3px; ... }`. The nearest positioned ancestor is `.workflow-step-wrapper` (app.css:2446-2448, `position: relative`) — `.workflow-step-card` never declares `position: relative`, and canon `.card` (design-system.css:784-793) does not set it either. The wrapper also contains the 12px `.workflow-step-connector` stub (app.css:2450-2458), so the pulse bar spans connector + card and sits 3px left of the card's own `border-left: 3px solid` (app.css:2460-2464) — a doubled bar with a gap on every running step. Not visible in the captured screenshots because no run in the seed data has a running step.
  - *Fix:* Add `position: relative` to `.workflow-step-card` and change the pseudo-element to `left: -3px` relative to the card (or drop it entirely when adopting `.pipeline-step--running .pipeline-node::after`, which is the canon pulse).


## C. Adoption gaps — canon has the answer, the app does not use it (118)

The canonical primitive exists and is already served; the page simply does not use it. Mostly template edits.

### `global` (22)

- **HIGH** · *color-depth* — **app.css never applies --shadow-sm; nine hand-rolled card clones drop both the shadow and the luminous top edge that make .card read as raised**
  - *Evidence:* Across app.css (3827 lines, 910 rule blocks) --shadow-sm is used 0 times, --shadow-md 2 times (lines 345, 3236), --shadow-lg 1 time (954), --border-highlight 2 times. Canon `.card` (design-system.css:784-793) sets `box-shadow: var(--shadow-sm)` + `border-top-color: var(--border-highlight)`, but nine app-local containers re-implement the card as bare `background: var(--bg-mantle); border: var(--border); border-radius: var(--radius-lg|--radius)` with neither cue: .metric-card (app.css:591), .heartbeat-card (616), .task-card-running (1740), .task-chat-embed (1853), .task-no-session (1872), .task-review-bar (1908), .agent-runner-card (2090), .knowledge-summary-item (3111), .knowledge-result-row (3141). Visible on desktop-dark/scheduling.png (heartbeat card), tasks.png (Primary #0 runner card) and knowledge-hub.png (the four summary tiles) — all read as flat rectangles with a hairline, unlike the health.png service cards which do get the top highlight (measured #2a2a36 at the card's first pixel row).
  - *Fix:* Delete the nine clones' surface declarations and compose on `.card` (adding a modifier class for layout only), so shadow-sm + border-highlight + hover lift come from canon.

- **HIGH** · *color-depth* — **`--fg-overlay` (the disabled/placeholder tier) is the app's most-used foreground token — 82 uses vs 75 for `fg-sub0` and 12 for `fg-sub1` — and carries essential metadata the canon forbids it for**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:583`
  - *Evidence:* DESIGN.md:990: "`fg-overlay` is intentionally low-emphasis; use it for placeholders/disabled/helper text, never for essential metadata or table labels." app.css violates this in the highest-traffic rules: `.card-row-label { color: var(--fg-overlay) }` (app.css:583) paints *every* Health service card label (Uptime/Version/Worker/State/Runtime/Size/FTS5 Index/Type — desktop-dark/health.png); `.detail-label` (app.css:563) paints task-detail's TYPE/STATUS/PROVIDER/CREATED BY/CREATED/STARTED/COMPLETED row; `.settings-header p` (489) the page subtitle; `.info-subtitle` (432) the session ID; `.metric-card .metric-label` (595) the workflow-detail KPI labels; `.empty-state-title` and `.empty-state-text` (1809-1810) all empty-state copy; `.metric-sub-inline` (1038) the "/ 50" denominators on memory KPIs; `.error-code` (313) the 404 hero. Measured contrast on `--bg-mantle` (#181825): `--fg-overlay` #7f849c = 4.75:1; `--fg-sub0` #a6adc8 = 7.9:1. On `--bg-base` (#1e1e2e) `--fg-overlay` drops to ~4.46:1, below AA. This one token choice, repeated 82×, is the largest single cause of the 'flat and gray' reading — most non-value text in the product sits one step above invisible.
  - *Fix:* Reserve `--fg-overlay` for placeholders, disabled controls, and genuine helper text. Reclassify: labels/metadata/table headers → `--fg-sub0`; empty-state and card body copy → `--fg-sub1`; page subtitles → `--fg-sub0`. Concretely retier app.css:432, 489, 563, 583, 595, 1038, 1809, 1810 first — they cover Health, Settings, Tasks, task-detail, Memory, and every empty state.

- **HIGH** · *component-adoption* — **.status-pill and all four variants are unused app-wide while app.css carries ~30 bespoke badge/pill families — every status in the product renders at the lowest-weight treatment**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:576`
  - *Evidence:* DESIGN.md:598 defines status pills as the gradient-filled, higher-weight status treatment "for card footers, table cells, compact status summaries", variants `--live/--error/--warning/--info` (components.css `.status-pill*`). Verified: `.status-pill`, `.status-pill--live`, `--error`, `--warning`, `--info` = ZERO references in templates/, *.dart, controllers/, and app.css. Meanwhile app.css defines a bespoke badge/pill sprawl next to canon `.status-badge`: `.card-badge`(576), `.badge-success/-error/-warning/-muted`(577-580), `.delivery-badge`(645), `.channel-meta-pill`(1311), `.entry-state-badge`(1492), `.type-badge`(1753), `.provider-badge`(1775), `.task-agent-badge`(2275), `.kg-state-badge`(3346), `.kg-window-pill`(3376), plus `.layer-badge`, `.mode-badge`, `.nav-badge`, `.pairing-badge`, `.restart-badge`, `.system-badge`, `.live-badge`, `.default-badge`, `.channel-mode-badge`, `.workflow-badge`, `.workflow-loop-badge`, `.workflow-parallel-badge`, `.workflow-artifact-badge`, `.tl-detail-badge`. desktop-dark/tasks.png shows every STATUS cell (review/accepted) as a faint outlined `.status-badge` — exactly the flat-gray reading; canon's gradient `.status-pill` is the documented answer for table cells.
  - *Fix:* Adopt `.status-pill--{live|error|warning|info}` for terminal/running status in the tasks and scheduling table STATUS columns and in card footers; collapse `.card-badge`/`.badge-*`/`.type-badge`/`.entry-state-badge`/`.kg-state-badge`/`.delivery-badge` onto `.status-badge` + `.status-badge-{variant}` and delete the duplicates.

- **HIGH** · *component-adoption* — **77 class names used in templates have no CSS rule anywhere — the whole projects page layer is unstyled**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/projects.html:15`
  - *Evidence:* Extracting every literal `class="…"` token from templates/*.html and templates/*.dart (705 unique) and matching each against the concatenation of app.css + design-system.css + icons.css + tokens.css yields 77 with no matching rule. The cluster that shows: all seven `project-*` classes — `project-card`, `project-card-header`, `project-card-title-row`, `project-card-actions`, `project-card-meta`, `project-url`, `project-error-banner` (templates/projects.html:15-46) — are entirely unstyled, so in shots/desktop-dark/projects.png the card has no internal rhythm, 'Fetch' sits flush under the title row, and the error text 'GitHub projects require a GitHub token credential reference.' renders as plain body copy despite the adjacent Error badge. A second concrete instance: `templates/memory_dashboard.html:187` and `:193` mark the Count column `class="text-right"`, but `.text-right` does not exist, so the numeric columns stay left-aligned against `.data-table td { text-align: left }` (design-system.css:1573-1577) — confirmed in shots/desktop-dark/memory.png. Other silent no-ops include `.notice`/`.notice-warning` (knowledge_hub.html:41 partial-failure warning), `.btn-secondary` (tasks.html:22, workflow_detail.html:133), `.archive-notice` (chat.html:56), `.form-help-text` (task_form.dart:122), `.font-mono` (settings.html:501,510), `.workflow-approval-*`, `.workflow-launch-*`, `.pairing-*` and `.sidebar-*-label`.
  - *Fix:* Write the missing rules for the load-bearing clusters (project card, workflow approval/launch, pairing, notice) reusing canon primitives — `.card` sections, `.banner banner-error` for `project-error-banner`, `.data-table` numeric alignment — and delete the genuinely vestigial tokens. Add a build-time fitness check that fails when a template class has no rule in the served CSS, since this class of rot is invisible at review time.

- **HIGH** · *component-adoption* — **Three parallel KPI-tile implementations render dashboard numbers at 32px, 20px and 16px on different pages**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:590`
  - *Evidence:* (1) Canon `.card.card-metric .metric-value` — `--text-3xl` (32px), weight 600, `--tracking-tight`, semantic colour, radial hover glow (design-system.css:978-1017) — used by memory_dashboard.html:16/35/45, settings.html:348-356, and the shared `metricCardTemplate` (components.html:25, components.dart:53) that health/scheduling/session_info go through. (2) App-only `.metric-card .metric-value` — `--text-xl` (20px), `--fg`, label in `--fg-overlay`, no hover treatment (app.css:590-595) — used only by workflow_detail.html:13-25. (3) `.knowledge-summary-count` — `--text-lg` (16px), weight 500, with a hand-rolled `border: 1px solid var(--bg-surface1)` and `border-radius: var(--radius)` instead of `.card` (app.css:3111-3130) — used by knowledge_hub.html:34-36. Compare desktop-dark/memory.png (large coloured 32px tiles with meters), desktop-dark/workflow-detail.png (visibly smaller, uncoloured 20px tiles) and desktop-dark/knowledge-hub.png (16px counts in flat outlined boxes) — three different answers to 'this is a number that matters', all in one product.
  - *Fix:* Delete `.metric-card` (app.css:590-595) and `.knowledge-summary-strip/-item/-count/-label` (app.css:3104-3130). Render workflow_detail.html:13-25 and knowledge_hub.html:33-38 through `metricCardTemplate` so all KPIs land on `.card.card-metric` with `--text-3xl` and the semantic hover glow.

- **HIGH** · *layout-spacing* — **No shared page-header component: six competing title treatments, and 5 major surfaces have no in-content title or primary-action slot at all**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/health_dashboard.html:15`
  - *Evidence:* `.pagehead` + `.page-title` (app.css:598-614) is used by exactly two templates: projects.html:10 and knowledge_hub.html:9 (+kg_timeline.html). settings.html:9 rolls its own `.settings-header` (app.css:487-489). session_info.html:9 uses a third pair, `.info-title`/`.info-subtitle` (app.css:432). channel_detail.html builds a fourth (identicon + title + subtitle inside a card — see desktop-dark/channel-signal.png). scheduling.html:9 leads with `.heartbeat-card` and uses `.section-header` (app.css:634) as its top-level type. health_dashboard.html:15, tasks.html:12, workflow_list.html and memory_dashboard.html emit **no `<h1>` at all** — the 20px topbar string is the only title (desktop-dark/health.png, tasks.png, workflows.png, scheduling.png, memory.png all open cold with a filter bar or a card). Because `pageTopbar` (topbar.html) exposes only a back link and a theme toggle, there is also no canonical home for a page action, so each page invents one: projects.html:11 top-right of `.pagehead`; tasks.html:18 wedged between two selects in the filter bar; scheduling.html:32 and :118 two competing primaries beside section headings; knowledge_hub.html:26 where the only `btn-primary` is a search submit; settings.html ships nine `btn-primary` "Save" buttons; health/workflows/memory/session_info have none. Labels have no grammar either: "New Task", "Add Project", "+ Add Job", "+ Add Task", "+ New Chat", "Start Task", "Add", "Add entry", "Add group".
  - *Fix:* Add one `pageHeader` fragment to components.html — `<header class="pagehead">` with title (`--text-xl`), optional subtitle (`--text-sm`, `--fg-sub0`), and a right-aligned action slot — and render it on every non-chat surface. Delete `.settings-header` (app.css:487), `.info-title`/`.info-subtitle` (app.css:432), and the ad-hoc channel/scheduling headers. Standardise action labels on verb+noun without a literal `"+ "` prefix (use `data-icon="plus"`).

- **HIGH** · *state-coverage* — **12 of 18 empty states are a bare line of --fg-overlay gray text, via 8 bespoke one-off classes that bypass the canon .empty-state block**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:1808`
  - *Evidence:* Inventory of empty-state sites: only components.html:12-19 (emptyAppState) has icon + title + explanation + action. Five have an icon (tasks.html:106 claw-mark, projects.html:53 claw-mark, task_detail.html:139 💬, components.html:7/14 ❯_). The remaining twelve are one muted line: workflow_list.html:63, workflow_detail.html:140, workflow_step_detail.html:12, task_timeline.html:46, task_detail.html:191/186, knowledge_hub.html:58, kg_timeline.html:41, scheduling.html:109 + :206, channel_detail.html:92/111/175, sidebar.html:24/91, audit_table.dart:38. app.css defines 8 separate bespoke classes for them — `.sidebar-placeholder` (:169), `.table-empty-cell` (:1126), `.allowlist-empty` (:1510), `.pairing-empty` (:1556), `.task-artifact-empty` (:1897), `.tl-empty-state` (:2228), `.knowledge-empty-state` (:3176), `.provider-empty-state` (:3673) — every one resolving to `color: var(--fg-overlay)` or `--fg-sub0`, the two dimmest steps of the fg ladder (tokens.css:27-28). Worse, app.css:1808 redefines the icon as `.empty-state-icon { color: var(--fg-overlay) }`, so the canon's accent-glow rule (design-system.css:1517-1522, `color: var(--accent)` + two-layer text-shadow) never applies to any app empty state. Proven on screen: knowledge-kg.png ('No facts extracted.'), kg-timeline.png ('No temporal KG facts recorded yet.'), health-audit.png ('No guard events recorded yet'), channel-whatsapp.png ('No pending pairing requests' / 'No entries' x2).
  - *Fix:* Collapse the 8 bespoke classes onto the canon `.empty-state` + `.empty-state .icon` block and delete app.css:1808-1810's parallel `-icon/-title/-text` family (or realign it to `--accent` / `--fg`). Give each of the twelve text-only sites the canon three-part shape: claw-mark or mascot, a `--fg`-weight title, one explanatory line, and a primary action where one exists (e.g. 'Run a workflow' on workflow_list.html:63, 'Add job' on scheduling.html:109).

- **HIGH** · *typography* — **The page-title tier (20px/600) is never applied to actual page titles — 13 of 13 page-shell pages render their title at 14px**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/design-system.css:317`
  - *Evidence:* All 13 dashboard pages route through `pageTopbarTemplate` (templates/topbar.dart:43; callers in health_dashboard.dart:47, tasks.dart:40, workflow_list.dart:23, memory_dashboard.dart:21, scheduling.dart:28, settings.dart:72, projects.dart:20, session_info.dart:50, task_detail.dart:40, workflow_detail.dart:33, knowledge_hub.dart:37, kg_timeline.dart:50, channel_detail.dart:39). The title renders as `.session-title-static` = `--text-base` 14px (design-system.css:317-321). `--text-xl` (20px, canon's page-title tier) appears ZERO times in design-system.css — no canon component implements the tier at all. Only 3 templates add a separate 20px in-content `<h1 class="page-title">` (knowledge_hub.html:9, kg_timeline.html:9, projects.html:10). Consequence, visible in shots/desktop-dark/health.png and tasks.png: the largest text anywhere on the page is 14px ("System Health" / "Tasks" in the topbar) — there is literally no type above body size on those screens, which is the single biggest driver of the "flat" read. Compounding inconsistency: settings.png shows the string "Settings" twice on one screen, at 14px in the topbar and 20px as `.settings-header h1` (app.css:488).
  - *Fix:* Pick one home for the page title and give it the canon tier. Either promote `.session-title-static` to the page-title tier (20px/600/leading-tight) and delete the duplicate in-content h1s, or keep the title in-content at 20px and reduce the topbar to a breadcrumb. Apply the same treatment on all 13 pageTopbar pages so health/tasks/workflows stop having zero type above 14px.

- **HIGH** · *typography* — **Section headings render at body size (14px) or below it (13px, muted), so headings never outrank the content they label**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:1610`
  - *Evidence:* Canon heading-md is 16px/600 (DESIGN.md:389). Actual section headings: `.task-status-group h3` (app.css:1610-1612) = --text-base 14px — exactly 1px above the 13px `.data-table` cells it heads; same for `.agent-overview h3` (app.css:2068) and `.task-chat-column h3, .task-artifact-column h3` (app.css:1848). Rendered in shots/desktop-dark/tasks.png: "Agent Pool", "Review", "Accepted" are visually indistinguishable in weight-class from the table content beneath them. Meanwhile canon's 16px `.section-title` (design-system.css:1541) is used in only 2 templates (signal_pairing.html, whatsapp_pairing.html) and app's 16px `.section-header` only in scheduling.html.
  - *Fix:* Standardise on one section-heading class at the canon heading-md tier (16px/600/leading-tight/--fg) and repoint .task-status-group h3, .agent-overview h3, .task-chat-column h3, .task-artifact-column h3, .channel-sub-card h3 and .workflow-step-detail-content h4 at it. Remove --fg-overlay from any heading.

- med · *component-adoption* — **Card anatomy is only half-adopted: .card-body, .card-footer, .card-sunken, .card-active and .card-header-gradient are unused everywhere; the shared infoCard substitutes bespoke .card-rows/.card-badge**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/components.html:31`
  - *Evidence:* DESIGN.md:572-579 documents the card sub-element vocabulary (`.card-header`, `.card-header-gradient`, `.card-body`, `.card-footer`) and DESIGN.md:560-570 the variants `.card-sunken`, `.card-active`, `.card-glass`. Usage: `.card-header` 19x, but `.card-body` = 0, `.card-footer` = 0, `.card-header-gradient` = 0, `.card-sunken` = 0, `.card-active` = 0, `.card-hover` = 0 (verified across templates/, *.dart, controllers/, app.css). The shared `infoCardTemplate` (templates/components.html:31-42) — the card used for every Services tile on the health dashboard — emits `.card > .card-header > .card-title + .card-badge`, then `.card-rows > .card-row > .card-row-label/.card-row-value` (app.css:576, 581-585) in place of `.card-body`, and `.card-badge` in place of `.status-badge`.   - *Fix:* Wrap infoCard rows in `.card-body` and move the badge/meta line to `.card-footer`; swap `.card-badge` for `.status-badge` + `.status-badge-{variant}`. Adopt `.card-active` for the selected task/session/step card and `.card-sunken` for embedded code/config regions.

- med · *component-adoption* — **Iconography stops at the sidebar: 11 of 26 templates render zero icons, so nav promises a vocabulary the page bodies never reuse**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/tasks.html:12`
  - *Evidence:* Counting `data-icon="…"` and `.icon.icon-*` per template: sidebar.html 3, topbar.html 5, settings.html 8, scheduling.html 7, workflow_detail.html 5 — versus **zero** in health_dashboard.html, tasks.html, projects.html, knowledge_hub.html, kg_timeline.html, session_info.html, task_detail.html, task_timeline.html, workflow_list.html, workflow_step_detail.html, and 1 in memory_dashboard.html. The `.dart` builders add only two more (project_form.dart, task_form.dart). DESIGN.md's vocabulary table (:836-886) defines `clipboard-list`, `folder-kanban`, `workflow`, `database`, `clock`, `gauge`, `file-text`, `git-branch`, `wrench` — all rendered in the sidebar nav and then never again on the page they lead to. Only scheduling.html has table row-action icons (pencil/x, desktop-dark/scheduling.png); Tasks, Projects and Workflows tables have none. scheduling.html:32/118 also marks its add actions with a literal `"+ "` text character instead of `data-icon="plus"`.
  - *Fix:* Apply the existing vocabulary at the three cheapest, highest-visibility places: `.section-label`/`.section-header` leading icons (Services/Metrics/Guard Activity, Scheduled Jobs/Tasks, Token Usage/Session Details), table row-action buttons on Tasks and Projects, and `data-icon="plus"` on every add/create button (replacing the `"+ "` literals at scheduling.html:32/118 and components.html:17).

- med · *state-coverage* — **SSE disconnection is invisible — both EventSource onerror handlers are no-ops while sidebar live dots keep pulsing**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/controllers/dc_shell_controller.js:438`
  - *Evidence:* dc_shell_controller.js:438-442: the global `/api/events` `onerror` only acts `if (document.getElementById('restart-overlay'))` — with no overlay present it does nothing. dc_tasks_controller.js:111-112: `taskEventSource.onerror = function() {` with the comment `// Intentionally empty: EventSource auto-reconnects`. Meanwhile sidebar.html:61-62 renders `.status-dot--live` (an animated pulse) per running task and dc_tasks_controller.js:295-297 injects `.scan-bar` sweeps. A dropped stream therefore leaves an app that looks live and current but is permanently stale, with no reconnecting indicator anywhere (`grep -n 'offline|Disconnected|reconnect' templates/ static/` returns only the two pairing-page 'Waiting for connection' strings).
  - *Fix:* On EventSource error, set a shell-level `data-connection="lost"` attribute and render the canon `.banner.banner-warning` ('Live updates disconnected — reconnecting…'), and gate `.status-dot--live` / `.scan-bar` animations behind that attribute so stale indicators stop pulsing. Clear it on the next successful `open`.

- med · *state-coverage* — **The mascot is shipped and specified for empty states but used on exactly one page**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/components.html:12`
  - *Evidence:* DESIGN.md:339 states the mascot 'appears at brand moments: masthead, empty states, onboarding'; DESIGN.md:332 lists `logo-avatar-512-8bit.png` for 'Favicon/avatar, masthead, empty states, about screens'; showcase.html:904-909 shows the canonical empty state as a 64px `<img class="pixel-art">` mascot + strong title + explanation + `.btn-primary`. The asset is served at static/mascot-avatar-512-8bit.png. `grep -rn 'logo-avatar|pixel-art|mascot' templates/` returns three hits total: layout.html:5-8 (favicons) and login.html:10 (`.login-mascot pixel-art`, 96px). Zero of the app's 18 empty states use it; DESIGN.md:391 likewise assigns the `display` tier (24px/600, gradient text) to 'Hero/empty-state moments' and no empty state uses it either.
  - *Fix:* Adopt the showcase.html:904 pattern for the app's highest-traffic empty states — the no-chats state (components.html:12), no tasks (tasks.html:105), no projects (projects.html:52), no workflow runs (workflow_list.html:62) — with the 64px mascot and a `display`-tier title.

- med · *state-coverage* — **Six mutually inconsistent renderings of an absent value across the app**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/workflow_detail.dart:204`
  - *Evidence:* The same semantic — 'no value' — renders six different ways: em-dash `—` (session_info.dart:62,68,73,77,78,79,95; audit_table.dart:120,124,128; scheduling.dart:107,111), double-hyphen `--` (workflow_detail.dart:204,261,269), `N/A` (memory_dashboard.dart:123,158,161), lowercase `unknown` (health_page.dart:50,54; settings_page.dart:76,77; page_support.dart:22,25; task_timeline.dart:92,94; health_dashboard.dart:186), `Not found` (settings_page.dart:142), a literal `0` (workflows_page.dart:110,125), and blank (settings.html Model/Max Turns fields — visible empty in desktop-dark/settings.png). settings.html:52 additionally puts a bare `<option value="">—</option>` in a form select, where an em-dash is not a readable choice label.
  - *Fix:* Define one absent-value convention in DESIGN.md (the canon already sanctions `—` at DESIGN.md:893 as text punctuation) and one shared Dart helper, then route all of the above through it. Replace `--`, `N/A`, `unknown` and `0`; for the select at settings.html:52 use a named option ('Default') instead of a dash.

- med · *state-coverage* — **No loading treatment on any navigation, and no list is bounded — content pops in and lists grow without limit**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/web/pages/workflows_page.dart:96`
  - *Evidence:* Loading: 28 `hx-get` sites across templates, `grep -c hx-indicator templates/*.html` = 0, and exactly one skeleton-backed fetch in the whole app (workflow_detail.html:126-135, which is also the only place with skeleton + `banner-error` + Retry — the correct exemplar). The canon ships `.skeleton` (design-system.css:1364), `.scan-bar` (:1164) and `.claw-loader` (:1734); app.css references skeleton/scan-bar in only two rules (:686, :2333). Bounding: `grep -rn 'limit|\.take\(|sublist|pageSize' web/pages/` returns zero matches across all nine page handlers — workflows_page.dart:96 iterates every run, tasks_page.dart:68 lists every task, and scheduling/projects/kg-timeline render every row. Only three surfaces paginate at all: knowledge_hub (knowledge_hub.html:62), the audit table (audit_table.dart:141), and chat messages (dc_chat_controller.js:307).
  - *Fix:* Add `hx-indicator` targets driving a `.scan-bar` under the topbar for SPA navigations, and `.skeleton` placeholders for the polling fragments (health, memory, task timeline). Cap the unbounded page handlers at a page size with a 'Load more' control, reusing the existing pager markup from knowledge_hub.html:62.

- med · *state-coverage* — **Empty states use five different container classes and four different "icons" — including one colour emoji — and none carries the page's primary action**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/task_detail.html:139`
  - *Evidence:* A shared `emptyState` fragment exists (components.html:6) using the `❯_` prompt glyph, but is consumed by exactly one caller (chat.dart:95). Everything else is hand-rolled: tasks.html:106 and projects.html:53 use `.claw-mark`; task_detail.html:139 uses `<div class="empty-state-icon">&#128172;</div>` — a 💬 colour emoji rendered by the OS emoji font at `font-size: 2.5rem` (app.css:1808), the only non-monospace, non-palette element anywhere in the UI (visible in desktop-dark/task-detail.png); task_detail.html:190, workflow_list.html:62 and workflow_step_detail.html:12 have no icon at all; and four further classes exist for the same job — `.knowledge-empty-state` (knowledge_hub.html:57), `.tl-empty-state` (task_timeline.html:45), `.table-empty-cell` (scheduling.html:109/206), `.provider-empty-state` (settings.html:424) — plus audit_table.dart:38 which inlines `style="padding:var(--sp-6);text-align:center;"`. Only `emptyAppState` (components.html:17) includes a call to action; the Projects and Tasks empty states describe the gap but do not offer the "Add Project" / "New Task" button that exists 40px above them.
  - *Fix:* Extend the `emptyState` fragment (components.html:6) to `claw-mark + title + text + optional action slot`, delete the emoji at task_detail.html:139 and the four duplicate classes, and pass the surface's primary action into every list/collection empty state (Projects → Add Project, Tasks → New Task, Workflows → the definition browser).

- med · *typography* — **15 of 21 heading-size rules set no line-height, so every heading renders at body leading (1.6) instead of the canon-mandated 1.3**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:317`
  - *Evidence:* DESIGN.md:380 requires "1.3 for headings and tight UI" and "≤1.2 at display sizes". design-system.css:40-46 sets `html { line-height: var(--leading) }` = 1.6, and nothing resets it for headings. app.css rules at ≥16px with no line-height: .error-title:317 (20px), .login-wordmark:352 (24px — a display-tier rule, canon requires ≤1.2), .info-title:431 (20px), .settings-header h1:488 (20px), .guard-detail-header h2:509 (16px), .section-header:634 (16px), .pairing-section-title:657 (20px), .pairing-connected-header:661, .pairing-code-display code, .pairing-expired-title, .channel-detail-hero-title h1:1296 (20px), .channel-panel-title, .task-dialog-header h2:1964, .session-item .session-delete, .pairing-step-icon. Canon's own `.section-title` (design-system.css:1541-1546, the canonical 16px section heading) also omits it, as does `.sidebar-header .logo` and `.messages:empty::after`. Rendered: shots/desktop-dark/settings.png — the 20px "Settings" h1 sits in a 32px line box (1.6) instead of 26px, which is why the header block reads soft and unanchored against the 13px subtitle beneath it.
  - *Fix:* Add `line-height: var(--leading-tight)` to every rule at --text-lg and above, and `line-height: 1.15` at --text-2xl/--text-3xl. Better: fix once at canon level by shipping the composite tier classes (see the tier-class finding) so leading travels with size.

- med · *typography* — **Six rules use off-scale sizes below the 12px floor (10px and 11px), on real content text rather than icons**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:1415`
  - *Evidence:* The scale's floor is --text-xs 12px (tokens.css:71, DESIGN.md:385). Rules below it in app.css: `.channel-mode-badge` 0.625rem/10px (1415), `.provider-badge` 0.625rem/10px (1782), `.workflow-artifact-badge` 10px (2631), `.task-event` 11px (2259) — this is task-timeline event *text*, not a glyph — and `.task-event-icon` 10px (2264). Additionally `.workflow-step-icon` hardcodes 13px (2516), duplicating --text-sm as a literal. Rendered: shots/desktop-dark/tasks.png and chat-session.png show the CLAUDE provider badge at 10px uppercase with 0.08em tracking; at that size in a mono face the counters close up and the badge reads as a texture rather than a word. These 6 sites are the entire remainder of the '5 hardcode px' figure plus the rem-expressed equivalents, so they are the complete off-scale set.
  - *Fix:* Raise all six to --text-xs (12px) and compensate density with padding, not size: reduce badge padding from 2px 6px to 1px 5px. If 12px genuinely cannot fit the provider badge, add a single `--text-2xs: 0.6875rem` (11px) token to canon and use it everywhere rather than three ad-hoc values.

- low · *brand* — **Brand presence is a favicon and a login page nobody sees — ~20 of 23 surfaces contain zero DartClaw-specific elements**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/layout.html:5`
  - *Evidence:* Repo-wide grep for the brand vocabulary: the mascot appears only at login.html:10 (a page that never renders in a no-auth deployment) and layout.html:5-8 (favicon); `.claw-mark` only at tasks.html:106 and projects.html:53 — empty states that render only with zero data; `.claw-loader` only at chat.html:112 and task_detail.html:224; `.terminal-frame--crt` only at login.html:2. Across all 23 seeded desktop screenshots the only DartClaw-specific mark visible on any surface is the `❯ DartClaw` sidebar wordmark. DESIGN.md:347 budgets *one* claw moment per view as a scarcity rule; the app is spending zero on roughly twenty of twenty-three views, which turns 'ration the signature' into 'omit the signature' — this is precisely why the surfaces read as a generic admin panel.
  - *Fix:* Spend the one-per-view budget the canon already grants: `.claw-mark` in the `pageHeader` action-adjacent slot or the sidebar footer; `.claw-loader` as the htmx `.htmx-indicator` for in-flight fragment swaps (today there is none); the mascot at ≥96px on the 404 and on the Knowledge/Tasks/Projects zero-state heroes.

- low · *dialog-feedback* — **Mutating actions succeed silently because their handlers full-page-reload, destroying any toast — failure is announced, success is not**
  - *Evidence:* Asymmetric feedback across sessions and projects: dc_shell_controller.js:373 `.then(… window.location.href = '/')` after a session delete, and dc_projects_controller.js:132 / :154 / :196 `window.location.reload()` after project create / update / remove. Each of those methods reports *failures* via `showToast('error', …)` (dc_projects_controller.js:136,158,199,201) but reports success only by the page changing under the user. A toast issued before a reload cannot survive it, and no flash/handoff mechanism exists — grep of static/controllers/ shows `sessionStorage` used zero times and `localStorage` only for the theme (dc_shell_controller.js:169,183) and the memory view mode (dc_memory_controller.js:32,91). The contrast is in-repo: dc_scheduling_controller.js refreshes in place and therefore *can* toast, and does — `showToast('success', 'Scheduled task deleted - restart required')` at :410. DESIGN.md:673 explicitly asks for this row (`| Transient success | Toast (.toast-success) | Session renamed, copied to clipboard |`).
  - *Fix:* Add a two-line flash handoff to shared.js — `queueToast(type, msg)` writes to `sessionStorage`, and dc_shell_controller `connect()` drains and shows it — then call it before each `location.reload()` / `location.href` assignment. Better still for the projects page: replace the reload with the htmx in-place refresh the scheduling page already uses, and toast directly.

- low · *typography* — **Five uppercase micro-labels omit tracking-caps, including one of the two table-header treatments**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:3126`
  - *Evidence:* DESIGN.md:381 mandates `+0.08em` on uppercase micro-labels ("section labels, role labels, table headers"). design-system.css is compliant: 9 of 9 uppercase rules carry letter-spacing. app.css is 21 of 26 — the five that omit it are `.layer-badge`, `.knowledge-summary-label` (app.css:3126-3130), `.unverified-flag`, `.kg-timeline-controls .field-label`, and `.guard-editor-table th`. The last is the sharpest: the app now has two table-header treatments, `.data-table th` (tracked, per canon) and `.guard-editor-table th` (untracked), rendered on the same Settings page. In shots/desktop-dark/knowledge-hub.png the untracked "WIKI / KG / MEMORY / INBOX" labels sit directly beside the tracked "READ-ONLY" marker, and the tighter set reads as a different label class.
  - *Fix:* Add `letter-spacing: var(--tracking-caps)` to all five rules. Longer term this is a symptom of the missing tier class — a `.t-caption-caps` class in canon would make the omission impossible.

### `knowledge-hub` (8)

- med · *accessibility* — **Filter chips and tabs are bespoke reimplementations; the active filter is signalled by colour alone with no ARIA state**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/knowledge_hub.html:28`
  - *Evidence:* knowledge_hub.html:28-31 emits `<a class="filter-chip filter-chip--active">` inside `role="list"` with no `aria-current` or `aria-pressed` (className comes from knowledge_hub.dart:63). DESIGN.md:737 defines the canonical toggle filter as `button.chip` with `aria-pressed="true"` and an accent tint of 14% fill / 40% border; `.chip` + `.chip-row` exist in the served design-system.css (1930-1992) and are unused here, while `.filter-chip` (app.css:3086-3102) re-derives them at a 10% mix. Same pattern for the tab row: `.knowledge-tabs` (app.css:2952-2957) is a bespoke `gap: var(--sp-2)` flex row instead of canon `.tab-bar` (app.css:1063, `gap: 0; border-bottom: var(--border)`), so in every capture the active green 2px underline under "Hub" floats with no rail beneath the inactive tab, and the nav carries no `role="tablist"`.
  - *Fix:* Use `button.chip` with `aria-pressed` (or keep anchors and add `aria-current="true"`) plus `.chip-row` for the filter row, and swap `.knowledge-tabs` for `.tab-bar`; delete `.filter-chip*` and `.knowledge-tabs` from app.css.

- med · *brand* — **Layer badges paint four categories with semantic state colors and the scarce brand accent**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:3027`
  - *Evidence:* app.css:3027-3049 — `.layer-badge--wiki` = `--info`, `.layer-badge--memory` = `--accent`, `.layer-badge--inbox` = `--warning`; only `--kg` uses an extended-palette hue (`--teal`). DESIGN.md:356 reserves semantic colors for state and DESIGN.md:352 reserves the accent for "primary actions, the streaming cursor, active selection, and the success state", while DESIGN.md:359 defines `--chart-1`…`--chart-6` as the ordered categorical ramp — which is exactly what four knowledge layers are, and it is unused app-wide. Screenshot consequence: an amber INBOX badge reads as a degraded/warning condition, and on the Memory filter view (knowledge-memory.png) the active green filter chip and a green MEMORY badge are the same accent, so "selected" and "category" become indistinguishable. Same misuse in `.read-only-marker` (app.css:3051-3062, accent for a mode label) and `.citation-marker` (app.css:2978-2994, `--info` for a non-state affordance).
  - *Fix:* Reassign the four `.layer-badge--*` variants to `--chart-1`…`--chart-4` by fixed layer index, and move `.read-only-marker` to a neutral `.status-badge` (components.css:1244) so accent stays scarce.

- med · *color-depth* — **Knowledge containers hand-roll .card minus every depth cue — card and page are the same value in light theme**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:3111`
  - *Evidence:* `.knowledge-summary-item` (app.css:3111-3118) and `.knowledge-result-row` (app.css:3141-3150) both declare `background: var(--bg-mantle); border: 1px solid var(--bg-surface1); border-radius: var(--radius-lg); padding: var(--sp-4)` — a near-copy of canonical `.card` (design-system.css:784-800) with `box-shadow: var(--shadow-sm)`, `border-top-color: var(--border-highlight)`, the hover glow/1px lift and the transition all dropped, and with a heavier border token than canon's `--border` (`1px solid var(--bg-surface0)`). Sampled from the screenshots: light theme card fill #e6e9ef on page #eaecf1 (~1.02:1 luminance ratio — the fill is invisible, only the #bcc0cc hairline separates them); dark theme #181825 on #1e1e2d (~1.10:1). DESIGN.md:473-491 states depth comes from the ladder *plus* hue-aware shadows and the luminous top edge; this page has neither, which is the literal "flat and gray" complaint.
  - *Fix:* Replace both bespoke rules with the canon classes on the elements: `class="card card-metric card-metric--{color}"` on the summary items and `class="card card-tint-{color}"` on `.knowledge-result-row` (components.css:1024 names "lists, search results" as the card-tint use case), deleting the duplicated background/border/padding declarations from app.css.

- med · *component-adoption* — **Result row duplicates its own layer badge and excerpt, while the result title is not a link**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/knowledge_hub.html:47`
  - *Evidence:* knowledge_hub.html:47 renders `.layer-badge` at the row head, and the attribution fragment injected at :53 renders the same `.layer-badge` again in the meta row (source_attribution.html:9) — visible in every capture as `WIKI … wiki/README.md [1] WIKI`, with the two badges in visibly different treatments. The popover behind `[1]` is also redundant on this surface: knowledge_hub_page.dart:66 passes `excerpt: item.snippet`, i.e. the popover reveals the same snippet, the same layer badge and the same source link already shown in the row. Meanwhile the result title is an inert `<h2 class="knowledge-result-title">` (knowledge_hub.html:49); the only way into the document is the small `wiki/README.md` link at :52, so the largest, most obviously clickable text on the card does nothing.
  - *Fix:* Wrap the title in an `<a href="${item.sourceHref}">` (and give `.knowledge-result-row` a hover state via `.card`), suppress the attribution fragment's inline layer badge when the parent already shows one, and either drop the `[1]` marker on the hub (it is a chat-footnote affordance with no footnote list here) or feed it a distinct, longer excerpt.

- med · *state-coverage* — **Empty state is a bare dashed box with one line — canon .empty-state with the claw mark is used by every other list page**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/knowledge_hub.html:57`
  - *Evidence:* knowledge_hub.html:57-59 renders `<div class="knowledge-empty-state"><p>…</p></div>`, styled at app.css:3176-3181 as `border: 1px dashed var(--bg-surface2); padding: var(--sp-4)` — left-aligned, no icon, no title/body split. Canon `.empty-state` (components.css:1504-1526) is centered, `--sp-12` padding, with a glowing accent `.icon` and a 400px-capped `--text-sm` paragraph; tasks.html:105-108 and projects.html:52-55 already use it with `.empty-state-icon > .claw-mark` plus `.empty-state-title` / `.empty-state-text`. Captures knowledge-memory.png / -kg.png / -inbox.png show the result: a wide grey dashed rectangle holding "Nothing remembered." / "No facts extracted." / "Inbox is clear.", with ~350px of dead page below it.
  - *Fix:* Replace with the app-standard block used by tasks.html: `.empty-state` > `.empty-state-icon` (claw-mark) + `.empty-state-title` + `.empty-state-text`, splitting the single `_emptyMessage` string (knowledge_hub.dart:97-106) into title and hint copy. Delete `.knowledge-empty-state`.

- med · *typography* — **The four layer counters are the page's only KPIs and render at 16px — .card-metric / 32px metric-value tier unused**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:3120`
  - *Evidence:* `.knowledge-summary-count` is `font-size: var(--text-lg)` (16px) `font-weight: var(--weight-medium)` with no color and no tracking (app.css:3120-3124). Canon `.card-metric .metric-value` is `--text-3xl` (32px) / `--weight-bold` / `letter-spacing: var(--tracking-tight)` with `--accent|--info|--error|--warning` variants and a radial hover glow (components.css:976-1011); DESIGN.md:392 assigns metric-value explicitly to "Dashboard KPI numbers". The screenshots show the "1 / 0 / 0 / 0" row at the same 16px as the result title next to it, so the numbers read as body text in a box. memory_dashboard.html:16,35,45 already uses `class="card card-metric card-metric--accent"`, proving the component is live and this page was simply skipped.
  - *Fix:* Emit `<div class="card card-metric card-metric--{accent|info|memory-color|warning}"><div class="metric-value">…</div><div class="metric-label">…</div></div>` in knowledge_hub.html:35-38 and delete `.knowledge-summary-item/-count/-label` from app.css.

- low · *layout-spacing* — **Every page section is double-spaced: .page-inner flex gap plus per-section margin-bottom stack to ~40px**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:3067`
  - *Evidence:* `.page-inner` is `display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: var(--sp-6)` (app.css:600), and then four of its five children add their own bottom margin: `.pagehead` `margin-bottom: var(--sp-4)` (app.css:606), `.knowledge-tabs` `--sp-4` (app.css:2955), `.knowledge-search-strip` `--sp-4` (app.css:3067), `.knowledge-summary-strip` `--sp-4` (app.css:3108). 24px + 16px = 40px between every block. Measured on desktop-dark/knowledge-hub.png: tab underline y≈200 → search field top y≈243 (43px); chip row bottom y≈322 → KPI top y≈365 (43px); KPI bottom y≈432 → result card top y≈474 (42px). DESIGN.md:397 puts major section separation at sp-8 (32px), so the page runs ~25% looser than canon and uniformly so — no section reads as more or less related than any other.
  - *Fix:* Drop the `margin-bottom` from `.knowledge-tabs`, `.knowledge-search-strip` and `.knowledge-summary-strip` and let the `.page-inner` gap own the rhythm; tighten the search-strip→chip-row relationship with a smaller internal gap so the filter row visibly belongs to the search field.

- low · *typography* — **Uppercase micro-labels on this page miss tracking-caps, and the KPI number is the same size as the result title**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:3024`
  - *Evidence:* Three rules in the knowledge block set `text-transform: uppercase` with no `letter-spacing`: `.layer-badge` (app.css:3024), `.knowledge-summary-label` (app.css:3129) and `.unverified-flag` (app.css:3223). DESIGN.md:381 mandates `+0.08em` (`tracking-caps`) on uppercase micro-labels, and app.css already uses `--tracking-caps` 27 times elsewhere against 26 uppercase declarations total — i.e. every uppercase label in the app carries it except these three. Separately, `.knowledge-summary-count` and `.knowledge-result-title` are both `var(--text-lg)` (app.css:3120, 3160), so the page's whole type hierarchy is 20 / 16 / 16 / 14 / 12 with no step between a KPI number and a list-item heading — visible in the captures where "1" and "README" have identical optical weight.
  - *Fix:* Add `letter-spacing: var(--tracking-caps)` to the three uppercase rules (or delete them in favour of `.metric-label` / `.status-badge`), and once the counters move to `.card-metric` the 32px/16px separation restores the hierarchy.

### `channel-detail` (6)

- med · *color-depth* — **Channel status badge drops the canon status dot and paints three different states the same amber**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/web/channel_status.dart:4`
  - *Evidence:* channel_status.dart:4-6 maps `notRunning` ('Not running'), `configured` ('Configured') and `pairingNeeded` ('Pairing needed') all to `status-badge-warning`. channel_detail.html:17 renders a bare `<span class="status-badge">` with text only. The canon pairs badge with dot — showcase.html:491-493 `<span class="status-badge status-badge-warning"><span class="status-dot status-dot--warning"></span>Pending</span>` — and DESIGN.md:582 describes status badges as "subtle semantic chip with embedded dot". Screenshots confirm: the healthy "Configured" badge on channel-gchat.png is pixel-identical in treatment to the action-required "Pairing needed" badge on channel-signal.png and the fault "Not running" badge on channel-whatsapp.png.
  - *Fix:* Emit the matching `.status-dot--{variant}` inside the badge (variant is already exposed as `ChannelStatus.badgeVariant`), and re-map `configured` to the neutral/`info` badge so a configured channel does not read as a warning.

- med · *color-depth* — **Nested wells land on exactly the page background colour, so panels have no depth in either theme**
  - `dev/design-system/tokens.css:17`
  - *Evidence:* `.card` = `--bg-mantle` (design-system.css:785), `.well`/`.well-content` = `--bg-base` (design-system.css:682/699), and `.channel-mode-card` = `--bg-base` (app.css:1383). tokens.css:17-18 sets dark `--bg-mantle:#181825` / `--bg-base:#1e1e2e`; tokens.css:167-168 sets light `--bg-mantle:#e6e9ef` / `--bg-base:#eff1f5`. In both themes `--bg-base` is the *lighter* value AND the page background, so every "recessed" container inside a card renders at exactly the page colour — the channel page stacks 7 mode cards and 6 sub-cards this way. In desktop-light/channel-gchat.png the panel (#e6e9ef) against the page (#eff1f5) is a ~1.6% lightness step and the inner cards return to page colour; the whole surface reads as one flat grey plane with hairline borders. DESIGN.md:533 calls wells "recessed", which the token assignment contradicts.
  - *Fix:* Either re-point the well family at `--bg-crust`/`--bg-sub-base` when nested inside a card, or add a documented `.card > .well` recession rule (inset shadow + one step down the ladder) to components.css so "recessed" is true rather than nominal.

- med · *component-adoption* — **Both configuration panels wear severity treatments (`panel-accent` / `panel-info`) that encode nothing**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/channel_detail.html:42`
  - *Evidence:* channel_detail.html:42 uses `class="card panel-accent …"` for DM Access and :131 `class="card panel-info …"` for Group Access. DESIGN.md:567 reserves `.card.panel-{color}` (3px left border + gradient bleed) for "severity-bearing content (guards, status, alerts)", and DESIGN.md:925 places panel-error/panel-warning under "Alerts". The result in desktop-dark/channel-whatsapp.png is a green 3px stripe on DM Access and a blue one on Group Access — two peer configuration sections, neither carrying severity, tinted with the accent (which elsewhere means success/active) and info hues. DESIGN.md:358 explicitly warns the decorative hues "never carry state", yet these read as state.
  - *Fix:* Use plain `.card` for both panels and let the `.section-label` kicker carry the distinction; reserve `panel-*` for the restart/guard banners on this page.

- med · *typography* — **Three competing uppercase micro-label tiers in one panel; one is 10px, below the type scale floor**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:1356`
  - *Evidence:* Inside a single panel the app invents three uppercase micro-labels: `.channel-panel-kicker` = `--text-xs` (12px) / `--fg-overlay` (app.css:1356-1361), `.channel-sub-card h3` = `--text-sm` (13px) / `--fg-sub0` (app.css:1433-1439), and `.channel-mode-badge` = hardcoded `0.625rem` = 10px (app.css:1415). tokens.css:73 floors the scale at 12px (`--text-xs`, "timestamps, metadata"). The canon already ships one label recipe, `.section-label` (design-system.css:1534-1541: 12px / `--weight-bold` / `--fg-sub0` / `--tracking-caps`), which this app uses in health_dashboard.html:29 and memory_dashboard.html:13 — channel_detail.html adopts none of it. Rendered result in desktop-dark/channel-whatsapp.png: "DM ACCESS" and "KNOWN DM ALLOWLIST" are two visibly different label styles stacked 300px apart in the same card.
  - *Fix:* Replace `.channel-panel-kicker` and `.channel-sub-card h3` styling with the canon `.section-label` class, and raise `.channel-mode-badge` to `var(--text-xs)`.

- low · *layout-spacing* — **Sub-card headings collide with their body — no vertical rhythm inside `.channel-sub-card`**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:1433`
  - *Evidence:* app.css:1433-1439 styles `.channel-sub-card h3` with `margin: 0` and defines no container `display`/`gap` for `.channel-sub-card` itself, so the h3 and the following block sit in default block flow with zero separation. Crop of desktop-dark/channel-whatsapp.png shows "ALLOWED GROUPS" sitting directly on top of "Allowlist (0 entries)" (~4px baseline gap) and "MENTION GATING" flush against the "Require @mention in groups" checkbox row — each pair reads as one wrapped two-line label rather than a heading plus content.
  - *Fix:* Add `.channel-sub-card { display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap: var(--sp-3) }` (matching `.channel-panel-header`'s existing flex/gap treatment at app.css:1351-1355).

- low · *layout-spacing* — **Hero identicon renders at sidebar-list scale, giving the page no identity moment**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:1296`
  - *Evidence:* channel_detail.html:12 emits a bare `<span class="identicon">`; design-system.css:1755-1763 sizes `.identicon` as `2.2em` at `font-size: var(--text-xs)` = 26px, and DESIGN.md:610 states identicons are "Sized via `font-size` on the element" — the hero sets none. desktop-dark/channel-whatsapp.png shows the hero "WH" tile at the same 26px as the "NC"/"CS" chat identicons in the sidebar (sidebar.html:98). Combined with the hero `h1` at `--text-xl` (app.css:1298) — the same 20px tier as the topbar text directly above it, which already reads "WhatsApp Channel" — the hero card carries no size hierarchy over either neighbour.
  - *Fix:* Set `.channel-detail-hero .identicon { font-size: var(--text-lg) }` (≈35px) or larger, and take the hero `h1` to `--text-2xl` (the canon `display` tier) so the page has one clear entry point.

### `memory-dashboard` (6)

- med · *accessibility* — **Essential KPI metadata is painted in --fg-overlay, failing AA in light theme (3.2:1)**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:1038`
  - *Evidence:* `.metric-sub-inline { color: var(--fg-overlay) }` (app.css:1038) and `.meter-label { … color: var(--fg-overlay) }` (app.css:1041) carry the two pieces of data that make the numbers meaningful: the cap ("/ 50") and the budget ("of 32 KB … %"). Light theme `--fg-overlay` is #7b8094 (tokens.css:179) on card `--bg-mantle` #e6e9ef → contrast 3.22:1, below the 4.5:1 AA threshold for 12-13px text (dark theme #7f849c on #181825 = 4.7:1, only just passing). DESIGN.md:354 is explicit: "`fg-overlay` is intentionally low-emphasis — never use it for essential metadata."
  - *Fix:* Switch `.metric-sub-inline` and `.meter-label` to `--fg-sub0` (light #62677d on mantle = 4.6:1, passes), keeping `--fg-overlay` for placeholders and disabled controls only.

- med · *color-depth* — **KPI colour is hardcoded category flavour, so the one real state (budget ≥80%) never reaches the number**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/memory_dashboard.html:16`
  - *Evidence:* memory_dashboard.html:35 hardcodes `card-metric--error` on the Errors card, so `--error` red paints the 32px value at 3/50 and would paint it at 0/50 — red here labels the file, not a state. memory_dashboard.html:16 hardcodes `card-metric--accent` on Memory Size; memory_dashboard.dart:54 computes `budgetWarn = budgetPercent >= 80` but memory_dashboard.dart:112 routes it only to `budgetWarnClass` → `meter-fill--warning`, i.e. a 6px bar. A workspace at 95% of budget still shows a green 32px number under a green "MEMORY SIZE" label; the only warning is a sliver of colour in a 6px track that is itself near-invisible (see meter-contrast finding). DESIGN.md:782: "Do use semantic colours only for state. State of *something*, not visual flavor."
  - *Fix:* Drive the card modifier from data: `card-metric--warning`/`--error` on Memory Size when budgetWarn/over-budget, neutral (`.card-metric` with no modifier) otherwise; make the Errors card neutral at 0 and `--error` only when errorsCount > 0. Pass the modifier through the context map in memory_dashboard.dart alongside budgetWarnClass.

- med · *component-adoption* — **"Prune Now" — the pruner card's only action — is .btn-ghost, i.e. plain grey text with no chrome at rest**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/memory_dashboard.html:111`
  - *Evidence:* shots/desktop-dark/memory.png and desktop-light/memory.png both show "Prune Now" below the Recent Runs table as unstyled body text; nothing marks it as a control. `.btn-ghost { background: transparent; border-color: transparent; color: var(--fg-sub0); box-shadow: none }` (design-system.css:392) — the resting state has zero affordance and only appears on hover (:393). It is applied at memory_dashboard.html:111 to an action that archives and de-duplicates the agent's memory. The same class is used for the timeline's "↺ now" reset (kg_timeline.html:30), which likewise renders as bare text beside a solid green `.btn-primary`. The controller then fights the token system with inline styles — `button.style.color = 'var(--warning)'` / `'var(--success)'` / `'var(--error)'` (dc_memory_controller.js:109,163,175) — so the confirm and result states are conveyed by text colour alone on a control that has no box.
  - *Fix:* Use `.btn` (default, visible border+surface) for "Prune Now", or `.btn-danger` given it is destructive; drive the confirm/success/failure states with a class swap (e.g. `.btn-danger` → `.btn-primary`) rather than `element.style.color`, so the states inherit real button chrome.

- med · *responsive* — **Five-card KPI row orphans at every breakpoint and two of the five cards have ~55px of dead space**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:1033`
  - *Evidence:* `.metrics-grid-5 { repeat(5,1fr) }` → `repeat(3,1fr)` ≤900px → `repeat(2,1fr)` ≤768px (app.css:1033-1035). shots/mobile-dark/memory.png shows the result: 2+2+1, with the LEARNINGS card alone on the last row and an equal-sized void beside it. On desktop the row is also internally ragged: cards 1, 4 and 5 carry a `.meter-wrapper`, but cards 2 and 3 come from `metricCardTemplate` (memory_dashboard.dart:97-107 → components.html:25-29) which emits only value + label, so in shots/desktop-dark/memory.png ACTIVE ENTRIES and ARCHIVED ENTRIES end at the label while the grid stretches their borders down level with the meter cards — roughly 55px of empty card. Canon's dashboard composition pattern is a 4-up KPI row (DESIGN.md:921).
  - *Fix:* Either drop to a 4-card KPI row (fold Archived into the Active card as a sub-value) or use `repeat(auto-fit, minmax(160px,1fr))` so the last row fills; and give every card in the row the same anatomy — add a meter or a sub-line to Active/Archived, or move the meters out of the cards for all five.

- med · *typography* — **Memory dashboard has no page title and exactly one heading level — a 12px grey micro-label used for both page sections and in-card subsections**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/memory_dashboard.html:13`
  - *Evidence:* There is no `<h1>` anywhere on the page (grep across memory_dashboard.html, layout.html, topbar.html, sidebar.html returns none) and no `.pagehead`/`.page-title`/`.page-subtitle` block — compare the sibling page kg_timeline.html:7-12 which has all three. The only page identity is the topbar `<span class="session-title-static">` (topbar.html:38) at `--text-base` = 14px. All five page sections use `<h2 class="section-label">` = `--text-xs` 12px, uppercase, `--fg-sub0` (design-system.css:1534-1541), and the *in-card* subsection "Recent Runs" (memory_dashboard.html:83) uses the identical class — visible in shots/desktop-dark/memory.png where "MEMORY PRUNING" and "RECENT RUNS" are typographically indistinguishable. Outside the five KPI values, no text on this page exceeds 13px. Canon has `page-title` 20px, `section-title` 16px and `display` 24px available (DESIGN.md:390-392, design-system.css:1543).
  - *Fix:* Add a `.pagehead` with `<h1 class="page-title">Memory</h1>` + subtitle at the top of memory_dashboard.html (matching kg_timeline.html:7-12), promote the five page sections to `<h2 class="section-title">` (16px, `--fg`), and keep `.section-label` for in-card subsections only.

- low · *typography* — **KPI numerals sit ~23px left of their own labels because the denominator is inside the centred value box**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/memory_dashboard.html:36`
  - *Evidence:* In shots/desktop-light/memory.png the ERRORS card spans x=950-1117 (centre 1033) and its label "ERRORS" is centred at 1033, but the "3" glyph centres at ~1010 — the 32px numeral reads 23px off-centre from the label under it. Same on LEARNINGS (label centre ~1216, "0" centre ~1192). Cause: memory_dashboard.html:36,46 put `<span class="metric-sub-inline">/ 50</span>` inside `.metric-value`, and `.card-metric { text-align:center }` (design-system.css:978) centres the combined value+denominator run, not the numeral. Compounding inconsistency in the same row: MEMORY SIZE dumps `formatBytes()` output whole into `.metric-value` (memory_dashboard.html:17), so its unit "B" renders at 32px accent while the sibling cards render their "/ 50" at 13px.
  - *Fix:* Make `.metric-value` a flex row with the numeral centred and the sub-part positioned as a baseline-aligned suffix that does not participate in centring (e.g. `position:relative` suffix or a grid with the numeral in the centre column); and split the byte unit into `.metric-sub-inline` so "0 B" and "3 / 50" use one treatment.

### `chat-session` (5)

- med · *component-adoption* — **The composer never shows which agent, model, or permission mode will answer — `.composer-model` and the guard `.status-badge` from the canon's composer anatomy are absent**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/chat.html:75`
  - *Evidence:* DESIGN.md:640-661 gives the composer anatomy explicitly: an attach button, a guard/permission `.status-badge` ("guards: standard"), a `.composer-model` text button ("claude · high") in `.composer-meta`, and the send button. The app's `.composer-toolbar` (chat.html:75-93) contains only a literal "/" ghost button, a hint row, and the send button. Grep of dc_chat_controller.js for `model|guard|provider` returns zero hits, and `.composer-model` appears in zero templates. The data is available — the sidebar renders a `provider-badge` reading "CLAUDE" for this same session (sidebar.html:101-103), and the topbar (topbar.html:1-20) carries only title / info / Reset / theme. So on the flagship surface the user cannot see which agent is answering or under what permission posture from anywhere on the page.
  - *Fix:* Adopt the canon anatomy: add `.composer-model` (provider · effort, opening the picker) into `.composer-meta`, and a guard-mode `.status-badge` on the left of `.composer-toolbar`, both driven by the session's existing provider/guard state.

- med · *component-adoption* — **Composer toolbar reads as one run-on sentence: the three quick-prompt buttons are styled identically to the static keyboard hints, and a bare "/" button duplicates the adjacent `/` keycap**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:78`
  - *Evidence:* In shots/desktop-dark/chat-session.png the toolbar row reads "/  [/] commands  [Ctrl/⌘] + [Enter] send  /workflow list  Summarize  What's changed?" as a single undifferentiated 12px line. `.composer-hint-action { border: none; background: transparent; font: inherit; color: var(--fg-sub0) }` (app.css:78-91) versus `.composer-hints { font-size: var(--text-xs); color: var(--fg-overlay) }` (app.css:67-76) — the interactive buttons (chat.html:81-86) differ from the static hint text by one step of foreground tone and nothing else: no border, no fill, no icon, no shape. The canon has the exact component for this: `.chip` / `.chip--ref`, "rectangular so they read engineered", with hover to `bg-surface1` and a focus ring (DESIGN.md:730-737). Separately, chat.html:76-77 renders `<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-icon">/</button>` — a raw text glyph in an icon button — immediately to the left of `<kbd>/</kbd> commands`, so the same slash appears twice in ~40px (visible at x≈305 and x≈337).
  - *Fix:* Render the three suggestions as `.chip--ref` in a `.chip-row`, and replace the literal "/" button with a proper `data-icon` command glyph (or drop it and let the `<kbd>/</kbd> commands` hint carry the affordance).

- med · *copy-content* — **Chat messages carry no timestamp — the conversation has zero temporal information, while the session-info page shows times for the same turns**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/chat.dart:15`
  - *Evidence:* The `userMessage` and `assistantMessage` fragments (chat.html:1-10) contain only `.msg-role` and `.msg-content`; `messagesHtmlFragment` passes only `content` (+ senderName / richInputHtml) into the render context (chat.dart:100-121), and `ClassifiedMessage` (chat.dart:15-23) has no time field at all. Confirmed in shots/desktop-dark/chat-session.png and desktop-light: neither message shows a time, a date, or any "n minutes ago". Meanwhile session-info's Recent Turns renders `.turn-time` at `--text-xs` mono for the exact same two turns (session_info.html:72, app.css:1244-1248) — visible as "10:00" in shots/desktop-dark/session-info.png. DESIGN.md:387 assigns the `caption` 12px tier to "Timestamps, metadata, role labels", so the tier exists and the recipe already exists in the app; chat simply never renders it.
  - *Fix:* Thread `createdAt` through `ClassifiedMessage` and both message fragments, rendering a `caption`-tier time next to `.msg-role` (reuse the `.turn-time` treatment), plus a day separator when the date changes between messages.

- med · *state-coverage* — **The waiting state drops a bare `.claw-loader` into `.msg-content`; the canonical `.msg-thinking` slot — DESIGN.md's "the claw moment of the chat view" — is never used**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/chat.html:112`
  - *Evidence:* chat.html:112 inserts `<span class="claw-loader" aria-label="Assistant is thinking">` directly inside `#streaming-content`, with no wrapper and no visible label. The canon defines `.msg-thinking { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:sp-2 }` plus `.msg-thinking-label` with a blinking animated ellipsis (design-system.css:494-507) and DESIGN.md:687 calls it "the sanctioned pre-stream composition state, and *the* claw moment of the chat view… this is where users stare longest, which is exactly why the brand lives here." Grep confirms `msg-thinking` appears in zero templates and zero controllers. Because the loader sits bare in `.msg-content`, the container also still carries `.streaming`, so the blinking `▊` block cursor (design-system.css:526-531) renders next to the loader — two competing motion cues where the canon specifies one composed object. Under reduced motion the app degrades to a silent, label-less, static blob (design-system.css:2254-2255 kills both animations, and there is no text cue).
  - *Fix:* Wrap the pre-stream slot in `<div class="msg-thinking"><span class="claw-loader">…</span><span class="msg-thinking-label">thinking</span></div>` per the canon, and withhold the `.streaming` class (and its cursor) until the first delta arrives.

- med · *state-coverage* — **The turn-status panel — the only "agent is stuck / waiting" affordance — prints three unlabeled raw timestamps in a 3-column grid**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/chat.html:44`
  - *Evidence:* `sessionTurnStatusView` passes `waiting_since`, `stuck_since` and `global_timeout_at` through as raw `toString()` values (session_info.dart:117-119). Both consumers render them as three bare spans with no labels — chat.html:44-48 and session_info.html:43-47 — laid out by `.turn-status-times { grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0,1fr)); font-size: var(--text-xs); color: var(--fg-overlay) }` with `text-overflow: ellipsis` (app.css:1866-1870). So during a stalled turn the user is shown three 12px overlay-toned ISO strings, ellipsized, with nothing saying which is which. DESIGN.md:801 requires "pair every attention treatment with a text cue"; the panel's amber border (app.css:1860) carries the state and the text carries nothing. The state label itself is a raw enum with underscores swapped for spaces (session_info.dart:115).
  - *Fix:* Give each time an inline `caption` label ("waiting since", "stuck since", "times out"), format as elapsed/remaining duration rather than an absolute timestamp, and hide slots that are empty instead of rendering blank grid cells.

### `workflow-detail` (5)

- **HIGH** · *typography* — **Workflow-detail KPI row uses app-local `.metric-card` (20px values) instead of canon `.card card-metric` (32px) — the metric-value tier is never reached on the page that most needs it**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/workflow_detail.html:13`
  - *Evidence:* workflow_detail.html:13-28 uses `class="metric-card"` → app.css:590-595: `.metric-card .metric-value { font-size: var(--text-xl) }` = 20px, label `--fg-overlay`. Canon is `.card-metric .metric-value { font-size: var(--text-3xl) }` = 32px with `--fg-sub0` label and a radial hover glow (design-system.css:977-1013), and DESIGN.md:392 assigns `metric-value | 32px / 600 | Dashboard KPI numbers`. memory_dashboard.html:16 and settings.html:348 already use the canon class, so the two near-identical names `.metric-card` vs `.card-metric` coexist in one codebase. Compounding it, the STATUS card puts a 12px `.status-badge` inside the `.metric-value` slot (workflow_detail.html:14), so in desktop-dark/workflow-detail.png the four-up row has no shared value baseline — a small pill next to "103d ago" / "48,750" / "2h 27m".
  - *Fix:* Replace `class="metric-card"` with `class="card card-metric"` (drop app.css:590-595 entirely so `.metric-card` cannot be picked again), and give the status tile a real 32px value (e.g. the status word) with the badge demoted to the label slot, or use `.card-metric--{color}` tinting instead of an embedded badge.

- med · *color-depth* — **Step-status colors contradict themselves and the canon state table: a running step gets a green border and a blue icon; completed and running are both green; queued is blue**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:2466`
  - *Evidence:* app.css:2466-2485 — `[data-step-status="completed"]` and `[data-step-status="running"]` both set `border-left-color: var(--success)`; `"queued"` and `"review"` both set `var(--info)`. app.css:2525-2528 then sets `.workflow-step-icon--running { color: var(--info) }` — so a single running step renders a blue icon inside a green-bordered card. DESIGN.md:756-761 specifies distinct nodes per state (`--done` success fill, `--running` accent ring + pulse-ring, `--failed` error, `--blocked` warning, `--pending` hollow with a dashed connector), and DESIGN.md § Status vocabulary rations green-pulse to "agent is working" and static green to "finished OK" — the app collapses both onto the same green and puts blue (info) on "not started yet".
  - *Fix:* Adopt `.pipeline-step--done/--running/--failed/--blocked/--pending` and `.pipeline-node` verbatim; map `queued`→`--pending` (hollow, dashed connector), `running`→`--running` (accent), `completed`→`--done` (success), `awaiting_approval`→`--blocked` (warning). Delete app.css:2466-2546.

- med · *component-adoption* — **Canonical .pipeline family (14 classes) is 100% unused; workflow detail re-implements the run spine as .workflow-pipeline/.workflow-step-card**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/workflow_detail.html:91`
  - *Evidence:* DESIGN.md:753 names `.pipeline` "the workflow-detail spine" and defines `.pipeline`, `.pipeline-step`, `.pipeline-step--done/--running/--failed/--blocked/--pending`, `.pipeline-node`, `.pipeline-step-body/-name/-meta` (components.css:2224-2280). Verified grep over all of packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src (templates + *.dart + controllers + app.css): every one of those 14 classes has ZERO references. templates/workflow_detail.html:91-136 instead emits `.workflow-pipeline > .workflow-step-wrapper > .workflow-step-connector + .workflow-step-card.card` with `.workflow-step-icon--{status}`, backed by app.css:2441-2560. The fork loses everything the canon spine provides: no `.pipeline-node` (canon gives success fill+glow, accent ring + pulse-ring on running, dashed connector for not-yet-reached); app.css:2454-2458 draws a flat 2px `var(--border)` connector of fixed `height: var(--sp-3)` and app.css:2460-2486 reduces step state to a 3px left border color. desktop-dark/workflow-detail.png shows the result as a flat empty column.
  - *Fix:* Replace `.workflow-pipeline`/`.workflow-step-wrapper`/`.workflow-step-connector`/`.workflow-step-card` with canonical `<ol class="pipeline">` > `li.pipeline-step.pipeline-step--{done|running|failed|blocked|pending}` > `.pipeline-node` + `.pipeline-step-body`(`.pipeline-step-name` + `.pipeline-step-meta`); delete the ~120 lines of app.css:2441-2560 that duplicate it.

- med · *component-adoption* — **Canonical .approval-card family (8 classes) unused — the HITL approval gate, the system's flagship governance object, renders as unstyled text divs**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/workflow_detail.html:113`
  - *Evidence:* DESIGN.md:724-728 defines `.approval-card--waiting/--approved/--rejected/--expired` plus `.approval-card-meta/-plan/-actions/-resolution` as "governance rendered as UX ... a first-class object rather than a line in the log", with a warning left edge, `.status-dot--attention`, a capped plan well and an Approve/Reject/Comment footer; showcase.html has a dedicated "Approval Gates" section. All 8 classes have ZERO references in packages/dartclaw_server. templates/workflow_detail.html:113-121 renders approval steps as `<div class="workflow-approval-detail">` > `.workflow-approval-message` / `.workflow-approval-feedback` with a bare label span — no card, no attention dot, no action footer.
  - *Fix:* Render the approval step body as `.card.approval-card.approval-card--waiting` (header with `.status-dot--attention` + "waiting" text cue, `.approval-card-plan` well for the request, `.approval-card-actions` footer), and the resolved states as `--approved`/`--rejected`/`--expired` with `.approval-card-resolution`.

- med · *typography* — **workflow_detail.html forks .card-metric as a local .metric-card, rendering workflow KPIs at --text-xl (20px) where every other dashboard uses the 32px metric-value tier**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/workflow_detail.html:13`
  - *Evidence:* Canon `.card-metric .metric-value` is `--text-3xl` / 32px with `--tracking-tight` (components.css:977-984), and templates/components.dart:53 already exposes a shared `metricCardTemplate()` emitting `.card.card-metric.card-metric--{color}` — used correctly by health_dashboard.dart:144-148, memory_dashboard.html and settings.html (desktop-dark/memory.png shows the 32px tier reading well). templates/workflow_detail.html:12-28 instead hand-writes four `<div class="metric-card">`, defined at app.css:590-595 with `.metric-card .metric-value { font-size: var(--text-xl) }` — 20px, no tracking, no card-metric hover glow, no semantic `--accent/--info/--error/--warning` variant. desktop-dark/workflow-detail.png shows the four visibly smaller, flatter tiles. Three parallel grid classes also coexist: `.metrics-grid`(586), `.metrics-grid-5`(1033), `.workflow-metrics-grid`(2387).
  - *Fix:* Replace the four `.metric-card` divs with `metricCardTemplate(color:…)` output (`.card.card-metric.card-metric--{accent|info|warning|error}`) inside `.metrics-grid`; delete `.metric-card`, `.metric-card .metric-value/.metric-label` (app.css:590-595) and `.workflow-metrics-grid` (app.css:2387).

### `scheduling` (4)

- med · *component-adoption* — **Heartbeat card abuses the 32px card-metric KPI tier for non-numeric text and duplicates its own badge**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/scheduling.dart:108-119`
  - *Evidence:* scheduling.dart:108-119 feeds `metricCardTemplate` three non-numeric strings: `'every 30 min'`, `'Active'|'Disabled'`, and a bare em-dash `'—'` when heartbeat is off. Those land in `.card-metric .metric-value` = --text-3xl/32px bold with tracking-tight (design-system.css:979-986). desktop-dark/scheduling.png shows the result: a 32px amber word "Disabled" dominating the page, and next to it a lone 32px blue dash labelled INTERVAL. DESIGN.md § Container decision flowchart is explicit: "Is a numeric KPI? → .card .card-metric--{color}". Worse, the same word already appears 60px above as the heartbeat status badge (scheduling.dart:103-106 / scheduling.html:12), so "Disabled" is rendered twice in one card at 12px and 32px. scheduling.dart:107 also passes a dead `intervalDisplay` context key that scheduling.html never references.
  - *Fix:* Replace the two heartbeat metric cards with a `.card-rows` key/value block (or a `.status-pill` + interval text) and reserve `.card-metric` for the numeric KPIs it is defined for. Delete the unused `intervalDisplay` key at scheduling.dart:107. If the interval is unknown, render nothing rather than a 32px em-dash.

- med · *interaction-glitch* — **The scheduling page ships two different destructive-confirmation experiences behind two pixel-identical buttons**
  - *Evidence:* shots/desktop-dark/scheduling.png: the "Scheduled Jobs" table and the "Scheduled Tasks" table each end in an ACTIONS column with a visually identical `btn-icon-sm` "×" control (jobs rows at y≈410/470, task row at y≈684). Clicking the job × builds an in-page confirmation: dc_scheduling_controller.js:180-214 injects a `<tr class="delete-confirm-row">` containing `.delete-confirm-bar` with `"Delete '" + jobName + "'?"`, a `btn btn-danger-fill btn-sm` "Confirm Delete" and a `btn btn-ghost btn-sm` "Cancel", styled at app.css:1149-1155, and hides the original row. Clicking the task × at dc_scheduling_controller.js:403 fires `window.confirm`. Same file, same page, same affordance, one styled in-page pattern and one OS dialog — and the in-page one is the only place in the entire app where a destructive action gets the red `btn-danger-fill` treatment.
  - *Fix:* Pick one and apply it consistently. The row-scoped inline bar is the better fit for table rows and is already built and styled; promote `.delete-confirm-bar` into components.css as canon (see finding 1's table rewrite) and convert `deleteScheduledTask` to use it — or route both through the confirm dialog. Do not leave both.

- med · *state-coverage* — **Scheduling renders a bare em-dash at metric-value tier as the INTERVAL KPI when heartbeat is disabled**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/scheduling.dart:107`
  - *Evidence:* scheduling.dart:107 `'intervalDisplay': heartbeatEnabled ? 'every $heartbeatIntervalMinutes min' : '—'` and :111 feed the same value into a `.card-metric` / `.metric-value`. desktop-dark/scheduling.png shows the result: a lone blue-accent em-dash rendered at the largest type size on the page, in the KPI slot, reading as if it were data. The paired card renders the word 'Disabled' at the same tier, so the two loudest elements on the page are both 'this feature is off'. There is no dimmed/disabled treatment for a metric card whose value is absent.
  - *Fix:* Add a `.card-metric--empty` state to the canon (dimmed value at --fg-overlay, no accent color) and use it here instead of substituting an em-dash into the value slot; or drop the interval card entirely when heartbeat is disabled and let the single 'Disabled' status card carry the state.

- med · *typography* — **The 32px metric-value tier is applied to prose status strings and an em-dash placeholder, making a disabled state the loudest type on the page**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/scheduling.dart:108`
  - *Evidence:* DESIGN.md:392 reserves metric-value (32px/600/tracking-tight) for "Dashboard KPI numbers". scheduling.dart:108-115 feeds `metricCardTemplate` the strings `'Disabled'` and `heartbeatEnabled ? 'every $heartbeatIntervalMinutes min' : '—'`.
