Rendered in shots/desktop-dark/scheduling.png: the word "Disabled" is 32px bold warning-orange and a bare em-dash is a 32px blue horizontal bar — together they are the largest, highest-contrast, most colour-saturated elements on the entire page, while the page's real headings "Scheduled Jobs" and "Scheduled Tasks" (`.section-header`, 16px) sit two tiers below them. A metric card whose value is 'every 15 min' also overflows the tracking-tight display treatment, which is tuned for numerals.
  - *Fix:* In scheduling.dart:108-115, render heartbeat status as a `.status-badge` inside a normal card row rather than through `metricCardTemplate`, and render Interval as the numeral only ('15') with 'MIN' in the metric-label — or drop the metric-card treatment entirely for this block. Add an explicit constraint to DESIGN.md § Typography that metric-value takes numerals plus at most a short unit, never a status word or a placeholder glyph.

### `settings/all-tabs` (4)

- **HIGH** · *layout-spacing* — **31 form fields, zero wells and zero fieldsets — every tab is one flat stack of identically-weighted full-width rows**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/settings.html:69`
  - *Evidence:* `settings.html` contains 31 `data-field=` groups, 0 occurrences of `class="well`, 0 `<fieldset>`/`<legend>`, and 1 `<h2>` (Security only). The Workflows tab (`settings.html:69-126`) is 8 identical full-width rows that are semantically 4 role pairs (workflow / planner / executor / reviewer × provider+model) plus a workspace dir, presented with no sub-heading, no rule, no indentation. Compounding it, `app.css:833` `.form-input, .form-select { width: 100% }` with no max-width inside a 900px `--container-max` card: the `type="number"` Port (`settings.html:138`), Max Turns (`settings.html:46`) and Reset Hour (`settings.html:181`) fields each render ~866px wide for a 2–4 character value (visible in desktop-dark/settings.png: the Max Turns box spans the full card). DESIGN.md § Wells names exactly this: `.well` = "form field clusters", `.well-content` = "form sections" — both shipped in design-system.css:681-704 and never used here.
  - *Fix:* Wrap each role pair / semantic cluster in `.well` inside the card (canon container for form field clusters), add a `--text-xs` uppercase `tracking-caps` cluster label, and cap short controls with a field-width scale (e.g. `.form-input--num { max-width: 12ch }`, `--short { max-width: 32ch }`) instead of letting every input stretch to container-max.

- **HIGH** · *typography* — **Card title, field label and field value all render at 14px — the settings card has no type hierarchy**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:493`
  - *Evidence:* desktop-dark/settings.png: "Agent Configuration", "Provider", and the value "claude" are visually the same size; only weight separates them. Cause: `app.css:493` `.settings-card .card-title { font-size: var(--text-base) }` (14px), `app.css:825` `.form-label { font-size: var(--text-base) }` (14px), `app.css:829` `.form-input, .form-select { font-size: var(--text-base) }` (14px). The whole card body uses exactly two sizes (14px and 12px hints); `app.css:488` puts the page h1 at `--text-xl` (20px). DESIGN.md § Typography defines `heading-md` 16px/600 explicitly for "Section headings" and `label-md` 14px/500 for "UI labels" — the 16px rung between page title and label is defined and unused on this page, and `.settings-card .card-title` actively overrides the canon `.card-header` (design-system.css:805, `--text-sm`) upward into the label tier instead of into the heading tier.
  - *Fix:* Move `.settings-card .card-title` to `--text-lg` / `--weight-bold` (canon `heading-md`), keep `.form-label` at `--text-base`/500 (`label-md`), and drop the field value to `--text-base`/400 so the ramp reads heading → label → value instead of three identical rows.

- med · *dialog-feedback* — **"Changes apply after a server restart" is permanent boilerplate rendered in a muddy warning/grey blend, not a state signal**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:1218`
  - *Evidence:* desktop-dark/settings.png (crop y580-790): the note sits below the Save row in a desaturated tan. `app.css:1218` `.section-note-restart { color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--warning) 60%, var(--fg-overlay)); }` blends `#fab387` 60% with `#7f849c` → ≈`#c8a08f`, a brown that reads as neither the warning peach nor neutral helper grey. DESIGN.md § Do's and Don'ts: "Do use semantic colors (success, error, warning, info) only for state. State of *something*." But `dc_settings_controller.js:120-148` populates this note on **every** card whose fields are restart-tier at page load, unconditionally and permanently — it is not state, it is a static label repeated on 7 of 10 tabs, while the actual restart-pending condition has its own separate banner (`restart_banner.html`). Two hairline rules stack 60px apart (`app.css:1016-1019` `.settings-form .form-actions` border-top and `app.css:1213` `.section-note` border-top), boxing the note into its own thin band.
  - *Fix:* Demote the always-on text to plain `--fg-overlay` helper copy inline with the Save row (one divider, not two), and reserve the undiluted `--warning` token for the actual pending-restart state so the colour means something when it appears.

- med · *state-coverage* — **No loading, empty-value or error state vocabulary: 22 "Loading..." disabled boxes, then blanks with the effective default never shown**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/controllers/dc_settings_controller.js:182`
  - *Evidence:* `settings.html` ships 22 `placeholder="Loading..."` inputs, all `disabled`, and 0 uses of `skeleton` — while `design-system.css:1364-1375` provides `.skeleton` / `.skeleton-text`. On the Workflows tab that is 8 greyed boxes each reading "Loading..." until `/api/config` resolves. Once loaded, `dc_settings_controller.js:182` unconditionally runs `input.placeholder = ''`, so nullable fields (Model, Max Turns, Effort — all `nullable: true` in config_meta.dart) become indistinguishable empty boxes: desktop-dark/settings.png shows Model and Max Turns as blank rectangles with no indication of the value actually in force. And if the fetch fails, `dc_settings_controller.js:734-736` only fires a toast — every field stays `disabled` and stuck on "Loading..." forever, with no on-page error or retry.
  - *Fix:* Render `.skeleton-text` rows for the loading pass instead of disabled inputs; on populate set `input.placeholder` to the effective/inherited default (e.g. "inherits claude/opus") rather than clearing it; on fetch failure replace the card body with a `.banner-error` + retry rather than leaving a permanently disabled form.

### `shell/sidebar` (4)

- **HIGH** · *component-adoption* — **Sidebar cannot scroll — canon's `.session-list` scroll container is never adopted, so overflowing nav/chat content is unreachable**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/sidebar.html:8`
  - *Evidence:* design-system.css:96-103 sets `.sidebar { display:flex; flex-direction:column; overflow:hidden; }`. The canon supplies the scroll region — `.session-list { flex:1; overflow-y:auto; padding: var(--sp-2) 0 }` (design-system.css:179-183) — and the reference shell in showcase.html:1046 wraps its session rows in it. sidebar.html renders every row as a direct child of `<aside class="sidebar">` (lines 8-146); no descendant anywhere declares `overflow-y` (grep of app.css + design-system.css confirms only line 102's `overflow:hidden`). Consequence visible in all five desktop screenshots: with only 5 chats + 1 running task the last nav item "Workflows" already sits at y≈872 of a 900px viewport. Clicking "ARCHIVED 13" (sidebar.html:107-113) inserts 13 more 44px rows (≈570px) into a container that clips instead of scrolling — the entire SYSTEM nav becomes unreachable with no scrollbar.
  - *Fix:* Wrap the Workspace/Channels/Running/Chats/Archived block in `<div class="session-list">` per the canon and showcase reference, leaving `.sidebar-header` and the `.sidebar-section` nav outside it as fixed top/bottom regions.

- med · *component-adoption* — **The sidebar's only creation affordance is a borderless ghost button whose centered label is the one center-aligned item in the rail**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/sidebar.html:90`
  - *Evidence:* sidebar.html:90 renders `<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-new-session" ...>New Chat</button>`. `.btn-ghost` is `background: transparent; border-color: transparent; box-shadow: none` (design-system.css:392) and `.btn` sets `justify-content: center` (design-system.css:350); because `.sidebar` is a flex column with default `align-items: stretch`, `.btn-new-session { margin: var(--sp-2) var(--sp-3) }` (design-system.css:403) stretches it to full width. Visible in all five desktop screenshots: "New Chat" sits at x≈130 (rail centre, y=303) while every other row — Agent, running task, all 4 chats, ARCHIVED, all 9 nav items — is left-aligned at x=16. It also has no fill, no border and no accent, so the primary create action is the least visually weighted control in the rail, contradicting DESIGN.md § Colors ("Accent … Reserve it for primary actions").
  - *Fix:* Left-align it to the 16px rail baseline (`justify-content: flex-start` on `.btn-new-session`) and promote it to `.btn-primary`, or at minimum drop `.btn-ghost` so it picks up `.btn`'s `bg-surface0` + top-lit edge.

- med · *typography* — **Provider badge is a hardcoded 10px — below the design system's 12px floor — and repeats "CLAUDE" six times in the rail**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:1775`
  - *Evidence:* `.provider-badge { font-size: 0.625rem }` (app.css:1775-1783) = 10px. The smallest token in DESIGN.md's typography table and in tokens.css:73 is `--text-xs` 12px (`caption`); 10px is one of the 5 hardcoded font-sizes in app.css and is also uppercase with `--tracking-caps`, i.e. the least legible text in the app. It is rendered unconditionally on the Workspace row (sidebar.html:15-17), every channel row (34-36, 47-49), every running task (67-68), every chat (100-102) and every archived chat (122-124) — in the seeded screenshots that is 6 identical "CLAUDE" pills stacked down the rail, carrying zero discriminating information because every session uses the same provider.
  - *Fix:* Use `--text-xs` (12px) instead of the hardcoded 0.625rem, and suppress the badge server-side when all visible sessions share one provider (sidebar.dart already computes `providerLabel` per row — add a single `providersAreUniform` flag to the context and gate `tl:if`).

- low · *brand* — **The brand claw mark is absent from the persistent shell — the one surface present on 100% of views**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/sidebar.html:2`
  - *Evidence:* DESIGN.md § Identity ranks `.claw-mark` as the #2 ownable element ("For logo lockups, empty states, and at most one hero moment per view") and the canon reference lockup in showcase.html:1044 pairs it with the wordmark inside `.sidebar-header`: `<span class="claw-mark" style="font-size:var(--text-base)"></span><span class="logo">DartClaw</span>`. The shipped `.sidebar-header` (sidebar.html:2-6) renders only the text `❯ DartClaw`. `rg claw-mark` across the server templates finds it in exactly two places — tasks.html:106 and projects.html:53 — both empty states that never render on a populated install. The mascot (`logo-avatar-512-8bit.png`) appears nowhere in the shell either, only as a favicon (layout.html:5-8). So the brand's two strongest assets are invisible in normal use, and the chrome's only identity signal is the ❯ glyph plus an animated gradient wordmark.
  - *Fix:* Adopt the showcase lockup in `.sidebar-header`: prepend `<span class="claw-mark" aria-hidden="true"></span>` before the `.logo` span in sidebar.html:3 (one claw per view, satisfying the scarcity doctrine).

### `task-detail` (4)

- **HIGH** · *color-depth* — **Task detail stacks four different container treatments — including one panel on the wrong side of the surface ladder and one column with no container at all**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:2130`
  - *Evidence:* Sampled from desktop-dark/task-detail.png: page bg (29,30,43); meta card (24,24,37) = `--bg-mantle`, *darker* than the page; timeline panel (39,40,57) = `--bg-sub-base`, *lighter* than the page — two top-level siblings on opposite sides of the ground. Sources: `.task-timeline` hand-rolls `background: var(--bg-sub-base); border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: var(--radius-lg)` with no shadow and no `--border-highlight` (app.css:2130-2136); `.task-chat-embed` (app.css:1852-1855) and `.task-no-session` (app.css:1871-1874) hand-roll a third recipe with `--radius` (4px) instead of `--radius-lg` (6px) and no shadow; and the Artifacts column has no container at all — task_detail.html:190-193 drops a bare `.empty-state` onto the page background, so "No artifacts yet" floats in dead space beside a bordered Session card (both themes, and mobile). Canon `.card` (`--bg-mantle` + `--border-highlight` top edge + `--shadow-sm`) is the single answer and is already applied to the meta card.
  - *Fix:* Make `.task-timeline`, `.task-chat-embed`/`.task-no-session` and the Artifacts column all `.card` (or `.well-content` per the container flowchart at DESIGN.md:511) and delete the three bespoke background/radius blocks.

- **HIGH** · *color-depth* — **Server-rendered diffs bypass the entire canonical diff treatment — .diff-line--add/--del/--hunk unused, hunks dumped as one unstyled <pre>**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/web/pages/tasks_page.dart:388`
  - *Evidence:* DESIGN.md:770 makes diffs the one deliberate exception to "syntax coloring is categorical, never state": added/removed lines take a 10% success/error wash with text pulled 60% toward the hue, hunk headers an info wash, via `.diff-line--add`/`--del`/`--hunk` (components.css). All three classes have ZERO references in packages/dartclaw_server. web/pages/tasks_page.dart:348-400 (`_renderDiffHtml`) instead joins every line of every hunk into a single `<pre class="task-artifact-raw">` blob, and renders the hunk header as `<div class="empty-state-text">`. The container app.css:1898-1903 `.diff-view` is `white-space: pre; color: var(--fg-sub0)` — a uniformly dimmed monochrome block. DESIGN.md:774 states outright: "an unhighlighted code block is a drift bug, not a style choice."
  - *Fix:* In `_renderDiffHtml`, emit one element per line classed `.diff-line` + `.diff-line--add`/`--del` by leading `+`/`-`, and the hunk header as `.diff-line--hunk` instead of `.empty-state-text`; drop the `color: var(--fg-sub0)` override on `.diff-view` (app.css:1902) so line colors survive.

- med · *component-adoption* — **The six token-summary KPIs are rendered as 12px-label / 14px-value metadata rows instead of the metric-card tier that already exists as a ready-made component**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/task_detail.html:72`
  - *Evidence:* task_detail.html:72-99 renders Total Tokens, Input/Output, Cache, Duration, Tool Calls and Turns using the same `.task-meta-grid` > `.task-meta-item` > `.task-meta-label` structure as the Type/Status/Provider strip — a 12px uppercase label over an unstyled 14px value. Canon has the exact component: `.card.card-metric--{color}` with `.metric-value` at `--text-3xl` (32px, tracking-tight) and `.metric-label` at `--text-xs` uppercase (design-system.css:978-991), already wrapped as a helper in `metricCardTemplate` (components.dart:53) and a `metricCard` fragment (components.html:25-29). `--text-3xl` has zero usages in app.css. Not visible in the captures only because the seeded tasks have `traceCount == 0`, which suppresses the block at task_detail.dart:82.
  - *Fix:* Render the token-summary block as a grid of `metricCardTemplate(...)` calls (accent for Total Tokens, info for Duration/Turns) so the one numeric payload on the page reaches the metric-value tier.

- med · *state-coverage* — **The timeline is a permanently-rendered empty slab in the page's prime slot: 5 bespoke filter links over zero events, with a bare-sentence empty state unlike its two siblings**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/task_timeline.html:45`
  - *Evidence:* desktop-dark/task-detail.png and task-detail-workflow.png, y=231-379: a 148px panel between the meta card and the real content holding five filter links (All/Status/Tools/Artifacts/Errors) and the sentence "No timeline events yet" — on a completed task that has session content below it. It renders whenever a TaskEventService exists, regardless of event count (tasks_page.dart:225-234 always assigns `timelineHtml`; task_detail.html:101 only tests the string is non-empty). Three problems compound: (a) the empty state is a bare `<span class="empty-state-text">` (task_timeline.html:45-47) while the Session panel 300px below uses the full `.empty-state` icon+title+text pattern (task_detail.html:137-145) and Artifacts uses a third, icon-less variant (task_detail.html:190-193) — three empty-state treatments on one screen; (b) the filters carry no counts and are bespoke `.tl-filter-link` text links (app.css:2138-2160) where DESIGN.md:737 defines toggle chips (`button.chip` with `aria-pressed="true"`, accent tint) as the sanctioned filter control; (c) filters are offered over an empty set.
  - *Fix:* Suppress the timeline panel (or collapse it to a one-line affordance) when `events.isEmpty`; when shown, use one `.empty-state` component across all three panels and rebuild `.tl-filter-bar` as a `.chip-row` of `button.chip[aria-pressed]` with per-kind counts.

### `tasks` (4)

- med · *component-adoption* — **Agent Pool progress bar is a 24px flat --bg-surface1 slab; the canonical .meter is used two elements away in the same template but not here**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/tasks.dart:236`
  - *Evidence:* desktop-dark/tasks.png y≈212-234: a full-width featureless bar, sampled RGB (69,71,90) = `--bg-surface1` (#45475a) against page bg (31,30,45) — the highest-contrast block on the page, and it means "nothing is running". Built by tasks.dart:236-240 `_buildPoolBarHtml` as `.agent-pool-bar > .bar-segment.bar-active{width:0%} + .bar-idle{width:100%}`, styled at app.css:2072-2081 (height 24px, `.bar-idle { background: var(--bg-surface1) }`). Canon `.meter` (design-system.css:1330, 6px recessed track with `--inset-sm`) + `.meter-fill` (gradient + 8px glow, design-system.css:1339) is the prescribed determinate-progress atom (DESIGN.md:602) and is already used for running task cards in the same template (tasks.html:49-51). Painting the *idle* remainder as the filled segment also inverts the reading: 0/1 active renders a 100%-full bar.
  - *Fix:* Replace `.agent-pool-bar`/`.bar-segment` with `.meter` + `.meter-fill` sized to `activePercent` (empty track when 0 active), keep the `0/1 runners active` label as the required paired readout, and delete app.css:2072-2081.

- med · *component-adoption* — **The largest solid grey block in the app is a hand-rolled 24px progress bar that ignores the .meter component**
  - *Evidence:* app.css:2072-2081 — `.agent-pool-bar { height: 24px; background: var(--bg-surface0) }` with `.bar-idle { background: var(--bg-surface1) }`, emitted by tasks.dart:236-240. Measured on desktop-dark/tasks.png: a 900px × 24px slab of flat #45475a spanning y=211–234 at full content width, with no fill, no track inset and no label inside it. It reads as a stalled loading bar. Canon `.meter` (design-system.css:1331-1345) is a 6px `--bg-crust` track with `--inset-sm` and a gradient `.meter-fill` carrying a 8px accent glow, and it is already used on task_detail/workflow_detail/memory_dashboard — just not here. The light equivalent (desktop-light/tasks.png) is the same slab in #bcc0cc.
  - *Fix:* Replace `.agent-pool-bar`/`.bar-segment` with `.meter` + `.meter-fill` (width = active %), delete the app.css rules, and put the "0/1 runners active" count in a `.meter-label` row as DESIGN.md § Meters requires.

- med · *component-adoption* — **Canonical .run-card family unused — the Tasks agent-pool board hand-rolls it as .agent-runner-card/.runner-metric and loses the dot, meter, identicon and pill anatomy**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/tasks.dart:280`
  - *Evidence:* DESIGN.md:751 defines `.run-card` as a `.card` with documented anatomy — status dot, name, `.run-card-step` counter, a `.tool-indicator`/`.meter` body, and a footer with identicon + `.status-pill` — plus `.run-card--attention`. `.run-card`, `.run-card-step`, `.run-card--attention` = ZERO references. templates/tasks.dart:280-297 instead writes `<span class="status-badge agent-state-$state">` followed by four `<div class="runner-metric">` rows (task link, provider badge, turns, tokens), styled at app.css:2097-2102 as plain 12px text lines inside `.agent-runner-card` (app.css:2085ff). desktop-dark/tasks.png shows the resulting "Primary (#0)" card: a grey `Idle` chip over three unlabelled text rows, with no status dot, no meter, no identicon and no pill.
  - *Fix:* Rebuild the runner card as `.card.run-card`: `.status-dot--{live|idle|error}` + runner name + `.run-card-step` counter in the header, `.meter`/`.meter-fill` or `.tool-indicator` for current activity, footer with `.identicon` + `.status-pill`; add `.run-card--attention` for blocked runners. Delete `.runner-metric`/`.agent-runner-card` from app.css.

- med · *layout-spacing* — **Table rows are 59px tall because a 9%-wide CREATED column wraps every relative timestamp onto two lines**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:1649`
  - *Evidence:* desktop-dark/tasks.png rows y=750-900: each row carries a single-line title yet stands ~59px tall because "101d ago" wraps to "101d"/"ago" and "visual-demo" wraps to "visual-"/"demo". `th/td:nth-child(6) { width: 9% }` (app.css:1649-1651) is ~81px of a 900px container; minus 24px of sp-3 padding that leaves ~57px of content box for an 8-character mono string needing ~62px. `vertical-align: top` (app.css:1627) then strands the badges at the top of a two-line row. The result inverts DESIGN.md:319 ("information-dense by default; whitespace is earned") — half of each row's height carries nothing. Meanwhile the TOKENS column is "—" on every visible row, so the column forcing the header to split into TO/KE/NS carries no data at all (it is only populated for running tasks — tasks.dart:142).
  - *Fix:* Give `.task-col-created` enough width to hold the timestamp on one line and set `white-space: nowrap` on it; drop the TOKENS column from the default table (or fold it into the title cell) since it is empty for non-running tasks by construction.

### `health-audit` (3)

- med · *accessibility* — **Guard audit rows put role="button" on a <tr>, destroying the table's row/cell semantics, and the disclosure has no aria-controls**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/audit_table.dart:101`
  - *Evidence:* audit_table.dart:101 emits `<tr class="audit-row" tabindex="0" role="button" aria-expanded="false"><td>…`. `role="button"` overrides the implicit `row` role, so the row is no longer a member of the table's grid: its `<td>` children become owned by a button (an invalid required-owned-element relationship) and screen readers lose column-header association for every audit cell — on the one page whose whole purpose is reading a tabular guard log. The expanded content lives in the next sibling `.audit-detail-row` (toggled by dc_shell_controller.js `toggleAuditRow`, lines ~296-303) with no `id` and no `aria-controls` from the trigger, so the relationship is invisible to AT. `.audit-row` also has no `:focus-visible` rule anywhere in app.css or design-system.css, so it falls back to the UA outline drawn on a table row inside `.table-scroll { overflow-x: auto }`.
  - *Fix:* Drop `role="button"` from the `<tr>` and move the trigger into the first cell as a real `<button class="audit-row-toggle" aria-expanded aria-controls="audit-detail-<id>">`; give the detail row that id. Add `.audit-row-toggle:focus-visible` to the canon focus selector list in design-system.css:337 rather than a new app-local rule.

- med · *component-adoption* — **Audit filter toolbar reimplements the canonical toggle chip as a bespoke .filter-btn**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/audit_table.dart:86-91`
  - *Evidence:* audit_table.dart:86-91 emits `<button class="filter-btn" aria-pressed="true|false" hx-get=...>` — exactly the toggle-filter semantics DESIGN.md canonises under § Chips: "**Toggle chips (filters)** — a `button.chip` with `aria-pressed=\"true\"` takes an accent tint (14% mix on the fill, 40% on the border)". Instead app.css:546-548 defines a private look-alike with its own sizing, borders and an active state built from `color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 15%, var(--bg-base))` — near the canon numbers but not them, and with no shared chip radius/hit-area contract. audit_table.dart:30 also injects a raw `<span style="margin-left:auto"></span>` spacer between the guard group and the verdict group instead of a layout class.
  - *Fix:* Emit `class="chip"` (plus the canon's pressed treatment) for the nine filter buttons and delete `.filter-btn` from app.css:546-548. Replace the inline `margin-left:auto` span with a `.chip-row` / toolbar group class so the guard and verdict groups are structural, not a hack.

- med · *state-coverage* — **Audit empty state claims .empty-state then inline-overrides away everything the component provides**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/audit_table.dart:37-40`
  - *Evidence:* audit_table.dart:37-40 emits `<div class="empty-state" style="padding:var(--sp-6);text-align:center;color:var(--fg-overlay)">No guard events recorded yet</div>`. The inline style halves the canonical `padding: var(--sp-12) var(--sp-4)` and overrides `color: var(--fg-sub0)` down to the disabled tier (design-system.css:1506-1515). No `.icon` child, so the canon's glowing accent icon (design-system.css:1517-1522) never appears; no `<p>`, so the 400px-capped body rule is dead too. Result visible in health-audit.png: one line of gray text, nothing else. Scheduling adds a third, unrelated empty treatment — `.table-empty-cell` (app.css:1126), plain fg-overlay text in a `<td>` (scheduling.html:109,206).
  - *Fix:* Drop the inline style and emit the canonical structure: `<div class="empty-state"><span class="icon icon-shield-check"></span><p>No guard events recorded yet</p></div>`. Do the same for the two `.table-empty-cell` rows on scheduling so all three empty states on this surface share one treatment.

### `health-dashboard` (3)

- **HIGH** · *layout-spacing* — **Health dashboard buries its KPI row below the fold — canon's own dashboard pattern puts card-metric first**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/health_dashboard.html:29-37`
  - *Evidence:* health_dashboard.html orders: featured status card (L15-27) → h2 Services + 5 info cards (L29-32) → h2 Metrics + 4 card-metrics (L34-37) → Guard Activity table (L39-40). In desktop-dark/health.png and desktop-light/health.png (1440x900) the "METRICS" label sits at y≈866 and the metric grid starts at y≈890 — ZERO of the four 32px KPI cards are visible in the first viewport. Everything the user actually sees is 12-13px label/value rows. DESIGN.md § Composition Patterns → "Dashboard layout" prescribes the inverse: `grid-4 card-metric card-metric card-metric ...  ← KPI row` FIRST, then `grid-2 card-featured-accent | card (activity)`, then alerts.
  - *Fix:* Move the metrics grid above the Services grid in health_dashboard.html so the card-metric KPI row is the first thing under the topbar, matching DESIGN.md's dashboard composition. Fold Uptime/Version/Worker out of the featured card and into that KPI row (they are already duplicated there — see the duplication finding).

- **HIGH** · *typography* — **Health page content contains no text above 13px — the 20px page-title tier is never reached**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/design-system.css:317-321`
  - *Evidence:* Full type inventory of desktop-dark/health.png above the fold: topbar "System Health" = `.session-title-static` at --text-base/14px (design-system.css:317-320, topbar.html pageTopbar fragment); status badge 12px; `.section-label` 12px; `.card-title` = `font: inherit` from `.card-header` = --text-sm/13px (design-system.css:805-816); `.card-badge` 12px; `.card-row` label+value 13px (app.css:582). Nothing on the page exceeds 14px, and the 14px is chrome, not content. DESIGN.md § Typography table: `page-title | 20px / 600 | App name, page title`. The sidebar logo is also under-tiered at --text-lg/16px (design-system.css:155-156). So the tier DESIGN.md reserves for page titles is used by neither the page title nor the app name.
  - *Fix:* Raise `.topbar .session-title-static` to --text-xl (20px) / --weight-bold to match the documented `page-title` tier, and `.sidebar-header .logo` likewise. This is the single cheapest change that breaks the 12/13px monotone on this page.

- med · *copy-content* — **Health service cards repeat the same three values 2-3x each and report hardcoded placeholder facts**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/health_dashboard.dart:55-56,64,72-74,83-84,147`
  - *Evidence:* health_dashboard.dart duplicates its own data: Worker card badge = `workerState` (L57) and row "State" = `workerState` (L55) → "idle" printed twice 40px apart; Sessions badge = `'$sessionCount total'` (L72) and row "Total" = `'$sessionCount'` (L74) → "28 total" / "28"; `dbSizeStr` appears as Database→Size (L64), Storage→Search DB (L83) AND metric card "DB Size" (L147) — the same "28 KB" presented as three different services' measurements. All four duplications are visible simultaneously in desktop-dark/health.png. Separately, three values are hardcoded strings, not measurements: `'claude binary'` (L56), `'active'` for FTS5 Index (L65), `'file-based'` (L84) — a health dashboard asserting green status it never checked. Net: 5 cards and ~13 rows convey roughly 6 distinct facts, which is why the page reads as filler.
  - *Fix:* Drop the rows that restate their own card badge (Worker→State, Sessions→Total) and collapse the Database and Storage cards into one (they report the same byte count). Either wire FTS5 Index / Runtime / Format to real probes or remove them — do not print a hardcoded "active".

### `kg-timeline` (3)

- med · *component-adoption* — **Timeline tab strip has no baseline rule, so the active tab's accent underline floats unanchored**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:2952`
  - *Evidence:* Visible in all four kg-timeline screenshots (clearest at 2.4x in the Hub/Timeline crop): a short green 2px underline sits below "Timeline" with a visible gap and no rule running across the strip, so the tabs read as two loose words. `.knowledge-tabs { display:flex; gap: var(--sp-2); margin-bottom: var(--sp-4); overflow-x:auto }` (app.css:2952-2957) is a near-copy of `.tab-bar` (app.css:1063) minus its `border-bottom: var(--border)` — but it reuses `.tab-btn`, whose active state is `border-bottom-color: var(--accent)` on a 2px bottom border (app.css:1064-1066), a treatment that only works against a container rule. The memory dashboard's own tab strip (memory_dashboard.html:145 `.tab-bar`) renders correctly, so the app ships two divergent tab containers.
  - *Fix:* Delete `.knowledge-tabs` and use `.tab-bar` in kg_timeline.html:14 (add `overflow-x:auto` to `.tab-bar` if horizontal scroll is needed), so both tab strips share one definition and the active underline sits on the baseline rule.

- med · *state-coverage* — **KG Timeline empty state is one 13px grey sentence in a bare card; the canonical .empty-state component and shared fragment go unused**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/kg_timeline.html:40`
  - *Evidence:* All four kg-timeline screenshots show the same thing: a full-width bordered box with a single left-aligned muted line, "No temporal KG facts recorded yet." — no icon, no title, no next step. Source: kg_timeline.html:40-42 `<div class="card kg-timeline-empty"><p class="card-detail card-detail-muted">` = `--text-sm` 13px in `--fg-overlay` (app.css:496,500). Meanwhile `.empty-state` (centred, sp-12 padding, accent glow icon, `.empty-state p` at max-width 400px) ships in the served design-system.css:1506-1526, a reusable `emptyState` Trellis fragment exists at components.html:6-10, and tasks.html:105-108 already renders the full pattern (claw-mark + title + text). Additionally `.kg-timeline-empty` and `.kg-timeline-error` have **zero** matching rules in app.css, design-system.css or tokens.css — dead class hooks.
  - *Fix:* Replace kg_timeline.html:40-42 with the `.empty-state` structure (icon + `<p><strong>No temporal facts yet</strong></p>` + explanatory line + a link back to /knowledge), and delete the unstyled `.kg-timeline-empty` / `.kg-timeline-error` hooks or give them rules.

- low · *typography* — **Timeline filter labels are uppercased without the canon's caps tracking, so they read cramped next to identical labels elsewhere**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:3280`
  - *Evidence:* `.kg-timeline-controls .field-label { color: var(--fg-sub0); font-size: var(--text-xs); text-transform: uppercase; }` (app.css:3280-3284) — uppercase with no `letter-spacing`. DESIGN.md:381 states the rule: "monospace gets … cramped at tiny uppercase sizes … `+0.08em` (`tracking-caps`) on uppercase micro-labels (section labels, role labels, table headers)." Every other 12px uppercase label in the app applies it — `.section-label` (design-system.css:1538), `.card-metric .metric-label` (design-system.css:991), `.data-table th` (design-system.css:1559). Side-by-side in the screenshots, "CATEGORY"/"AS OF" on kg-timeline.png are visibly tighter than "OVERVIEW"/"MEMORY SIZE" on memory.png at the same size.
  - *Fix:* Add `letter-spacing: var(--tracking-caps);` to app.css:3280 — or drop the local override entirely and reuse `.section-label`/an app-level `.field-label` base, since `.field` and `.field-label` currently have no unscoped definition anywhere.

### `settings/security` (3)

- **HIGH** · *dialog-feedback* — **`window.prompt` is the editor for security guard rules — two chained, uncancellable free-text OS prompts, one of which asks for an enum the app already renders as a `<select>` 60px away**
  - *Evidence:* dc_settings_controller.js:569 `var next = window.prompt('Guard extension value', defaultValue)` and :573 `var level = window.prompt('File access level', current && current.level ? current.level : 'no_access')`. This is the edit path for guard extensions — rules that decide what an agent may execute, read, write, or reach. Three concrete defects: (a) the level prompt is free text, while the *add* form immediately below in the same panel renders the correct control — templates/settings.html:502-506 `<select class="form-select" data-guard-editor-level>` with exactly the three legal values `no_access` / `read_only` / `no_delete`, matching the server enum at api/guard_editor_service.dart:431 `const _fileLevels = {'no_access','read_only','no_delete'}` (and security/guard_builder.dart:6). A typo in the prompt round-trips to the server and comes back as a red toast at dc_settings_controller.js:592. (b) The two prompts are chained, not atomic: cancelling the second silently discards the value typed into the first. (c) Add and Edit are two different UIs for the same record — Add is a styled inline form row, Edit is a pair of OS prompts.
  - *Fix:* Replace both prompts with one `<dialog class="dialog card card-glass">` guard-rule form reusing the exact controls the add-row already has (`[data-guard-editor-field]` select, `[data-guard-editor-value]` input, `[data-guard-editor-level]` select with its three options, shown/hidden by the same `guard === 'file'` logic at dc_settings_controller.js:467), pre-populated from the row being edited and submitted as one PUT. Same dialog, blank, can back the Add action — one editor, two entry points.

- med · *component-adoption* — **Guard editor table is a hand-rolled boxed grid instead of the canon .data-table**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:3715`
  - *Evidence:* `settings.html:485` uses `class="guard-editor-table"`, styled at `app.css:3715-3739`: `border: var(--border)` on **every** `th` and `td` (a fully boxed grid), `th { text-transform: uppercase }` with no `letter-spacing` and no `font-weight`, and no `tbody tr:hover`. The canon `.data-table` (design-system.css:1568-1590) is the opposite treatment on all four points: `border-bottom` only, `th` at `--text-xs` / `--weight-bold` / `letter-spacing: var(--tracking-caps)` / `--fg-sub0` over a stronger rule, plus row hover — and its comment states the intent ("Open header (no filled bar) … reads lighter and more deliberate than a boxed header row"). DESIGN.md § Typography also names table headers specifically as a `tracking-caps` site: "+0.08em (tracking-caps) on uppercase micro-labels (section labels, role labels, table headers)" — mono uppercase at 12px without it is exactly the cramped case the canon corrects.
  - *Fix:* Change the markup to `class="data-table guard-editor-table"` and reduce the app rule to the genuinely local bits (`min-width: 560px`, `.guard-editor-value` mono/break-all), deleting the duplicated border/th/padding declarations.

- med · *dialog-feedback* — **Risk gradient is inverted: the three security-critical deletes have no confirmation at all, while recoverable config deletes get one**
  - *Evidence:* Sweep of every destructive call site (`method: 'DELETE'` across static/controllers/): 7 total. Confirmed — dc_scheduling_controller.js:226 (inline bar), :407 (native confirm), dc_shell_controller.js:370 (native confirm), dc_projects_controller.js:194 (native confirm). Unconfirmed, single click, no undo — dc_settings_controller.js:597-600 guard-extension delete (`var del = event.target.closest('[data-guard-editor-delete]'); if (!del) return; fetch(… method: 'DELETE'…)`, button rendered at :489), :907 DM allowlist entry removal, :925 group allowlist entry removal. The three unguarded ones remove security rules and channel access-control entries — i.e. they *widen* the agent's blast radius and who can talk to it — whereas the guarded scheduled-task delete only edits a config file the user can re-add. Success is reported by an unremarkable `showToast('success', 'Entry removed')` (:917) after the fact.
  - *Fix:* Route all three through the canonical confirm dialog, naming the exact rule/entry being removed and stating the consequence ("Removing this rule allows the agent to …"). Given DESIGN.md §Panels reserves severity-bearing treatment for guards, use the danger variant. This is the only bucket where the confirmation is load-bearing rather than merely polite.

### `tasks + task-detail` (3)

- **HIGH** · *typography* — **Nothing on the tasks or task-detail surface is typeset above 14px — page-title, heading-md, display and metric-value tiers are all unused, and three sizes fall below the scale floor**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:1612`
  - *Evidence:* Every font-size declaration in the tasks / task-detail / agent-overview / timeline blocks of app.css (lines 1601-2270) resolves to: 21× `--text-xs` (12px), 8× `--text-sm` (13px), 4× `--text-base` (14px), plus `2.5rem` on an emoji glyph and three off-scale hardcodes — `0.625rem` (app.css:1782), `11px` (app.css:2259), `10px` (app.css:2264). The only `--text-lg`/`--text-xl` in that whole range are inside the New Task modal (app.css:1964,1967), which is off-screen. So the section headings that should be `heading-md` 16px/600 per the DESIGN.md:387 scale — `.task-status-group h3` (app.css:1612), `.task-chat-column h3, .task-artifact-column h3` (app.css:1849), `.agent-overview h3` (app.css:2069) — all render at `--text-base`, identical to the body text beneath them. In desktop-dark/tasks.png "Agent Pool", "Review" and "Accepted" are typographically indistinguishable from row content: the surface has no hierarchy above body size at all.
  - *Fix:* Move the three `h3` rules to `--text-lg` (16px/600) per heading-md; replace `0.625rem`/`11px`/`10px` with `--text-xs`; reserve `--text-2xl`/`--text-3xl` for the empty-state title and the token KPIs.

- med · *color-depth* — **Metadata labels are painted in --fg-overlay, the one color canon explicitly forbids for them**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:1827`
  - *Evidence:* DESIGN.md:990 states: "`fg-overlay` is intentionally low-emphasis; use it for placeholders/disabled/helper text, never for essential metadata or table labels." `.task-meta-label` — which labels Type, Status, Provider, Created by, Created, Started, Completed, Description and the entire token summary — is `color: var(--fg-overlay)` (app.css:1827-1830). So are `.task-goal-text` (app.css:1936-1940), `.tl-event-timestamp` (app.css:2213-2219), `.task-event` (app.css:2255-2261) and `.empty-state-text` (app.css:1810). In desktop-light/task-detail.png the TYPE/STATUS/PROVIDER/CREATED labels are the palest text on the card and read as disabled; this blanket de-emphasis of every label is a large part of the "flat and gray" impression.
  - *Fix:* Move `.task-meta-label`, `.tl-event-timestamp` and `.task-goal-text` to `--fg-sub0` (the metadata/caption color canon uses for `.card-footer` and `.data-table th`), reserving `--fg-overlay` for placeholder and disabled text.

- med · *component-adoption* — **Status badges ship without the canonical embedded dot, table cells use the low-weight badge where canon prescribes .status-pill (0 usages app-wide), and the same value is cased two ways**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/components.html:21`
  - *Evidence:* components.html:21-23 renders `<span class="status-badge status-badge-{variant}">text</span>` with no `.status-dot` child, though DESIGN.md:597 defines status badges as a "subtle semantic chip with embedded dot" and showcase.html:491-493 shows every badge carrying one. Canon even reserves the space — `.status-badge { gap: var(--sp-2); padding: 3px var(--sp-2) 3px 6px }` (design-system.css:1246-1257) — so badges render with 8px of unexplained left padding. Separately DESIGN.md:598 assigns status *pills* ("gradient-filled … more weight than badges") to "card footers, **table cells**, compact status summaries"; `.status-pill`/`--live/--error/--warning/--info` are fully defined in design-system.css but grep over `lib/src/templates/` and `app.css` returns zero hits. And the same value is cased two ways: tasks.dart:150 passes `text: statusName` (lowercase "accepted"/"review" in the table) while task_detail.dart:129 passes `titleCase(statusName)` ("Accepted" on the detail page) — both visible in the captures.
  - *Fix:* Add a `.status-dot status-dot--{state}` child to the `statusBadge` fragment; switch the tasks-table STATUS column to `.status-pill status-pill--{live|error|warning|info}`; pass `titleCase(statusName)` at tasks.dart:150. Note dc_tasks_controller.js:382-387 and :406-411 poll off `.status-badge` textContent — keep the dot span empty so that string stays the label.

### `workflows` (3)

- med · *component-adoption* — **Status filter row hand-rolls a flat gray active state although the app already ships an accent-underlined tab component**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:2766`
  - *Evidence:* workflow_list.html:15-21 renders the six status filters as `.btn.btn-ghost` with `.btn-active` appended; app.css:2766-2769 defines `.workflow-status-filters .btn-active { background: var(--bg-surface1); color: var(--fg); }` — a plain gray rectangle indistinguishable from `.btn-ghost:hover` (design-system.css:393, `background: var(--bg-surface0)`). In desktop-dark/workflows.png the selected "All" reads as a hover artifact, not a selection. app.css:1063-1066 already ships `.tab-bar` / `.tab-btn.active { color: var(--accent); border-bottom-color: var(--accent) }` (used on settings). Neither `.btn-active` nor any segmented-control/filter-chip exists in DESIGN.md or design-system.css.
  - *Fix:* Render the status filters with `.tab-bar` / `.tab-btn` (accent underline + accent text) and delete `.btn-active`; if a pill-shaped filter set is wanted instead, add one filter-chip component to the canon rather than a page-local override.

- med · *layout-spacing* — **Workflow run cards double their padding and replace the canon card hover with a non-transitioning flat background swap**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:2773`
  - *Evidence:* workflow_list.html:39 wraps `.card` (which already sets `padding: var(--sp-4)`, design-system.css) around `.workflow-run-link { display: block; padding: var(--sp-3) }` (app.css:2780) — a 28px inset on every edge for two lines of text. In desktop-dark/workflows.png each run card is ~110px tall for a name + one meta line, against ~75px for a Projects card carrying the same amount of content (projects.png) — the two card families visibly disagree on density. Worse, `.workflow-run-card { transition: background var(--transition) }` (app.css:2773) replaces `.card`'s four-property transition shorthand at equal specificity and later in the cascade, so the canon 1px hover lift, border tint and glow now snap with no easing; `.workflow-run-card:hover { background: var(--bg-surface0) }` (app.css:2776) then overwrites the accent top-glow gradient with a flat fill. Finally `.workflow-run-meter { width: 60px; height: 4px }` (app.css:2815) contradicts the canon `.meter { height: 6px }` and, at 0/0 steps, renders as an orphan 60px dash floating between "0/0 steps" and "101d ago" with nothing to indicate.
  - *Fix:* Drop `padding` from `.workflow-run-link` (app.css:2780) and let `.card` own it. Delete the `transition` and `:hover` overrides at app.css:2773/2776 so run cards inherit the canon lift + accent glow. Set `.workflow-run-meter` to `height: 6px` and suppress the meter entirely when `totalSteps == 0`.

- low · *layout-spacing* — **Run cards double their padding (card 16px + inner link 12px = 28px), inflating each 2-line row to ~110px**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:2780`
  - *Evidence:* `.card` sets `padding: var(--sp-4)` (design-system.css:789). `.workflow-run-card` (app.css:2771-2774) does not reset it, and `.workflow-run-link` (app.css:2780-2785) adds `display: block; padding: var(--sp-3)`. Total inset 28px on every side. Measured in desktop-dark/workflows.png: first run card spans y=203→313 = 110px tall to carry one 14px title line and one 13px meta line, so four runs consume 480px of a 900px column with almost no information density.
  - *Fix:* Zero the card padding on `.workflow-run-card` (`padding: 0`) and let the anchor own the inset, or drop the inner padding and make the whole `.card` the click target with `stretched-link`-style positioning.

### `404` (2)

- med · *brand* — **404 page ignores the canon empty-state/brand vocabulary and drops the app shell**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/error_page.html:1`
  - *Evidence:* error_page.html is four bespoke divs (`.error-page`/`.error-code`/`.error-title`/`.error-detail`) plus a button — no mascot, no `.claw-mark`, no `.empty-state`, no `.text-gradient`. DESIGN.md:332 lists `logo-avatar-512-8bit.png` for "Favicon/avatar, masthead, empty states, about screens", :339-340 name empty states as the mascot's and claw-mark's designated brand moment, and showcase.html:904-909 shows the exact recipe (`.empty-state` > 64px pixel-art mascot > bold line > body line > primary button). desktop-dark/notfound.png and desktop-light/notfound.png show three centered lines of muted text on an otherwise blank 1440x900 canvas, with no sidebar, no topbar and no route back other than "Back to Home".
  - *Fix:* Rebuild `error_page.html` on `.empty-state` with the 8-bit mascot at 64px, keep the code/title/detail lines inside it, and render it inside the app shell (sidebar + topbar) so a bad deep link doesn't eject the user from navigation.

- med · *color-depth* — **404's largest element is painted in `--fg-overlay`, the disabled/placeholder token**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:310`
  - *Evidence:* app.css:310-314 `.error-code { font-size: 4rem; color: var(--fg-overlay) }`. tokens.css:28 defines `--fg-overlay: #7f849c` as "Placeholders, disabled, low-emphasis helper text" — the bottom of the four-step foreground ramp. In desktop-dark/notfound.png the 64px "404" is visibly dimmer than the 20px "Page Not Found" beneath it (`--fg`), inverting the hierarchy: the biggest thing on the page reads as greyed-out chrome.
  - *Fix:* Use `--fg-sub0` at minimum, or make the code the deliberate brand moment with `.text-gradient` (design-system.css:1639, "hero titles and brand moments only, max once per view") — this page is exactly the one-per-view case.

### `chat` (2)

- low · *component-adoption* — **The chat command palette re-implements canonical .palette-section/.palette-item as .composer-palette-header/.composer-palette-option, dropping the icon, context and keycap columns**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/controllers/dc_chat_controller.js:451`
  - *Evidence:* DESIGN.md:743-745 defines `.palette-section` (uppercase section header) and `.palette-item` (four-column grid: icon · `.palette-item-label` · ellipsized `.palette-item-context` · trailing `kbd`) with `.palette-item--active` as the keyboard cursor (bg-surface0 + accent left edge); showcase.html has a Command Palette section. All 5 classes = ZERO references. templates/chat.html:63-71 builds `.composer-palette.card.card-glass` > `.composer-palette-header` + `.composer-palette-list`, and dc_chat_controller.js:451,505 generates rows as `.composer-palette-option`. app.css:16-24 (`.composer-palette-header`) duplicates the `.palette-section` recipe declaration-for-declaration (sp-2/sp-3 padding, fg-overlay, text-xs, weight-medium, uppercase, tracking-caps); app.css:32-52 (`.composer-palette-option`) is a two-column flex with no icon slot, no context column, no keycap, and colors the whole first span `var(--accent)` — contrary to DESIGN.md:778 accent scarcity.
  - *Fix:* Emit `.palette-section` for the header and `.palette-item` rows (icon span, `.palette-item-label`, `.palette-item-context`, trailing `<kbd>`), using `.palette-item--active` for the keyboard cursor; delete `.composer-palette-header`/`-option`/`-empty` from app.css.

- low · *state-coverage* — **Canonical .msg-thinking slot unused — chat's pre-stream state is a bare .claw-loader with no label, no reduced-motion fallback, sharing an element with the streaming block cursor**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/chat.html:110`
  - *Evidence:* DESIGN.md:687 defines `.msg-thinking` as "the sanctioned pre-stream composition state, and *the* claw moment of the chat view: an assistant message showing the `.claw-loader` plus a muted 'thinking' label with an animated ellipsis (reusing the `.tool-indicator.pending` blink)", degrading under reduced motion to the static claw-mark + text. `.msg-thinking` and `.msg-thinking-label` = ZERO references. templates/chat.html:110-113 instead nests the loader directly inside `<div class="msg-content streaming" id="streaming-content">` with only an `aria-label`, so there is no visible "thinking" text and no reduced-motion degradation path; dc_chat_controller.js:328 removes it on the first delta. Because the loader lives on the element that also carries `.streaming` (whose `::after` is the blinking block cursor, DESIGN.md:711), the claw and the block cursor render simultaneously before the first token.
  - *Fix:* Emit the pre-stream state as a sibling `.msg-thinking` block (`.claw-loader` + `.msg-thinking-label`), swap it out on first delta, and add `.streaming` to `#streaming-content` only once streaming begins so the cursor and the claw never coexist.

### `projects / workflows` (2)

- med · *interaction-glitch* — **The only action on a project card and the only launch control on a workflow-definition card are `.btn-ghost` — transparent bg, transparent border, so they read as static text**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/workflow_list.html:85`
  - *Evidence:* design-system.css:392 `.btn-ghost { background: transparent; border-color: transparent; ... }` — chrome only appears on hover. projects.html:29 makes "Fetch" (the sole visible action on both cards, since `isEditable` is false for local/config-defined projects) a `btn btn-ghost btn-sm`; in desktop-dark/projects.png it renders as an unaligned gray word floating below the title with nothing to indicate it is clickable. workflow_list.html:85 makes the definition card's launch control `<summary class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm">Run</summary>` — `.btn`'s `display: inline-flex` also suppresses the `<summary>` disclosure marker, so in mobile-dark/workflows.png "Run" is a bare word with no triangle and no button. DESIGN.md § Buttons assigns `.btn-primary` to primary actions and `.btn-ghost` to "transparent bg, no border".
  - *Fix:* Promote the card's single/primary action to `.btn` (default surface + border) or `.btn-primary`; keep `.btn-ghost` only for secondary actions that sit beside a stronger one. For the `<summary>`, add a chevron affordance (the `.tool-call-summary::after` recipe) or make it a real `.btn`.

- med · *typography* — **Card titles carry no typographic step above their own metadata — 14px inherited vs 13px meta across run, definition, and project cards**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:2798`
  - *Evidence:* app.css:2798 `.workflow-run-name { font-weight: var(--weight-medium); }` and app.css:2843 `.workflow-definition-name { font-weight: var(--weight-medium); }` declare no `font-size`, so both inherit the body 14px (`--text-base`, design-system.css:51-58), while `.workflow-run-meta` (app.css:2807) and `.workflow-definition-desc` (app.css:2854) are `--text-sm` 13px. projects.html:19 uses a bare `<strong>` with no class, also 14px. A 1px delta is not a hierarchy — in desktop-dark/workflows.png "code-review" and "0/0 steps  101d ago  18,640 tokens" are the same optical size, which is the direct cause of the run board reading as undifferentiated text blocks.
  - *Fix:* Give card titles a real tier — `--text-lg` (16px) at `--weight-bold` per DESIGN.md:389 `heading-md` — and push meta down to `--text-xs` (12px, the `caption` tier) so the ratio is 16/12 rather than 14/13.

### `settings/tab-bar` (2)

- med · *color-depth* — **Sticky settings tab strip paints an opaque flat --bg-base slab over the atmospheric body gradient**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:1167`
  - *Evidence:* desktop-light/settings.png (crop x400-1310, y280-420): the tab row renders as a lighter rectangle with hard vertical seams at both container-max edges and an abrupt top edge, clearly separated from the page behind it. Cause: `app.css:1167-1178` `.settings-tabs { position: sticky; top: 0; background: var(--bg-base); }`. DESIGN.md § Body background specifies a fixed 3-stop `linear-gradient(170deg, crust → base → mantle)` plus three ambient radial glows and film grain — a single flat `--bg-base` fill can only match that gradient at one scroll position, so at the top of the page it reads as a pasted-on block.
  - *Fix:* Make the sticky strip inherit the ground instead of overpainting it: drop the opaque `background: var(--bg-base)` in favour of `backdrop-filter: blur(...)` over a translucent `color-mix(in srgb, var(--bg-base) 70%, transparent)` (the same material logic as `.card-glass` in design-system.css:884), or bleed the strip full-bleed to the content-area edges so there is no seam.

- med · *layout-spacing* — **Tab labels don't predict contents: "Server" hides Authentication, System Health and Workspace, and pending-pairing badges are unreachable inside display:none panels**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/settings.html:13`
  - *Evidence:* Card-to-tab distribution in settings.html: `data-tab="server"` carries **4** cards — Server Configuration (`:131`), Authentication (`:434`), System Health (`:449`) and Workspace + git-sync toggles (`:548`) — none of which the label "Server" predicts, while Memory (`:212`, 2 fields), Scheduling (`:273`, 2 fields) and Context (`:238`, 3 fields) each get a whole top-level tab for a single small card, producing the near-empty page seen in desktop-dark/settings.png (the Agent card ends at y≈768 of a 900px viewport). More seriously, the pairing-pending counters at `settings.html:306`, `:322` and `:338` live inside the three `data-tab="channels"` cards, which `dc_settings_controller.js:31` sets to `display: none` unless that tab is active — so an actionable "3 devices waiting to pair" is completely invisible from the Settings page unless the user happens to click Channels.
  - *Fix:* Group the ten flat tabs into 3–4 labelled sections (Agent & Workflows / Server & Security / Channels & Providers / Memory & Context) or move Authentication+Health+Workspace out of "Server" into their own labelled tab, and surface counts on the tab itself (a `.status-badge` on `.settings-tab` fed by the same pairing/guard counts) so hidden panels can still signal that they need attention.

### `shell/topbar` (2)

- med · *dialog-feedback* — **Three `alert()` calls report restart failures in a controller that already imports and uses `showToast` eight times**
  - *Evidence:* dc_shell_controller.js:488 `.then((data) => alert('Restart failed: ' + (data.error?.message || 'Unknown error')))`, :489 `.catch(() => alert('Restart failed'))`, :491 `.catch(() => alert('Failed to reach server'))` — three blocking OS alerts inside a single method, `confirmRestart()`. The same file imports `showToast` at line 10 and calls it 8 times for structurally identical failures (e.g. :375 `showToast('error', error.message || 'Failed to delete session')`, :386 resume failure). DESIGN.md:674 already classifies exactly this case — `| Transient error | Toast (.toast-error) | Failed to rename, failed to delete |` — so unlike the confirm sites this one is not even canon-blocked; it is a plain adoption miss that 0.22 walked past. Aggravating: an alert here can fire while the full-viewport `.restart-overlay` (app.css:2005-2010, `z-index: 9999`) is being shown, and the alert blocks the event loop on top of it.
  - *Fix:* Swap all three `alert(...)` for `showToast('error', ...)` with the same strings. For the persistent case (server genuinely unreachable after a restart attempt) prefer the canonical banner tier instead — the app already ships `.banner.banner-restart` markup at templates/restart_banner.html:1 and a `showRestartBanner()` handler at dc_shell_controller.js:467, so the failure state has a home.

- med · *layout-spacing* — **Three different page-header patterns across pages; the page title is printed twice on Settings, Knowledge Hub and Projects**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/settings.html:9`
  - *Evidence:* Pattern A — no in-page title, topbar only: tasks.html, health_dashboard.html, scheduling.html, memory_dashboard.html. Pattern B — bespoke `.settings-header h1` (settings.html:9-12, styled separately at app.css:487-489). Pattern C — canonical `.pagehead > .page-title` (knowledge_hub.html:8-13, projects.html:9-12, app.css:601-613). Visible result: settings.png shows "Settings" in the topbar at y=23 AND again as a 20px h1 at y=88; knowledge-hub.png shows "Knowledge Hub" twice the same way; tasks.png and health.png show it once. The shell therefore gives no consistent answer to "where does the page title live", and two of five audited pages waste ~70px of first-fold height repeating it.
  - *Fix:* Pick one rule (recommend: topbar owns the title at the `page-title` tier, pages carry only a subtitle/description + actions), delete the duplicate `<h1>`s from settings.html and knowledge_hub.html, and retire `.settings-header` in favour of `.pagehead`.

### `channel-whatsapp / channel-signal / channel-gchat` (1)

- low · *state-coverage* — **A not-running channel is expressed by a badge alone; every control stays live and the hints actively lie**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/channel_detail.dart:51`
  - *Evidence:* channel_detail.dart:51 `final isConnected = statusLabel == 'Connected';` and :74 `'showDisconnectAction': isConnected && pairingHref != null` — the sole behavioural consequence of not being connected is hiding the Disconnect button. desktop-dark/channel-whatsapp.png shows the full consequence: a small 'Not running' pill top-right while the entire page below renders as an operational console — four live DM policy cards, four group policy cards, two enabled allowlist forms with 'Add entry'/'Add group' buttons, checkboxes — and the hints at channel_detail.html:125 ('DM allowlist changes take effect immediately.') and :189/:201 ('require a restart') both assert a running channel. There is no Start/Connect action and no explanation of what 'Not running' means or how to fix it. Same on channel-signal.png and channel-gchat.png.
  - *Fix:* Add a not-running state to channel_detail.dart: a `banner banner-warning` under the hero explaining the channel is not running with a Start/Pairing action, dim the policy panels (`aria-disabled` + reduced opacity), and swap the 'takes effect immediately' hint for 'applies when the channel starts'.

### `global (health, memory, session-info, scheduling, tasks, task-detail, workflow-detail, workflow-list, chat, error page)` (1)

- **HIGH** · *accessibility* — **Most pages have no <h1> and the visible page title is a <span>, so the document outline starts at h2 and there is no skip link to #main-content**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/topbar.html:38`
  - *Evidence:* topbar.html:38 renders the page title as `<span class="session-title-static" tl:text="${title}">` — not a heading — and the pageTopbar fragment is what every dashboard page uses. Counting `<h1>` per template: chat.html 0, health_dashboard.html 0, memory_dashboard.html 0, session_info.html 0, scheduling.html 0, tasks.html 0, task_detail.html 0, workflow_detail.html 0, workflow_list.html 0, error_page.html 0 — only channel_detail, kg_timeline, knowledge_hub, login, projects and settings have one. health_dashboard.html:29 is the first heading on that page and it is `<h2 class="section-label">Services</h2>`. Separately, every page sets `<main id="main-content" tabindex="-1">` (a skip-link target) but no template renders a skip link — grep for `skip` across lib/src/templates returns nothing — so keyboard users tab the full sidebar (nav + up to 13 archived chats + 8 system links) on every page load.
  - *Fix:* Promote the topbar title to `<h1 class="session-title-static">` in the pageTopbar/plainTopbar fragments (styling is unchanged; `.session-title-static` already sets its own font-size/weight), or add a page-level `<h1 class="sr-only">` to the templates that lack one. Add `<a class="skip-link" href="#main-content">Skip to content</a>` as the first child of `<body>` in layout.html with the standard visually-hidden-until-focus treatment, and add `.skip-link` to design-system.css so it is canon rather than app-local.

### `global (health, memory, session-info, task-detail, settings, scheduling)` (1)

- **HIGH** · *accessibility* — **--fg-overlay is used for essential metric/detail/table labels across the app, which DESIGN.md explicitly forbids and which fails AA in light theme**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:563,583,595,1041,1084,1126,1810`
  - *Evidence:* DESIGN.md:990: `fg-overlay` is intentionally low-emphasis; use it for placeholders/disabled/helper text, never for essential metadata or table labels. app.css uses it for exactly that in at least seven label classes: `.detail-label` (563), `.card-row-label` (583), `.metric-card .metric-label` (595), `.meter-label` (1041), `.tab-meta-label` (1084), `.table-empty-cell` (1126), `.empty-state-text` (1810). Computed ratios for #7b8094 (light): 3.47:1 on --bg-base, 3.22:1 on --bg-mantle (cards), 2.54:1 on --bg-surface0 — all below AA 4.5 at the 12px size these labels render. Dark #7f849c is 4.44:1 on --bg-base (also below 4.5) and 3.40:1 on --bg-surface0. Visible in shots/desktop-light/health.png: the Uptime/Version/Worker/State/Runtime/Size/Type row labels are washed out to near-invisible against the card, while their values in --fg read normally.
  - *Fix:* Move the label tier from `--fg-overlay` to `--fg-sub0` (light 4.94:1 on base / 4.60:1 on mantle; dark 7.37:1 / 7.89:1) for `.detail-label`, `.card-row-label`, `.metric-label`, `.meter-label`, `.tab-meta-label`, `.table-empty-cell` and `.empty-state-text`. Leave `--fg-overlay` only on true placeholder/disabled/helper strings (`.form-hint`, `::placeholder`).

### `global (knowledge-hub, kg-timeline, task-detail, workflow-detail)` (1)

- med · *responsive* — **The mobile 48px touch-target rule keys on button/summary/[role=button], so every anchor-based control (tabs, pager) stays at ~30px**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:3755,3771,3785`
  - *Evidence:* app.css:3755 `@media (max-width: 768px) { button, summary, [role="button"] { min-width: 48px; min-height: 48px } }` plus an explicit allow-list at app.css:3771 (`.settings-tab, .topbar-back`) and 3785 (`.card-link, .guard-audit-link`). The knowledge/timeline/task-detail tabs are anchors: knowledge_hub.html:15-17 `<a class="tab-btn active" href="/knowledge">Hub</a>` — `.tab-btn` (app.css:1064) gives only `padding: var(--sp-2) var(--sp-3)` on 13px text ≈ 30px tall. Same for `.pager-link` (app.css:3191, `padding: var(--sp-2) var(--sp-3)`, knowledge_hub.html:63-66). Measured in shots/mobile-dark/knowledge-hub.png: the Hub/Timeline tab hit boxes span roughly y=163–190, ~28px — against DESIGN.md's ≥48px mobile contract. The class-name allow-list approach guarantees this recurs for every new anchor control.
  - *Fix:* Replace the allow-list with an intent-based selector inside the ≤768px block: `:is(button, summary, [role="button"], a.tab-btn, a.pager-link, .settings-tab, .topbar-back, .card-link, .knowledge-tabs a) { min-height: 48px; display: inline-flex; align-items: center }`. Better: give `.tab-btn` and `.pager-link` the min-height at all widths so the rule can't be missed, and drop the bare `min-width: 48px` which distorts narrow inline chips.

### `global (sidebar, tasks, task detail, scheduling, knowledge)` (1)

- **HIGH** · *accessibility* — **App-local badges mix their hue into --bg-surface0 instead of the canon --bg-base recipe, dropping every one of them below WCAG AA (light theme as low as 2.13:1)**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:1782,1790,1794,646,647,3036`
  - *Evidence:* Canon `.status-badge-*` (design-system.css:1259-1279) uses `color-mix(in srgb, var(--hue) 10%, var(--bg-base))` and measures 5.37–8.69:1 in both themes — DESIGN.md:989 correctly claims status badges pass AA. The app then re-rolled the same visual with `--bg-surface0` as the mix base: app.css:1790 `.provider-badge-claude { background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--brand-claude) 15%, var(--bg-surface0)); color: var(--brand-claude) }` measures 2.42:1 light / 3.49:1 dark; app.css:1794 `.provider-badge-codex` measures 2.13:1 light; app.css:646 `.delivery-badge.announce` 3.85:1 light; app.css:647 `.delivery-badge.webhook` 3.80:1 light; app.css:3036 `.layer-badge--kg` (teal on 10% mix into bg-base) 2.98:1 light. `.provider-badge` additionally sets `font-size: 0.625rem` (10px, app.css:1782) — below the smallest token `--text-xs` (12px) — so the worst-contrast text in the app is also the smallest. The CLAUDE badge repeats on every sidebar session row, every task table row and every task detail header (visible in shots/desktop-light/task-detail.png, shots/desktop-dark/tasks.png).
  - *Fix:* Rebase every app-local hue badge on the canon recipe: `background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--hue) 10%, var(--bg-base))` + `border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--hue) 22%, var(--bg-surface1))`, i.e. reuse `.status-badge-*` rather than re-declaring. Drop `font-size: 0.625rem` on `.provider-badge` to `var(--text-xs)`. Audit the remaining `color-mix(… var(--bg-surface0))` badge declarations (app.css:2591, 2640, 2650, 1837) the same way.

### `health-dashboard (Guard Activity)` (1)

- med · *component-adoption* — **Audit table opts out of the canonical .data-table and is re-styled as a boxed spreadsheet grid**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/audit_table.dart:44`
  - *Evidence:* audit_table.dart:44 emits `'<div class="table-scroll"><table>'` — no `class="data-table"`. app.css:549-554 then reimplements table styling by descendant selector: `#audit-table-container .table-scroll th, td { border: var(--border); white-space: nowrap; }` (a full 1px box around EVERY cell) and `th { background: var(--bg-surface0); }` (a filled header bar). Canon does the opposite — design-system.css:1573-1587 gives `.data-table` `border-bottom` only, with the comment "Open header (no filled bar) — caption-toned label over a stronger rule reads lighter and more deliberate than a boxed header row". The scheduling page on the same surface correctly uses `class="data-table"` (scheduling.html:71,167), so the two tables in this surface group render in incompatible table languages.
  - *Fix:* Add `class="data-table"` to the `<table>` in audit_table.dart:44 and delete the app.css:551-552 cell-border and header-fill overrides, keeping only the `min-width: 700px` scroll rule.

### `health-dashboard + scheduling` (1)

- **HIGH** · *typography* — **Canonical 16px section-heading tier is used by zero templates; health and scheduling use two different, non-canonical h2 treatments**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/health_dashboard.html:29,34,39`
  - *Evidence:* design-system.css:1543 defines `.section-title { font-size: var(--text-lg); font-weight: bold; color: var(--fg) }` — DESIGN.md's `heading-md | 16px / 600 | Section headings`. `rg 'class="section-title"' templates/*.html` returns ZERO hits across the whole app. health_dashboard.html:29,34,39 instead puts `<h2 class="section-label">` on its page sections — `.section-label` is the 12px uppercase fg-sub0 micro-caps token (design-system.css:1534). scheduling.html:31,117 uses a third class, app-local `.section-header` (app.css:634), which is a byte-for-byte duplicate of canonical `.section-title`. Screenshots confirm: "SERVICES"/"METRICS" render as 12px gray micro-caps, "Scheduled Jobs"/"Scheduled Tasks" as 16px white — same semantic role, two visual languages inside one surface group.
  - *Fix:* Use `.section-title` for all page-section h2s on both pages: swap `section-label` → `section-title` in health_dashboard.html:29,34,39 and `section-header` → `section-title` in scheduling.html:31,117, then delete the redundant `.section-header` rule at app.css:634. Reserve `.section-label` for in-card/in-rail micro-labels only.

### `health-dashboard + scheduling + health-audit` (1)

- **HIGH** · *accessibility* — **fg-overlay (the disabled-text token) carries primary labels and data across all three pages; light theme fails WCAG AA**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:583,644,1122,1123`
  - *Evidence:* DESIGN.md defines `--fg-overlay` as "Placeholders, disabled, low-emphasis helper text" and its Do's/Don'ts say "`fg-overlay` is helper/disabled text only" and "Do maintain WCAG AA contrast". It is nonetheless used for load-bearing content: `.card-row-label` (app.css:583 — every KPI label on all 5 health service cards and the status card), `.cron-expr` (app.css:644 — the actual cron expression, the primary value of the Schedule column), `.cron-human` (app.css:1122), `.info-footer` (app.css:648), `.pagination-info` (app.css:566), `.detail-label` (app.css:563), `.table-empty-cell` (app.css:1126). Measured against `--bg-mantle`: light `#7b8094` on `#e6e9ef` = **3.22:1** (AA needs 4.5 at 12-13px) — fails; dark `#7f849c` on `#181825` = 4.75:1, marginal. Worse, `.cron-human--sub-id` (app.css:1123) stacks `opacity: 0.6` on top: effective **1.91:1** light / **2.54:1** dark — visible in desktop-light/scheduling.png where the task id "visual-review-seed" is barely legible. Switching to `--fg-sub0` gives 4.6:1 light. Because `.cron-expr` and `.cron-human` are the same size (12px) and the same color, the Schedule column also has zero internal hierarchy: `0 9 * * *` and `Daily at 9:00 AM` read as one gray block.
  - *Fix:* Move `.card-row-label`, `.cron-expr`, `.cron-human`, `.info-footer`, `.pagination-info`, `.detail-label`, `.table-empty-cell` to `--fg-sub0`. Drop the `opacity: 0.6` from `.cron-human--sub-id` and differentiate the Schedule column by tier instead: `.cron-expr` at --fg-sub1 / --text-sm mono, `.cron-human` at --fg-sub0 / --text-xs.

### `knowledge/wiki, health/audit` (1)

- **HIGH** · *layout-spacing* — **Two navigable routes render completely outside the design system — raw text/plain and an unstyled HTMX fragment**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/web/web_routes.dart:558`
  - *Evidence:* `GET /knowledge/wiki/<path>` (web_routes.dart:534-559) returns `Response.ok(file.readAsStringSync(), headers: {...htmlHeaders, 'content-type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8'})` — the raw markdown file with no shell, no CSS, no rendering, no back link. This is a *linked* destination: knowledge_hub.html:51 renders `<a class="source-link" href=${item.sourceHref}>wiki/README.md</a>`, visible in desktop-dark/knowledge-hub.png. desktop-dark/knowledge-wiki-doc.png shows the result: unrendered markdown (`# Wiki`, `` `MEMORY.md` ``) on a bare black page — the user is ejected from the product by clicking a search result. Separately `GET /health-dashboard/audit` (web_routes.dart:398) serves the HTMX polling fragment with no layout wrapper; desktop-dark/health-audit.png shows Times New Roman body text and native OS buttons on a white page, in both dark and light captures.
  - *Fix:* Wrap the wiki route in the standard shell + `pageTopbar(backHref: '/knowledge')` and render the markdown through the existing marked/DOMPurify path into `.msg-content` (or a `.card` + `.well-deep`). For `/health-dashboard/audit`, return the full page when the request is not an HTMX request (`HX-Request` header absent) rather than a naked fragment.

### `knowledge/wiki-doc` (1)

- **HIGH** · *component-adoption* — **Wiki document view is served as text/plain — no shell, no theme, no design system at all**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/web/web_routes.dart:558`
  - *Evidence:* web_routes.dart:558 returns the .md file with `'content-type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8'`. The captured page proves the consequence: desktop-dark/knowledge-wiki-doc.png and desktop-light/knowledge-wiki-doc.png are byte-identical (md5 71cd4ec7fbc22a9a16cf5ed69eb97f97, 43 KB vs ~900 KB for every other page) because no app CSS, no theme class and no layout is involved — the screenshot shows browser-default monospace text at the window's top-left corner with zero padding, raw `# Wiki` and backticks unrendered, no sidebar, no topbar, no back link. This is the primary drill-down target of the hub: every wiki result links here (knowledge_hub_service.dart:213 and source_attribution.dart:143 both emit `/knowledge/wiki/<path>`).
  - *Fix:* Render through a real page: layout.dart shell + pagehead + a `.card` body using the existing in-app markdown convention `<div data-markdown tl:text="${content}">` (shared.js:214-221 already wires marked+DOMPurify; chat.html:9 and task_detail.html:179 use it). Keep the raw text behind a raw/rendered toggle like memory_dashboard if the raw view is wanted.

### `notfound` (1)

- low · *typography* — **The 404 page abandons the app shell and renders its hero number in the disabled-text colour at a size outside the type scale**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/app.css:310`
  - *Evidence:* error_page.html has no `.shell`, no sidebar and no topbar — the user is dropped out of the application with a single "Back to Home" link. `.error-code { font-size: 4rem; color: var(--fg-overlay) }` (app.css:310-314): 64px sits entirely outside the declared scale (DESIGN.md's largest tier is `metric-value` at 32px), and `--fg-overlay` is the placeholder/disabled tier the canon reserves for non-essential text (DESIGN.md:990). The result in desktop-dark/notfound.png is a washed-out grey "404" centred in a 1440×900 empty field — the single largest surface in the product and the one that reads most like a default framework error page. No mascot, no claw-mark, despite DESIGN.md:332 naming "empty states, about screens" as mascot territory and this being the app's only full-bleed hero moment.
  - *Fix:* Render the error page inside the shell with `pageTopbar` so nav survives. Retier `.error-code` to `--text-3xl` with `--tracking-tight` in `--fg-sub0`, and put the 96px `.pixel-art` mascot above it — one sanctioned brand moment on the one view that has room for it.

### `projects / workflow-detail` (1)

- med · *dialog-feedback* — **Destructive workflow and project actions use the OS `window.confirm` dialog, bypassing the shipped `.card-glass` dialog and toast system**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/static/controllers/dc_projects_controller.js:191`
  - *Evidence:* dc_projects_controller.js:191 `if (!window.confirm('Remove project \'' + projectName + '\'? Running tasks will be cancelled.')) return;`. workflow_detail.html:66 and :78 use `hx-confirm="Cancel this workflow? Running tasks will be stopped."` / `"Reject this approval? The workflow will be cancelled."` — HTMX's `hx-confirm` is also `window.confirm`. The app already ships a styled modal (`project_form.dart:7`, `task_form.dart:46`: `<dialog class="task-dialog card card-glass">`, the canon overlay tier per DESIGN.md:565) and a `showToast()` helper used three lines away in the same controller. Additionally dc_projects_controller.js:175 and :197 call `window.location.reload()` after Fetch/Remove, full-reloading an otherwise HTMX-swapped app and discarding scroll position and sidebar state.
  - *Fix:* Route all three confirms through a shared `.card-glass` confirm dialog (reuse the `<dialog>` pattern already in project_form.dart) and replace the two `window.location.reload()` calls with an `hx-trigger`ed swap of `#projects-content`.

### `projects / workflows / workflow-detail` (1)

- **HIGH** · *state-coverage* — **Three different empty-state treatments across one surface group; the workflow-detail one is a bare left-aligned sentence, not the `.empty-state` component**
  - `packages/dartclaw_server/lib/src/templates/workflow_detail.html:140`
  - *Evidence:* projects.html:52-57 uses the full canon pattern (`.empty-state` + `.empty-state-icon` with `.claw-mark` + title + text + primary CTA). workflow_list.html:62-64 uses `.empty-state` but its only child is `<span class="empty-state-text">No workflow runs found.</span>` — no `.icon` (design-system.css:1517 gives it an accent glow), no title, no CTA, on the page whose whole job is launching runs. workflow_detail.html:140 doesn't use `.empty-state` at all: `<div tl:unless="${hasSteps}" class="empty-state-text">No steps defined.</div>` sits inside `.workflow-pipeline` (padding-left sp-4), so it renders as a lone left-aligned 13px gray sentence floating mid-page — exactly what desktop-dark/workflow-detail.png shows at y=310. Separately app.css:1809 sets `.empty-state-title` to `--text-base` (14px) although DESIGN.md:391 reserves the 24px `display` tier for "Hero/empty-state moments".
  - *Fix:* Give all three the same recipe: `.empty-state` > `.icon` (or `.claw-mark`) + a `display`-tier (24px) title + `.empty-state-text` + a primary CTA ("Launch a workflow" / "Add project"). Bump `.empty-state-title` from `--text-base` to `--text-2xl`.
